# PART 08 — TONE 8: SUNDAY AND WEEKDAY OFFICES (SATURDAY VESPERS THROUGH SATURDAY MATINS)

*On Saturday Evening.*
*At Little Vespers.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," we set 4 verses, and sing the Resurrection Stichera.*
*Tone 8:*[^9489]

*Sticheron 1 (Resurrectional):* An evening hymn and rational worship do we offer unto Thee, O Christ: for Thou wast well-pleased to have mercy on us by Thy Resurrection. *(Twice)*[^9490]

*Sticheron 2 (Resurrectional):* O Lord, O Lord, cast us not away from Thy presence; but be Thou well-pleased to have mercy on us by Thy Resurrection.[^9491]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion (Dogmatikon), Tone 8:* How shall we call thee blessed, O Theotokos? How shall we hymn, O most blessed one, the incomprehensible mystery of thy childbearing? For the Creator of the ages and Fashioner of our nature, taking pity on His own image, brought Himself down into unfathomable self-emptying; being in the immaterial bosom of the Father, He dwelt in thy womb, O pure one, and unchangeably became flesh from thee, O unwedded one: remaining what He was, God by nature. Therefore we worship Him as perfect God and perfect man, the same in both forms: for both natures are truly in Him; and we proclaim all His natural properties as twofold according to His twofold essence, reverencing two operations and wills. For being consubstantial with God and the Father, He sovereignly wills and operates as God; and being consubstantial also with us, He sovereignly wills and operates as man. Pray Him, O pure and all-blessed one, that our souls may be saved.[^9492]

*At the Aposticha, Resurrection Sticheron. Tone 8:*[^9493]

*Sticheron 1 (Resurrectional):* Thou didst ascend upon the Cross, O Jesus, Who camest down from heaven; Thou camest unto death, O immortal Life; unto those in darkness, the true Light; unto the fallen, the Resurrection of all. O our Enlightenment and Savior, glory to Thee![^9494]

*Other Stichera to the Most Holy Theotokos. Tone 8, Podoben: "O most marvelous wonder" (Ѡ҆ пресла́внагѡ чудесѐ):*[^9495]

*Verse:* I will remember thy name in every generation and generation.[^9496]

*Sticheron 1:* Rejoice, O all-hymned Theotokos! Rejoice, fountain welling forth life unto the faithful...[^9497]

...rejoice, Sovereign Lady of all and blessed Mistress of creation! Rejoice, all-immaculate and most glorified one! Rejoice, all-pure one! Rejoice, palace! Rejoice, divine dwelling! Rejoice, pure one! Rejoice, Virgin-Mother! Rejoice, Bride of God![^9498]

*Verse:* Hear, O daughter, and see, and incline thine ear.[^9499]

*Sticheron 2:* Rejoice, all-pure Mother of God! Rejoice, hope of the faithful! Rejoice, cleansing of the world! Rejoice, thou who deliverest thy servants from every affliction! Rejoice, life-bearing consolation of men! Rejoice, protection! Rejoice, rampart of those who call upon thee! Rejoice, divine abode of God, and holy mountain![^9500]

*Verse:* Before thy face shall the rich among the people make their supplication.[^9501]

*Sticheron 3:* Rejoice, O Theotokos, Mother of Christ! Rejoice, only hope and protection of men! Rejoice, refuge and radiant candlestick of the Light! Rejoice, taper! Rejoice, sanctified palace! Rejoice, paradise! Rejoice, fountain welling forth waters unto those who hasten unto thee![^9502]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion (Dogmatikon), Tone 8:* He Whom heaven could not contain, O Virgin Theotokos, was roomily contained in thy womb, and thou didst remain pure by the ineffable Word, without thy virginity being defiled in any way. For thou alone among women wast both Mother and Virgin; and thou alone, O All-Pure One, didst nurse the Life-giving Son, and didst bear in thine embrace the slumberless Eye: yet He left not the Father's bosom, as He was before the ages; but on high He is wholly God with the Angels, and below wholly from thee with men, and everywhere ineffably. Him do thou beseech, O all-holy Sovereign Lady, that they may be saved who in Orthodox manner confess thee as the pure Theotokos.[^9503]

*Then: "Now lettest Thou depart." Trisagion, and after "Our Father," the Resurrection Troparion:* From on high didst Thou descend. *Theotokion:* Thou Who for our sake wast born of a Virgin. *Litany, and Dismissal.*[^9504]

*[Engraved Tailpiece: An ornamental baroque vignette with floral motifs, marking the conclusion of Saturday Little Vespers in Tone 8]*[^9505] *On Saturday at Great Vespers.*
*On "Lord, I have cried," we set 10 verses, and sing the Resurrection Stichera.*
*Tone 8:*[^9506]

*Sticheron 1 (Resurrectional):* An evening hymn and rational worship do we offer unto Thee, O Christ: for Thou wast well-pleased to have mercy on us by Thy Resurrection.[^9507]

*Sticheron 2 (Resurrectional):* O Lord, O Lord, cast us not away from Thy presence; but be Thou well-pleased to have mercy on us by Thy Resurrection.[^9508]

*Sticheron 3 (Resurrectional):* Rejoice, O holy Zion, mother of churches, dwelling-place of God: for thou wast the first to receive the remission of sins by the Resurrection.[^9509]

*Other Stichera, Eastern (Anatolian). Tone the same:*[^9510]

*Sticheron 4 (Anatolian):* The Word of God the Father, begotten before the ages, in the latter times the Same having willingly been incarnate of the Unwedded One, endured crucifixion unto death: and anciently slain man He saved by His Resurrection.[^9511]

*Sticheron 5 (Anatolian):* Thy Resurrection from the dead do we glorify, O Christ, whereby Thou didst set free the race of Adam from the torment of Hades: and as God Thou hast granted unto the world life everlasting, and great mercy.[^9512]

*Sticheron 6 (Anatolian):* Glory to Thee, O Christ the Savior, only-begotten Son of God, Who wast nailed to the Cross, and didst rise from the tomb on the third day.[^9513]

*Sticheron 7 (Anatolian):* Thee do we glorify, O Lord, Who willingly didst endure the Cross for our sake; and Thee do we worship, O almighty Savior: cast us not away from Thy presence, but hear and save us by Thy Resurrection, O Lover of mankind.[^9514]

*Other Stichera to the Most Holy Theotokos, by Paul of Amorion. Tone 4, Podoben: "As one valiant among martyrs" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ до́бла):*[^9515]

*Sticheron 8 (To the Theotokos):* The ranks of Angels glorify thee; for, O Pure One, thou didst give birth to God, Who with the Father and...[^9516]

...the Spirit is co-eternal, and Who brought into being by His will the angelic hosts out of nothingness. Pray Him to save and enlighten the souls of those who in Orthodox manner praise thee as the Theotokos.[^9517]

*Sticheron 9 (To the Theotokos):* As a fountain of sanctification and an all-golden ark illumined by the Divine Spirit, I pray and fall down before thee: do thou enlighten my soul, O Sovereign Lady, given over to passions and wretched, delivering me from the bitter torment of demons, and granting me an unhindered path of salvation.[^9518]

*Sticheron 10 (To the Theotokos):* When the thrones shall be set, and the books opened, and deeds exposed, and every man shall stand burdened and naked, wholly trembling before the indignation of God and that righteous sentence: then spare me, O Sovereign Lady, and snatch me away from all judgment, me who am guilty of every torment.[^9519]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion (Dogmatikon), Tone 8:* The King of heaven, through His love for mankind, appeared on earth and lived with men; for having taken flesh from the pure Virgin, and having come forth from her with the assumption, He is one Son, twofold in nature, but not in hypostasis; therefore, proclaiming Him truly as perfect God and perfect man, we confess Christ our God. Pray Him, O unwedded Mother, that our souls may have mercy shown them.[^9520]

*O Gladsome Light. The Prokeimenon of the day, and the rest.*[^9521]

*At the Aposticha, Resurrection Stichera. Tone 8:*[^9522]

*Sticheron 1 (Resurrectional):* Thou didst ascend upon the Cross, O Jesus, Who camest down from heaven; Thou camest unto death, O immortal Life; unto those in darkness, the true Light; unto the fallen, the Resurrection of all. O our Light and Savior, glory to Thee![^9523]

*Other Stichera (Alphabetical):*[^9524]

*Verse:* The Lord is King, He is clothed with majesty.[^9525]

*Sticheron 2 (Alphabetical):* Christ do we glorify, Who is risen from the dead; for taking soul and body, He cut off the passions from both: His most pure soul indeed having descended into Hades, which also He despoiled, and in the tomb His holy body saw no corruption, the Deliverer of our souls.[^9526]

*Verse:* For He hath established the world, which shall not be moved.[^9527]

*Sticheron 3 (Alphabetical):* With psalms and hymns do we glorify Thy Resurrection from the dead, O Christ, whereby Thou didst deliver us from the tyranny of Hades, and as God hast granted unto us life everlasting, and great mercy.[^9528]

*Verse:* Holiness becomes Thine house, O Lord, unto length of days.[^9529]

*Sticheron 4 (Alphabetical):* O Master of all, incomprehensible Creator of heaven and earth...[^9530]

...having suffered through the Cross, Thou didst pour forth impassibility unto me; and having received burial and risen in glory, Thou didst raise Adam together with Thyself by Thine almighty hand. Glory to Thy third-day rising, whereby Thou hast granted us life everlasting, and cleansing from sins, as Thou alone art compassionate.[^9531]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* O Unwedded Virgin, who ineffably didst conceive God in the flesh, Mother of the Most High God, accept the supplications of thy servants, O all-immaculate one, granting unto all the cleansing of transgressions. Now receiving our entreaties, pray that we all may be saved.[^9532]

*Now lettest Thou depart. Trisagion, and after "Our Father," the Resurrection Troparion, Tone 8:*[^9533]

From on high didst Thou descend, O Compassionate One; Thou didst accept three days' burial, that Thou mightest free us from the passions. O our Life and Resurrection, Lord, glory to Thee![^9534]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* O Good One, Who for our sake wast born of a Virgin, and didst endure crucifixion, who by death didst despoil death, and didst manifest the Resurrection as God: despise not those whom Thou hast fashioned with Thy hand; show forth Thy love for mankind, O Merciful One; accept the Theotokos who bore Thee, who prays for us; and save, O our Savior, a despairing people.[^9535]

*On Saturday at Compline. Supplicatory Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^9536]
Let us sing unto the Lord, Who led His people through the Red Sea: for He alone is gloriously glorified.[^9537]

*Refrain:* Most Holy Sovereign Lady Theotokos, save us.[^9538]

*Troparion 1:* Come mystically, O brethren, as from the beginning let us the faithful bring a new song unto the holy Theotokos, today praising her magnificent deeds.[^9539]

*Troparion 2:* Anciently illumined in mind by the vision of God, Moses was clearly taught concerning thy God-befitting conception, O Pure One, beyond nature, O Virgin, when the bush in the garden was manifested unto him.[^9540]

*Glory:* Unto thee do I offer the workings of my heart, and acceptably do I present this writing, having put thee forward as a divine shelter of protection close at hand unto Christ the Master.[^9541]

*Both now:* Incline thine ear unto me, O Pure One, who with Orthodox faith hasten unto...[^9542]

...the shadow of thy countenance, and with honor and love venerate thee in fear: hear my supplicatory voice.[^9543]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou art the confirmation of those that hasten unto Thee, O Lord; Thou art the light of the darkened, and my spirit praises Thee.[^9544]

*Troparion 1:* Anciently the Patriarch's ladder prefigured thee, O all-immaculate one; for the descent of Angels manifested the divine descent of God unto us, coming down into thy womb.[^9545]

*Troparion 2:* The tribe of Judah settled, which Jacob foretold, that out of his tribe should bud forth the Deliverance, Jesus Christ: Whom having borne, O All-Pure One, thou hast been glorified.[^9546]

*Glory:* Despairing because of sins, I have found thee as a haven of salvation, O pure Theotokos, our hope and help: therefore guide me unto repentance.[^9547]

*Both now:* I have thee as one who art close unto the Master, O all-glorious Sovereign Lady: the book of my deeds have I laid upon thee in faith; keep not silence to have compassion on me.[^9548]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* From the overshadowed mountain, O Word, the prophet in a divine manner perceived that Thou wast to be incarnate of the only Theotokos, and with fear he glorified Thy power.[^9549]

*Troparion 1:* Arrayed as with golden necklaces, O Bride of the Father, having received grace and been adorned with the comeliness of virginity, thou wast shown forth as the Mother of the Son of God.[^9550]

*Troparion 2:* Thee as the true Zion did Christ the Word choose for Himself as a divine dwelling, having chosen thee, an elect one, O Theotokos, for the renewal of all the world.[^9551]

*Glory:* Rejoice, beautiful palace of the Word, virginal bridal chamber of the King! Rejoice, boast of all the bodiless ones! Rejoice, help of men![^9552]

*Both now:* They withdraw from God and therefore perish, who reject the icons of Thy Son, O Mother of God, Virgin Theotokos, through which they that honor thee are saved.[^9553]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Dispel the darkness of my soul, O Christ God, Giver of light, having driven away the primeval darkness of the abyss; and grant me the light of Thy commandments, O Word, that waking early I may glorify Thee.[^9554]

*Troparion 1:* Having assembled together with the divine Gabriel, let us faithfully cry aloud unto the Theotokos: Rejoice, O holy Virgin, full of grace, the Lord is with thee, Who for thy sake consumed sorrow and granted joy.[^9555]

*Troparion 2:* Gideon beheld thy most pure womb, O pure Virgin, into which the Word came down as dew and was incarnate by the Divine Spirit, remaining inseparable from the Father's Godhead.[^9556]

*Glory:* Thou art the helper of the world and protector of sinful men, O Virgin Mother of God! Unto those that flee unto thee in faith and love thou art a saving change and the loosing of many transgressions.[^9557]

*Both now:* Without seed didst thou bud forth Him Who before all creation was the Offshoot of the Father, ageless and without beginning, through the Divine Spirit, O pure Mother of God: the likeness of Whose form we all revere.[^9558]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Accept me, held fast by many sins, O Lover of mankind, who fall down before Thy compassions, as Thou didst the Prophet, O Lord, and save me.[^9559]

*Troparion 1:* As the mirror of virginity and the pure receptacle of the Godhead, we praise thee with hymns, O unwedded Virgin.[^9560]

*Troparion 2:* God was incarnate in thy womb, impassibly and awesomely, O Bride of God, as upon a new scroll written by the Father's finger.[^9561]

*Glory:* We have thy protection as cleansing, and an assured hope and defense, O pure Virgin! Put not to shame Thy servants, O Sovereign Lady.[^9562]

*Both now:* By thine intercession, O Bride of God, stay the unceasing madness of passions into stillness; and guide us unto the haven of calm.[^9563]

*Then: Lord, have mercy, thrice. Glory... both now...*
*Sessional Hymn. Tone 8:*[^9564]
To thee, the Champion Leader, do we, thy servants, dedicate a thank-offering of victory, having been delivered from evils, O Theotokos: but as thou hast invincible might, free us from all dangers, that we may cry unto thee: Rejoice, O Bride Unwedded![^9565]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The fire in Babylon was once put to shame by God's condescension; therefore the children in the furnace, dancing with joyful step as in a flowery garden, sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9566]

*Troparion 1:* Thou wast shown forth as the mediatress of our joy, O Virgin, and of grace...[^9567]

...bringing a crown with love, we cry unto thee: Rejoice, O blessed and pure one, praising thee.[^9568]

*Troparion 2:* A holy mountain art thou of God, overshadowed; a fertile mountain, O Most Undefiled One; a curdled mountain in divine radiances; a mountain in which God was pleased to dwell.[^9569]

*Glory:* There is no sin that overcoms thy grace: for thou hast maternal boldness and will, and dost loose transgressions by thy prayers, and dost turn aside every onslaught.[^9570]

*Both now:* Of the Trinity thou didst bear One, O Theotokos, Who became unchangeable by bodily union, being twofold in nature; the image of Whose form we revere.[^9571]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Hymn the Lord Who was glorified on the holy mountain, and revealed to Moses in the bush by fire the mystery of the Ever-Virgin, and exalt Him unto all ages.[^9572]

*Troparion 1:* A censer wast thou shown forth unto the prophet, bearing the divine coal that takes away sins, O Virgin Theotokos, Mother of our God.[^9573]

*Troparion 2:* Daniel foresaw thee as a great mountain, O Virgin Theotokos, from which Christ, the precious stone, was clothed in flesh, and cast down the deceitful temples of idols.[^9574]

*Glory:* The great sea-monster of grievous sin and the despair of my passions seeks to swallow me up; but forestall, and save thy servant, O Sovereign Lady.[^9575]

*Both now:* He Who through thee conversed with men, being God of all things, took on the likeness of man; Whose countenance we revere, O Virgin, in sacred icons.[^9576]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* In truth we confess thee as Theotokos, who are saved through thee, O pure Virgin, with the bodiless choirs magnifying thee.[^9577]

*Troparion 1:* A garden enclosed and a fountain sealed by the Divine Spirit doth the all-wise one sing of thee in the Canticle, O Virgin Theotokos; therefore, as the garden of life, Christ is incarnate.[^9578]

*Troparion 2:* Prefiguring thine ineffable childbearing, the prophet foresaw a sealed book, the mystery whereof no one hath understood, the incarnation of thy childbearing.[^9579]

*Glory:* Unto thee in compunction of soul do we all pray: despise not our prayers, O our Sovereign Lady, but be...[^9580]

...a brightly-shining protection for us, and hear our prayer.[^9581]

*Both now:* I fall down before thine icon and that of thy Son; and those who hesitate to venerate them, as falsehoods of Manes, I reject, O Virgin Theotokos; therefore I conclude my song in an Orthodox manner.[^9582]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion Prayers, and the rest according to custom.*[^9583]

*On Sunday, at the Midnight Office,*
*The Canon to the Most Holy and Life-Creating Trinity.*
*(The acrostic whereof is: Trinity, O Unity, save me, thy servant.)*
*A composition of Metrophanes.*[^9584]
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:* The charioteer Pharaoh did the wonder-working rod of Moses once plunge into the deep, striking crosswise and dividing the sea; but it saved fugitive Israel marching on foot, as they sang a song unto God.[^9585]

*Refrain: Most Holy Trinity our God, glory to Thee.*[^9586]

*Troparion 1:* Unto the thrice-radiant King, and Builder, and Provider of all things, and the only Good One, existing by nature and possessing the single glory of Godhead, unto God the One Principle do we fall down, singing the thrice-holy song.[^9587]

*Troparion 2:* Remembering the divine sayings and the prophecies from on high, manifestly do we glorify the single God-originating nature, ever-existing, co-unoriginate, in three Persons: the Father, and the Son, and the Spirit, all-powerful and creative.[^9588]

*Glory:* Abraham, having become an initiate of sacred mysteries, in a sacred manner of old, received with joy the Creator of all and God and Lord, in three Hypostases; and he showed forth the single dominion of the three Hypostases.[^9589]

*Both now: Theotokion:* Without knowing wedlock didst thou bear Christ, Who for our sake took on our nature, O Most Pure One, and remained unchanged in both; Whom unceasingly entreat, that He may grant me forgiveness of sins and deliverance from temptations.[^9590]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens with understanding, and didst found the earth upon the waters: establish me, O Christ, upon the rock of the Church; for there is none holy save Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^9591]

*Troparion 1:* Thee, the unapproachable God and King of glory, did Isaiah behold upon a high throne, and the Cherubim and Seraphim glorifying Thee with unceasing hymns, the single Tri-hypostatic One.[^9592]

*Troparion 2:* Having attained unto the Word, Who alone was begotten of the Father as from the Mind, and the Spirit proceeding ineffably, according to each of the thoughts and teachings of the scriptures, we revere the One thrice-radiant God.[^9593]

*Glory:* The unbegotten Father, Who is the radiance of His own essence, having incorruptibly begotten the Son, Light of Light, by procession brings forth the co-growing Light of the Spirit, all-effecting and equal in honor.[^9594]

*Both now: Theotokion:* A pure temple wast thou shown forth, O Virgin Mother Mary, unto Christ Who all-powerfully and all-wisely fashioned all things, and set them in order, and sustains them; Whom make merciful unto me by thy maternal prayers.[^9595]

*Lord, have mercy, thrice. Then:*
*Sessional Hymn. Tone 8, Podoben: "That which was commanded mystically" (Повелѣ́нное та́йнѡ):*[^9596]
The power of the thrice-radiant and honorable Godhead, let us the faithful now praise, for by a mere nod He hath constituted all the angelic choirs on high, and the lowlier church hierarchies below, that they might cry: Holy, Holy, Holy art Thou, O most good God! Glory and praise be unto Thy dominion![^9597]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* Having borne the immutable God, O Good One, do Thou by Thy maternal prayers establish my heart, which is ever altered by sin and slothfulness through the assaults of the deceitful one; that I also may thankfully glorify thee, O Mother of God Mary: have mercy on Thy flock, which Thou hast acquired, O All-Undefiled One.[^9598]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Thou art my strength, O Lord, Thou art also my power; Thou art my God, Thou art my rejoicing, Who leftest not the bosom of the Father, and didst visit our poverty. Therefore with the Prophet Habakkuk do I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind![^9599]

*Troparion 1:* The Dayspring of the Godhead, having appeared unto those in darkness, drove away the whole somber night of the passions, and the Sun of Righteousness shone forth, simple indeed in essence, but thrice-radiant in Persons; Whom we ever sing and glorify.[^9600]

*Troparion 2:* Him Who is hymned by the mouths of the Seraphim, we with clay lips glorify as the single and Triune Lord of glory, in essence and Hypostases, crying aloud: O Sovereign King of all, grant unto Thy servants forgiveness of diverse transgressions![^9601]

*Glory:* O honorable and God-originating Trinity, Who sustainest all things that exist, invisible, all-bountiful, tender-hearted, Lover of mankind! Forget not me, Thy servant, utterly, nor make void the covenant which Thou hast ordained for Thy servants, according to Thine ineffable mercy.[^9602]

*Both now: Theotokion:* Having found thee, O Most Pure Maiden, as the only fair beauty of Jacob from of old, the pre-unoriginate Word, having dwelt in thee for the sake of tender compassion, renewed human nature; Whom unceasingly entreat, that I may be delivered from every sorrow.[^9603]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Why hast Thou cast me off from Thy countenance, O Light unwaning, and alien darkness hath covered me, a wretch? But turn me back, and direct my ways toward the light of Thy commandments, I pray.[^9604]

*Troparion 1:* We glorify the three co-ever-existing Persons, and the single Lord, the divine nature, simply dividing and uniting; and we faithfully cry aloud: O holy God-originating Trinity, deliver Thy servants from sorrow.[^9605]

*Troparion 2:* I weep bitterly over the weakness of my mind, how unwillingly I suffer truly an involuntary change! Therefore I cry aloud: O holy Life-Creating Trinity, establish me in the state of the good.[^9606]

*Glory:* Weighted down with the slumber of sin, and plunging into the sleep of death, do Thou, as a Trinity holy, God-originating, Lover of mankind, all-good, and all-merciful, show compassion and raise me up.[^9607]

*Both now: Theotokion:* O Mother-Virgin Maiden, most pure, all-undefiled, full of divine grace, by thy prayers make thy Son and God and Lord merciful unto me, and deliver thy servant quickly from passions and transgressions.[^9608]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine iniquities; and lead me up from the depth of evils, I pray: for unto Thee have I cried, and hear me, O God of my salvation.[^9609]

*Troparion 1:* Imitating the ranks of heavenly minds, O transcendent Trinity, monarchical over all, with thrice-holy hymns we glorify Thee with our clay lips.[^9610]

*Troparion 2:* Unto Him Who fashioned man according to Thine image, and from non-existence...[^9611]

...all things didst all-wisely compose, unto the Tri-hypostatic God do I bow down, and honor, and hymn, and magnify Thee.[^9612]

*Glory:* O Almighty God, and only Uncircumscribed One, dwell Thou in me according to Thine ineffable mercy, O thrice-radiant Master, and enlighten me, and give me understanding, as Thou art tender-hearted.[^9613]

*Both now: Theotokion:* A temple of the uncontainable God wast thou shown forth, O Most Pure One: make me also a temple of His divine grace, O all-holy Sovereign Lady, by thy supplications, and keep me unharmed.[^9614]

*Then: Lord, have mercy, thrice.*
*Sessional Hymn. Tone 8, Podoben: "That which was commanded mystically" (Повелѣ́нное та́йнѡ):*[^9615]
The unoriginate Father, the co-unoriginate Son, and the Divine Spirit, let us the faithful truly praise in song: undividedly, immutably, and unchangeably, the simple, holy, and co-growing Trinity, crying aloud with the Angels: Holy art Thou, O Father, O Son, with the All-Holy and Honorable Spirit! Have mercy on those whom Thou hast created according to Thine image, O Master.[^9616]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* We give thanks unto thee ever, O Theotokos, and we magnify thee, O pure one, and bow down, hymning thy childbearing, O full of grace, crying unceasingly: Save us, O all-merciful Virgin, as thou art good, in the hour of trial, that we thy servants may not be put to shame.[^9617]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* By God's condescension the fire in Babylon was once put to shame; therefore the children in the furnace, dancing with joyful step as in a flower-garden, sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9618]

*Troparion 1:* By Thine ineffable wisdom and by the abyss of goodness, show me, Thy servant, to be pitied freely; and now, as of old, deliver me from affliction, O Trinity, O Unity, God, of sins and passions. *Twice.*[^9619]

*Glory:* Unbegotten Mind the Father, and the Word begotten of Him, and the Divine Spirit, incomprehensibly proceeding, O God, One Principle, thrice-radiant, I sing unto Thee: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^9620]

*Both now: Theotokion:* I have been slain, O Most Pure One, having been given to drink of the venom of sin, and I flee unto thee in faith, who didst bear the Prince of life: by thy prayers make thy servant alive, and from tempta-...[^9621]

...-tions and passions deliver me, O only Pure One.[^9622]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* Sevenfold did the Chaldean tyrant madly kindle the furnace for the godly; but seeing them saved by a greater power, he cried aloud unto the Creator and Deliverer: Ye children, bless; ye priests, sing; ye people, exalt Him unto all ages.[^9623]

*Troparion 1:* Being an unwaning Light, thrice-radiant and thrice-sunlit, and of one principle, sovereign, most simple, God incomprehensible, and sovereign Lord: do Thou now enlighten my dark and clouded heart, and make me to sing unto Thee brightly and luminously, and to glorify Thee unto all ages.[^9624]

*Troparion 2:* With most sacred wings do the most divine Seraphim reverently veil their faces and their feet, not enduring the glory of Thine incomprehensible beauty, O most holy Trinity, well-originating, God-originating, monarchical; yet do we also dare to hymn Thee and glorify Thee, faithfully unto all ages.[^9625]

*Glory:* The unoriginate Dominion, all-powerful and all-good, perfectly originating, beneficent, uncircumscribed, causeless cause, creative, ever-existing, provident, and saving unto all, Unity in essence, and Trinity in Persons, do I glorify Thee, my God, faithfully unto all ages.[^9626]

*Both now: Theotokion:* Upon the earth hath the unwaning Sun shone forth by the virginal childbearing from thee, O Most Pure Sovereign Lady, and delivered men from the clouded gloom of idols; therefore do thou now enlighten me with the rays of that Godhead beyond measure, and keep thy servant.[^9627]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Heaven was struck with awe at this, and the ends of the earth were amazed, that God appeared unto men in the flesh, and thy womb became more spacious than the heavens. Therefore the hierarchies of Angels and of men magnify thee, O Theotokos.[^9628]

*Troparion 1:* Reigning over all things, and all-creative sovereign nature, ageless, life-creating, tender-hearted, Lover of mankind, good, monarchical Trinity: as we now glorify Thee, we ask forgiveness of sins, peace for the world, and oneness of mind for the churches. *Twice.*[^9629]

*Glory:* One Lordship and thrice-radiant, single Godhead thrice-sunlit: receive Thy singers...[^9630]

...in a manner befitting God: and deliver us from transgressions, and from temptations, and from cruel things, and swiftly grant peace in Thy love for mankind, and union unto the churches.[^9631]

*Both now: Theotokion:* Having settled into the virginal womb, O Christ my Savior, Thou wast shown forth unto Thy world as God-man, truly unchangeable and unconfused; and Thou didst promise to be manifestly ever with Thy servants. Therefore, by the prayers of her that bore Thee, establish peace for all Thy flock.[^9632]

*After this, the refrains of Gregory of Sinai: It is truly meet: and the rest of the Midnight Office, written at the end of this book.*[^9633]

*On Sunday, at Matins:*[^9634]
*After the Six Psalms: "God is the Lord," and hath appeared unto us: in Tone 8; and we say the Resurrectional Troparion: "From the heights didst Thou descend": twice. Glory: to the Saint, from the Menaion. Both now: Resurrectional Theotokion of the same Tone as the troparion of the Saint. Then the usual stichology of the Psalter.*[^9635]

*After the First Stichology, Resurrectional Sessional Hymns, Tone 8:*
Thou art risen from the dead, O Life of all, and a radiant Angel cried aloud unto the women: Cease from tears, announce the good tidings unto the Apostles, cry out singing: That Christ the Lord is risen, Who was pleased as God to save the human race.[^9636]

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end [LXX Ps. 9:33].*[^9637]

Having risen from the tomb in truth, Thou didst command the venerable women to proclaim the rising unto the Apostles, as it is written; and speedy Peter came to the tomb, and beholding the light in the tomb, he was struck with awe. Therefore he beheld the winding sheets alone lying in it, without the Divine Body, and with fear he cried aloud: Glory to Thee, O Christ God, for Thou savest all things, O our Savior; for Thou art the Father's Radiance.[^9638]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:* The Heavenly Door, and the Ark, the all-holy Mountain, the radiant Cloud let us hymn, the Heavenly Ladder, the spiritual Paradise, the deliverance of Eve, the great treasure of all the universe: for in her was salvation wrought for the world, and remission of ancient transgressions. Therefore do we cry unto thee: Pray thy Son and God, to grant remission of transgressions unto those that piously worship thy most holy childbearing.[^9639]

*After the Second Stichology, Resurrectional Sessional Hymns, Tone 8:*
Men sealed Thy tomb, O Savior, but an Angel rolled away the stone from the doors; the women beheld Thee risen from the dead, and they announced the good tidings unto Thy disciples in Zion: That Thou art risen, O Life of all, and loosed are the bonds of death. O Lord, glory to Thee![^9640]

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will tell of all Thy marvelous works [LXX Ps. 9:2].*[^9641]

Having brought funeral myrrh, the women heard the angelic voice from the tomb: Cease from tears, and in place of grief receive joy; cry aloud singing: That Christ the Lord is risen, Who was pleased as God to save the human race.[^9642]

*Glory... both now... Theotokion:*
*(We sing not sitting, but standing, and with fear and reverence.)*[^9643]
In thee rejoices, O Thou Who art full of grace, all creation: the assembly of Angels, and the race of men; O sanctified temple, and spiritual paradise, virginal boast, from whom God was incarnate, and became a child, Who is our God before the ages: for He made thy womb a throne, and fashioned thy body more spacious than the heavens. In thee rejoices, O Thou Who art full of grace, all creation. Glory to Thee![^9644]

*Hypakoe, Tone 8:*[^9645]
The Myrrh-bearers, standing before the tomb of the Giver of Life, sought the immortal Master among the dead; and having received the joy of the good tidings from the Angel, they announced unto the Apostles: That Christ God is risen, granting unto the world great mercy.[^9646]

*Songs of Ascents [Anabathmoi], Tone 8:*[^9647]
*The verses whereof we sing repeating.*
*Antiphon 1:*
From my youth the enemy tempts me, he burns me with pleasures; but trusting in Thee, O Lord, I vanquish him.[^9648]

Let them that hate Zion become even as grass before it is plucked up; for Christ will cut through their necks with the severing of torments.[^9649]

*Glory:* By the Holy Spirit all things have life: Light of Light, great God! We hymn Him with the Father, and with the Word.[^9650]

*Both now: The same.*[^9651]

*Antiphon 2:*
I have cried unto Thee, O Lord, attend, incline Thine ear unto me...[^9652]

...who cry aloud, and cleanse me before Thou takest me hence.[^9653]

He that hath hope in the Lord will not be terrified then, when He will judge all things with fire and torment.[^9654]

*Glory:* By the Holy Spirit, every one who is godly beholds and foretells, and works exalted wonders, singing unto the One God in three Persons; for though it shines with three radiances, the Godhead reigns as one.[^9655]

*Both now: The same.*[^9656]

*Antiphon 3:*
Let my heart, humbly minded, be covered with Thy fear, lest being lifted up it fall away from Thee, O All-Compassionate One.[^9657]

Whosoever departs unto the earth his mother, is loosed again to receive torments, or the rewards of life lived.[^9658]

*Glory:* By the Holy Spirit, theology is a thrice-holy Unity: for the Father is unoriginate, of Whom the Son was agelessly begotten, and the Spirit, co-enthroned, co-formed, who shone forth from the Father.[^9659]

*Both now: The same.*[^9660]

*Antiphon 4:*
Behold now, what is so good or what is so pleasant, but for brethren to dwell together? For in this the Lord hath promised life eternal.[^9661]

He that adorns the lilies of the field commands that it is not fitting to be anxious concerning one's garment.[^9662]

*Glory:* By the Holy Spirit, as the single Cause, all things are sustained peace-bestowing; for this is God, consubstantial with the Father and the Son in lordship.[^9663]

*Both now: The same.*[^9664]

*Prokeimenon, Tone 8:*
The Lord shall reign for ever, thy God, O Zion, unto generation and generation [LXX Ps. 145:10].
*Verse: Praise the Lord, O my soul; I will praise the Lord in my life [LXX Ps. 145:1-2].*[^9665]

*Let every breath:*
*Verse: Praise God in His Saints: Resurrectional Morning Gospel. Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ: Psalm 50: and the rest in order.*[^9666]

*Resurrectional Canon.*
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:* The charioteer Pharaoh did the wonder-working rod of Moses once plunge into the deep, striking crosswise and dividing the sea; but it saved fugitive Israel marching on foot, as they sang a song unto God.[^9667]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy holy Resurrection.*[^9668]

*Troparion 1:* How should we not marvel at the almighty Godhead of Christ? Which from the passions pours forth dispassion and incorruption unto all the faithful, and from...[^9669]

...the holy rib trickling a fountain of immortality, and life everlasting from the tomb.[^9670]

*Troparion 2:* How comely did an Angel now appear unto the women, bearing bright natural images of immaterial purity, and by his aspect announcing the light of the Resurrection, crying: The Lord is risen![^9671]

*Theotokion:* Most glorious things have been spoken of thee in generations of generations, who didst contain God the Word in thy womb, yet remainedst pure, O Virgin Mary Theotokos; therefore do we all honor thee, our true protection after God.[^9672]

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Having passed through the water as dry land, and having escaped the evils of Egypt, the Israelite cried aloud: Let us sing unto our Deliverer and God.[^9673]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy Cross and Resurrection.*[^9674]

*Troparion 1:* The gates of pain were shattered, and the gatekeepers of hades were struck with fear, beholding Him Who descended into the nethermost parts, Who is on high above all nature.[^9675]

*Troparion 2:* The angelic ranks were amazed, beholding human nature, which had fallen and was shut up in the nethermost parts of the earth, seated upon the Father's throne.[^9676]

*Theotokion:* The angelic and human ranks unceasingly praise thee, O unwedded Mother; for their Creator hast thou borne as a babe in thine arms.[^9677]

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Let us sing unto the Lord, Who led His people through the Red Sea, for He alone is gloriously glorified.[^9678]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^9679]

*Troparion 1:* O Most Pure Theotokos, who didst bear the incarnate, ever-existing, and supremely divine Word beyond nature: we sing unto thee.[^9680]

*Troparion 2:* A life-bearing cluster of grapes, trickling the sweetness of universal salvation, did the Virgin bear, even Christ.[^9681]

*Troparion 3:* The race of Adam, having been raised up through thee unto blessedness beyond understanding, O Theotokos, worthily glorifies thee.[^9682]

*Katavasia: I shall open my mouth:*[^9683]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens with understanding, and didst found the earth upon the waters: establish me, O Christ, upon the rock of the Church; for there is none holy save Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^9684]

*Troparion 1:* Adam having become condemned by the tasting of sin, Thou didst justify by the saving passion of Thy flesh, O Christ; for Thou Thyself wast not liable to the trial of death, O Sinless One.[^9685]

*Troparion 2:* The light of the Resurrection dawned upon those in darkness and sitting in the shadow of death,[^9686] even Jesus my God; and having bound the strong one by His Godhead, He plundered his vessels.[^9687]

*Theotokion:* Higher than the Cherubim and Seraphim hast thou been shown to be, O Theotokos; for thou alone didst receive the uncontainable God in thy womb, O Undefiled One; therefore do all we, the faithful, ever bless thee with hymns.[^9688]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 8. Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord, Maker of the vault of heaven and Builder of the Church: do Thou establish me in Thy love, O summit of desires, firm support of the faithful, O only Lover of mankind.[^9689]

*Troparion 1:* Having alienated myself from the commandment of Him Who truly made me, O Lord, Thou, having fashioned Thyself into my form and taught obedience through Thyself, didst renew me by Thy crucifixion.[^9690]

*Troparion 2:* O Lord, Who by wisdom didst foresee all things, and by Thine understanding didst establish the nethermost parts: Thou didst not deem it unworthy, by Thy condescension, O Word of God, to raise up that which was made according to Thine image.[^9691]

*Theotokion:* Having bodily made Thy dwelling in the Virgin, O Lord, Thou didst appear unto mankind as it was fitting to see Thee; and her hast Thou revealed as truly the Theotokos, and helper of the faithful, O only Lover of mankind.[^9692]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 8. Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou art the firm foundation of those who flee unto Thee, O Lord; Thou art the light of the darkened, and my spirit sings of Thee.[^9693]

*Troparion 1:* Grant us help by thy prayers, O All-Pure One, repelling the assaults of grievous tribulations.[^9694]

*Troparion 2:* Thou wast the restoration of our foremother Eve, O Theotokos, having borne Christ, the Author of life for the world.[^9695]

*Troparion 3:* Gird me with power, O All-Pure One, who didst truly bear God in the flesh, the hypostatic Power of the Father.[^9696]

*Resurrectional Canon.*
*Tone 8. Ode 4. Heirmos:* Thou art my strength, O Lord, Thou art my power, Thou art my God, Thou art my rejoicing; Who, without leaving the bosom of the Father, didst visit our po-...

...verty; therefore with the Prophet Habakkuk I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind.[^9697]

*Troparion 1:* Being Thine enemy, Thou didst love me exceedingly; by a strange emptying Thou didst descend to earth, O Compassionate Savior, not rejecting my uttermost humiliation; and remaining on the height of Thine ineffable glory, Thou didst glorify him who was formerly dishonored.[^9698]

*Troparion 2:* Beholding, O Master, who is not now amazed at death being destroyed by passion, corruption fleeing through the Cross, and hades emptied of its riches through death? A marvelous deed of the divine power of Thee, Who wast crucified, O Lover of mankind.[^9699]

*Theotokion:* Thou art the praise of the faithful, O Unwedded One; thou art the protectress, thou art the refuge of Christians, a wall and a haven; for thou bearest petitions unto thy Son, O All-Blameless One, and dost save from perils those who with faith and love know thee as the pure Theotokos.[^9700]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 8. Ode 4. Heirmos:* I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I considered Thy works, and glorified Thy Godhead.[^9701]

*Troparion 1:* The children of the lawless nailed Thee upon the Cross, O Christ God; by which, as Compassionate, Thou didst save those who glorify Thy sufferings.[^9702]

*Troparion 2:* Having risen from the tomb, Thou didst raise up together all the dead who were in hades, and didst enlighten, as Compassionate, those who glorify Thy Resurrection.[^9703]

*Theotokion:* Pray unto God, Whom thou didst bear, O most pure Mary, to grant unto thy servants the forgiveness of sins.[^9704]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 8. Ode 4. Same Heirmos.*
*Troparion 1:* O untilled field, who didst bring forth the life-creating ear of corn that gives life unto the world, O Theotokos: save those who sing of thee.[^9705]

*Troparion 2:* Having been enlightened, we all proclaim thee to be the Theotokos, O All-Pure One; for thou didst bear the Sun of righteousness, O Ever-Virgin.[^9706]

*Troparion 3:* Grant cleansing unto our ignorances, as Thou art sinless, and give peace unto Thy world, O God, through the prayers of her who gave Thee birth.[^9707]

*Resurrectional Canon.*
*Tone 8. Ode 5. Heirmos:* Why hast Thou cast me off from Thy presence, O Never-Setting Light, and alien darkness hath covered me, the wretched one? But convert me, and direct my paths unto the light of Thy commandments, I pray.[^9708]

*Troparion 1:* Mocked before Thy passion, O Savior, Thou didst endure to be clothed in purple, covering the shapeless nakedness of the first-fashioned; and naked in the flesh wast Thou nailed upon the Cross, stripping off, O Christ, the garment of deadness.[^9709]

*Troparion 2:* Having risen from the dust of death, Thou didst build up again my fallen essence, O Christ; and Thou didst make it unaging, showing it once more as a royal image, glistening with the light of incorruption.[^9710]

*Theotokion:* Having motherly boldness toward thy Son, O All-Pure One, despise not, we pray, the kindred providence for us; for thee alone do Christians mercifully offer unto the Master as propitiation.[^9711]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 8. Ode 5. Heirmos:* Enlighten us by Thy commandments, O Lord, and by Thine arm on high grant us Thy peace, O Lover of mankind.[^9712]

*Troparion 1:* Guide us by the power of Thy Cross, O Christ, for by it we fall down before Thee; grant us peace, O Lover of mankind.[^9713]

*Troparion 2:* Steer our life, as Most Good, as we sing of Thy rising; and grant us peace, O Lover of mankind.[^9714]

*Theotokion:* Beseech thy Son and our God, O pure and unwedded Mary, most pure one, to send down great mercy upon us, the faithful.[^9715]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 8. Ode 5. Heirmos:* Rising early from the night, we cry unto Thee: O Lord, save us; for Thou art our God, and we know none other beside Thee.[^9716]

*Troparion 1:* Still the unbearable tempest of my passions, O thou who didst bear God, the Helmsman and Lord.[^9717]

*Troparion 2:* The angelic ranks and the assembly of men serve thy Childbearing, O most pure Theotokos.[^9718]

*Troparion 3:* O Mary Theotokos, unwedded one: bring to naught the hopes of the enemies, and gladden those who sing of thee.[^9719]

*Resurrectional Canon.*
*Tone 8. Ode 6. Heirmos:* Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine iniquities; and lead me up from the depth of evils, I pray; for unto Thee have I cried, and hear me, O God of my salvation.[^9720]

*Troparion 1:* By a tree did the author of evil mightily cast me down; but Thou, O Christ, having been lifted up upon the Cross, didst more mightily cast him down, putting him to shame, and didst raise up him who had fallen.[^9721]

*Troparion 2:* Having shone forth from the tomb, Thou didst show compassion unto Zion, having by Thy divine blood made it new instead of old, as Compassionate; and now Thou reignest therein unto the ages, O Christ.[^9722]

*Theotokion:* May we be delivered from grievous transgressions by thy petitions, O pure Mother of God; and may we obtain the divine radiance of the Son of God, ineffably incarnate of thee, O Most Pure One.[^9723]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 8. Ode 6. Heirmos:* I will pour out my prayer unto the Lord, and unto Him will I declare my griefs; for my soul is filled with evils, and my life hath drawn near unto hades; and like Jonah I pray: Lead me up from corruption, O God.[^9724]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst stretch out Thine hands upon the Cross, healing the hand of the first-fashioned incontinently stretched out in Eden; and having of Thine own will tasted gall, Thou didst save, as Mighty, those who glorify Thy sufferings, O Christ.[^9725]

*Troparion 2:* The Deliverer tasted of death and of the ancient condemnation, that He might destroy the kingdom of corruption; and having descended into the nethermost parts, Christ rose again and saved, as Mighty, those who sing of His Resurrection.[^9726]

*Theotokion:* Cease not to pray for us, O most pure Theotokos and Virgin, for thou art the firm foundation of the faithful; by hope in thee and love for thee are we strengthened, and Him Who was ineffably incarnate of thee do we glorify.[^9727]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 8. Ode 6. Heirmos:* Grant me a robe of light, Thou Who deckest Thyself with light as with a garment, O greatly merciful Christ our God.[^9728]

*Troparion 1:* A temple of God and an ark, an animate bridal chamber and a heavenly portal, do we, the faithful, proclaim thee to be, O Theotokos.[^9729]

*Troparion 2:* Having as God destroyed the idolatrous shrines, Thy Child, O Mary, Bride of God, is worshipped together with the Father and the Spirit.[^9730]

*Troparion 3:* The Word of God showed thee, O Theotokos, to be a heavenly ladder unto earthly beings; for through thee He came down unto us.[^9731]

*Kontakion, Tone 8.*
*Tone 8, Podoben: "As first-fruits of nature" (Ꙗ҆́кѡ нача́тки):*
Having risen from the tomb, Thou didst raise up the dead and didst resurrect Adam; and Eve keeps festival in Thy Resurrection, and the ends of the world celebrate Thine arising from the dead, O Greatly Merciful One.[^9732]

*Ikos:*
Having despoiled the kingdoms of hades and resurrected the dead, O Long-Suffering One, Thou didst meet the Myrrh-bearing women, bringing joy instead of sorrow; and unto Thine Apostles Thou didst announce the tokens of victory, O my Savior, Giver of life, and Thou dost enlighten creation, O Lover of mankind! Therefore the world also rejoices together at Thine arising from the dead, O Greatly Merciful One.[^9733]

*Resurrectional Canon.*
*Tone 8. Ode 7. Heirmos:* The fire was put to shame before God's condescension in Babylon of old; therefore the Children in the furnace, dancing with joyful foot as in a meadow of flowers, sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9734]

*Troparion 1:* The glorious emptying, the divine wealth of Thy poverty, O Christ, amazes the Angels, as they behold Thee nailed upon the Cross to save those who cry with faith: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9735]

*Troparion 2:* By Thy divine descent Thou didst fill the nethermost parts with light, and the darkness that formerly pursued was driven away; whence the prisoners from the ages arose, crying: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^9736]

*Triadicon:* Proclaiming Thee in orthodox manner as Lord of all, and as the only Father unto the only Only-begotten Son, we bless Thee, and we acknowledge the one upright Spirit proceeding from Thee, co-natural and co-eternal.[^9737]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 8. Ode 7. Heirmos:* The Children having arrived from Judea in Babylon of old, by faith in the Trinity trampled down the flame of the furnace, singing: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^9738]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst work salvation in the midst of the earth, as was prophesied, O God; for having been lifted up upon the Tree, Thou didst call all who cry with faith: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^9739]

*Troparion 2:* Having risen from the tomb as from sleep, O Compassionate One, Thou didst deliver all from corruption; and creation is assured by the Apostles who proclaim the Resurrection: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^9740]

*Triadicon:* We worship the Father, and honor the Son, and the Holy Spirit, confessing three consubstantial Hypostases, and one God and Lord, crying aloud: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^9741]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 8. Ode 7. Same Heirmos.*
*Troparion 1:* Having become incarnate from virginal loins, Thou didst appear for our salva-...

...tion; therefore, acknowledging Thy Mother as Theotokos, we cry in orthodox manner: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^9742]

*Troparion 1:* Thou didst bud forth as a rod from the root of Jesse, O All-Blessed Virgin, bearing flower-blossoming fruit of salvation unto those who cry with faith unto thy Son: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^9743]

*Troparion 2:* Fill all with wisdom and divine power, O hypostatic Wisdom of the Most High, through the Theotokos, who sing unto Thee with faith: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^9744]

*Resurrectional Canon.*
*Tone 8. Ode 8. Heirmos:* Sevenfold did the Chaldean tormentor furiously heat the furnace for the godly ones; but seeing them saved by a higher power, he cried unto the Creator and Deliverer: Ye children, bless; ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all the ages.[^9745]

*Troparion 1:* The supremely divine power of Jesus' Godhead hath divinely shone forth in us; for having tasted death on the Cross in the flesh for all, He destroyed the power of hades: Whom unceasingly, ye children, bless; ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all the ages.[^9746]

*Troparion 2:* He Who was crucified is risen; the stiff-necked one hath fallen; he that was fallen and crushed is righted; corruption is cast out, and incorruption hath blossomed; for mortality hath been swallowed up by life. Ye children, bless; ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all the ages.[^9747]

*Triadicon:* The thrice-radiant Godhead, shining forth one radiance from one three-hypostatic essence—the unoriginate Begetter, the Word consubstantial with the Father, and the co-reigning, consubstantial Spirit—ye children, bless; ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all the ages.[^9748]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 8. Ode 8. Heirmos:* Having become victors over the tormentor and the flame by Thy grace, cleaving earnestly unto Thy commandments, the Children cried: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.[^9749]

*Troparion 1:* Him Who stretched out His hands upon the Tree unto me who was naked, and calls me to be warmed by His comely nakedness: bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all the ages.[^9750]

*Troparion 2:* Him Who raised me up, the fallen one, from nethermost hades, and honored me with the high-enthroned glory of the Begetter: bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and supremely exalt Him unto all the ages.[^9751]

*Triadicon:* The indivisible three-hypostatic Godhead depicted in three Persons, in one essence and glory: bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and supremely exalt Him unto all the ages.[^9752]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 8. Ode 8. Heirmos:* The King of heaven, Whom the angelic hosts hymn: praise and supremely exalt Him unto all the ages.[^9753]

*Troparion 1:* Quench the fiery and flaming arrows of the adversaries directed against us, that we may sing of thee unto all the ages.[^9754]

*Troparion 2:* Beyond nature didst thou bear the Creator and Savior, God the Word, O Virgin; therefore do we sing of thee and supremely exalt thee unto all the ages.[^9755]

*Troparion 3:* The unapproachable Light that made His dwelling in thee, O Virgin, revealed thee as an enlightening and golden-radiant lamp unto all the ages.[^9756]

*My soul magnifies the Lord:*[^9757]

*Resurrectional Canon.*
*Tone 8. Ode 9. Heirmos:* Heaven was amazed thereat, and the ends of the earth were astonished, that God appeared bodily unto mankind, and thy womb became more spacious than the heavens; therefore the hierarchies of angels and men magnify thee as Theotokos.[^9758]

*Troparion 1:* Being simple by Thy divine and unoriginate nature, Thou didst become composite by the assumption of the flesh, having hypostasized it within Thyself, O Word of God; and having suffered as man, Thou didst remain beyond passions as God; therefore in two natures, indivisibly and without confusion, we magnify Thee.[^9759]

*Troparion 2:* Having become man by nature, Thou didst call the Father according to the divine essence Thy God, O Most High, condescending unto Thy servants; having risen from the tomb, by grace Thou didst grant Him Who is by nature God and Master to be the Father of the earthborn; with Whom all we magnify Thee.[^9760]

*Theotokion:* Thou wast revealed, O Virgin Mother of God, having beyond nature borne in the flesh God the Word, Whom the Father belched forth from His heart before all ages as Good; Whom we now understand to be higher than bodily forms, even though He clothed Himself in a body.[^9761]

*Canon of the Cross and Resurrection.*
*Tone 8. Ode 9. Heirmos:* Every hearing was affrighted at the ineffable condescension of God, that the Most High willingly came down even unto the flesh from the Virgin's womb, having become man; therefore, we the faithful magnify the most pure Theotokos.[^9762]

*Troparion 1:* We know Thee, O Son, to be God by nature, conceived in the womb of the Mother of God, and having become...

...man for our sake; and beholding Thee upon the Cross, suffering indeed by human nature, but remaining impassible as God, we magnify Thee.[^9763]

*Troparion 2:* The gloomy darkness hath been destroyed; for from hades Christ, the Sun of righteousness, hath shone forth, enlightening all the ends of the earth, shining with the light of the Godhead, the heavenly man, the earthly God: Whom in two natures we magnify.[^9764]

*Theotokion:* Direct, and prosper, and reign, O Son of the Mother of God, subduing the Ishmaelite people that war against us, granting unto our pious Emperor (Name)[^9765] the Cross together with the spear as an invincible weapon, when he comes unto Thee.[^9766]

*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 8. Ode 9. Heirmos:* Confessing thee to be truly the Theotokos, having been saved through thee, O pure Virgin: with the bodiless choirs we magnify thee.[^9767]

*Troparion 1:* Thy memory is full of joy and gladness, pouring forth healings unto those who draw near, who piously proclaim thee to be the Theotokos.[^9768]

*Troparion 2:* With psalms do we sing of thee, O Full of Grace, and unceasingly offer the greeting, "Rejoice!"; for thou didst pour forth joy unto all.[^9769]

*Troparion 3:* Fair sprang forth thy Fruit, O Theotokos, not mediating corruption unto those who partake, but life, unto those who magnify thee with faith.[^9770]

*After the Katavasia, the Little Litany. Then: Holy is the Lord our God. Thereafter the Exapostilarion.*[^9771]

*At the Praises, Resurrectional Stichera. Tone 8.*[^9772]

*Sticheron 1:* O Lord, though Thou didst stand before the tribunal to be judged by Pilate, yet Thou didst not depart from the throne, seated with the Father; and having risen from the dead, Thou didst deliver the world from the bondage of the alien, as Compassionate and Lover of mankind.[^9773]

*Sticheron 2:* O Lord, Thou hast given us Thy Cross as a weapon against the devil; for he trembles and shakes, not enduring to look upon its power; for it raises up the dead and hath abolished death. Therefore do we worship Thy burial and Thy rising.[^9774]

*Sticheron 3:* O Lord, though the Jews laid Thee in the tomb as dead, yet as a sleeping King did the soldiers watch over Thee, and as a treasure of life did they seal Thee with a seal; but Thou didst rise again and didst bestow incorruption upon our souls.[^9775]

*Sticheron 4:* Thine Angel, O Lord, having proclaimed the Resurrection, affrighted the guards, but cried aloud unto the women, saying: Why seek ye the living among the dead? He is risen, being God, and hath granted life unto the universe.[^9776]

*Other Stichera, Anatolian. Same Tone.*[^9777]

*Sticheron 5:* Thou didst suffer by the Cross, being impassible in Thy Godhead; Thou didst accept three-day burial, that Thou mightest free us from the bondage of the enemy; and having made us immortal, Thou dost make us alive by Thy Resurrection, O Christ God, Lover of mankind.[^9778]

*Sticheron 6:* I worship, and glorify, and sing, O Christ, of Thy Resurrection from the tomb; whereby Thou didst free us from the indissoluble bonds of hades, and didst grant unto the world, as God, eternal life and great mercy.[^9779]

*Stichos: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thine hand be lifted up:*[^9780]

*Sticheron 7:* Guarding Thy life-receiving tomb, the transgressors of the law sealed it then with a custody of soldiers; but Thou, as immortal and almighty God, didst rise on the third day.[^9781]

*Stichos: I will confess Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart, I will tell of all Thy marvelous works.*[^9782]

*Sticheron 8:* When Thou didst reach the gates of hades, O Lord, and hadst shattered them, the captive cried aloud thus: Who is this, that He is not condemned in the nethermost parts of the earth, but hath dissolved the prison of death as a shadow? I received Him as dead, and I tremble at Him as God. O Almighty Savior, have mercy upon us.[^9783]

*Glory: The Evangelical Sticheron. Both now: Theotokion: Most blessed art thou: Great Doxology, troparion: Having risen from the tomb: Litanies and Dismissal.*[^9784]

*At the Liturgy, the Beatitudes. Tone 8.*[^9785]

*Troparion 1:* Remember us, O Christ, Savior of the world, even as Thou didst remember the thief upon the Tree; and make all worthy, O only Compassionate One, of Thy heavenly Kingdom.[^9786]

*Troparion 2:* Hear, O Adam, and rejoice with Eve; for he that formerly stripped you both, and through deception took you captive, hath been brought to naught by the Cross of Christ.[^9787]

*Troparion 3:* Having been nailed upon the Tree of Thine own will, O our Savior, Thou didst deliver Adam from the curse of the tree, restoring, as Compassionate, that which is according to Thine image, and the dwelling of Paradise.[^9788]

*Troparion 4:* Today Christ is risen from the tomb, granting incorruption unto all the faithful; and He renews joy unto the Myrrh-bearing women after the Passion and the Resurrection.[^9789]

*Troparion 5:* Rejoice, ye wise Myrrh-bearing women, who first beheld the Resurrection of Christ and announced it unto the Apostles, the recalling of the whole world.[^9790]

*Troparion 6:* As the multitude of Angels in heaven, so also upon earth the human race celebrates the most holy Resurrection of Thy goodness, O Lord.[^9791]

*Troparion 7:* Thou didst raise us up from the grave of sin, O Savior, who were slain by many passions; and by Thy rising Thou didst destroy the tyranny of death, as Lover of mankind.[^9792]

*Glory: Triadicon:* Unoriginate Trinity, indivisible Essence, co-enthroned Unity, equal in honor of glory, supreme-beginning Nature and Kingdom: save those who sing of Thee with faith.[^9793]

*Both now: Theotokion:* Rejoice, holy Theotokos for us. Rejoice, thou that didst bear joy unto the world. Rejoice, only protectress of mankind and salvation of our souls.[^9794]

*On Sunday Evening.*[^9795]
*At Lord, I have cried, Penitential Stichera. Tone 8.*
*Tone 8, Podoben: "O Lord, though Thou didst stand before the tribunal" (Гдⷭ҇и, а҆́ще и҆ сꙋди́лищꙋ):*

*Sticheron 1:* O Lord, Who didst come into the world to call sinners and robbers and publicans, and didst receive the harlot and the prodigal: call me also in Thy love for mankind, who have sinned against Thee, O my Christ, more than all, and am no longer repentant.[^9796]

*Sticheron 2:* O Lord, Who from among publicans dost appoint pastors for Thy Church, and didst make him who was formerly its persecutor now to be its defender: through their prayers reveal me also as Thine own sheep, O Savior, that I, being unprofitable, may not become food for the alien.[^9797]

*Sticheron 3:* Weep, O wretched soul, before the end, and cleave unto God, crying unto Him from the heart and with groanings, O all-defiled one: I have sinned against Thee, O Christ; abhor me not, I pray Thee, but turning me back as Compassionate, vouchsafe me forgiveness.[^9798]

*Then the Stichera of the Menaion for the Saint. If there is no Menaion:*[^9799]
*Other Stichera, to the holy Bodiless Angels.*
*Tone 8, Podoben: "O Lord, though Thou didst stand before the tribunal" (Подо́бенъ то́йже):*[^9800]

*Sticheron 1:* O Lord, the assemblies of the bodiless hosts unceasingly glorify Thee, delighting in Thy beauty and ineffable goodness, and richly illumined with thrice-radiant beams; by whose prayers and intercession, O Savior, save our souls.[^9801]

*Sticheron 2:* O Lord, Thou didst establish the all-comely angelic choirs by Thine all-powerful Word and by Thy Most Holy Spirit, having granted them radiance with unfailing rays; by whose entreaties and intercessions, O Savior, save our souls.[^9802]

*Sticheron 3:* O Lord, the Seraphim hymn Thee, and Powers, and Angels, Principalities, Dominions, Thrones, and Authorities, and Archangels, and the awesome Cherubim offer praise unto Thee; by whose entreaties and intercessions, O Savior, save our souls.[^9803]

*Glory: Both now: Theotokion.*
*Tone 8, Podoben: "O most marvelous wonder" (Ѽ пресла́вное чꙋ́до):*[^9804]

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, slaying of demons! Rejoice, daughter of Adam, flower truly blossomed and unfading! Rejoice, glory of thy servants! Rejoice, O All-Immaculate, release from cruel woes! Rejoice, gift granted by God, saving unto the world! Rejoice, precious protectress of those who call upon thee! Rejoice, O Sovereign Lady, glory of those who glorify thee![^9805]

*O Gladsome Light: The Prokeimenon of the day. Vouchsafe, O Lord:*[^9806]
*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 8.*[^9807]

*Sticheron 1:* The Angels unceasingly hymn Thee, the King and Master; and I fall down before Thee, crying as the publican: O God, cleanse me and have mercy on me.[^9808]

*Stichos: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven... So look our eyes unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^9809]

*Sticheron 2:* Being immortal, O my soul, be not engulfed by the waves of this life; but rise up, crying aloud unto thy Benefactor: O God, cleanse me and save me.[^9810]

*Stichos: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly filled with contempt; our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^9811]

*Martyrion:* O Martyrs of the Lord, ye sanctify every place and heal every sickness; and now pray ye...[^9812]

...that our souls may be delivered from the snares of the enemy, we beseech you.[^9813]

*Glory: Both now: Theotokion.*
*Tone 8, Podoben: "O most marvelous wonder" (Ѽ пресла́вное чꙋ́до):*[^9814]

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, ensouled palace, spiritual dove! Rejoice, city of thy Creator! Rejoice, refuge and bulwark of the faithful, protectress of those who love thee, and salvation and deliverance! Rejoice, bridge that leadest all unto heaven! Rejoice, all-honored holy Sovereign Lady![^9815]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion Prayers. After the Our Father: dismissal troparion, litany, and dismissal.*[^9816]

*On Sunday at Compline.*[^9817]
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:* The wonder-working rod of Moses, striking crosswise and dividing the sea, once engulfed Pharaoh the chariot-driver, but saved Israel the fleeing foot-traveler as he sang a song unto God.[^9818]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^9819]

*Troparion 1:* Glorious things have been spoken of thee, O All-Pure One, who alone hast become the glory of the faithful; therefore by thy prayers show me, who glorify thee, to be a partaker of the glory to come, O most holy and all-hymned Virgin.[^9820]

*Troparion 2:* Blessed is the Fruit of thy womb, O All-Pure One, whereby all we earthborn were delivered from the curse, O blessed and All-Immaculate Lady! O ineffable wonder! O incomprehensible tidings, and salvation of all the faithful![^9821]

*Glory:* The multitude of Angels, the assembly of Disciples and Prophets, the company of Martyrs, and Thy Venerable Ones pray unto Thee ever, O Lord: for the sake of the Theotokos grant us forgiveness of sins, as Lover of mankind.[^9822]

*Both now:* Unto thee, O Most Pure, who didst receive in thy womb the unendurable Fire, I pray with faith: that by thy acceptable prayers I may be delivered and snatched from the fire of Gehenna, and freed from eternal torment.[^9823]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord, Supreme Creator of the celestial vault and Builder of the Church, do Thou establish me in Thy love, O Summit of desire, Confirmation of the faithful, O only Lover of mankind.[^9824]

*Troparion 1:* Having fallen away from a pure life, O All-Pure One, I have become like unto the beasts, and am wholly condemned; O thou that didst bear the Judge, deliver me from all condemnation and save me.[^9825]

*Troparion 2:* Grant me sighing, O Pure One, and a tear of the soul, and perpetual contrition, O Maiden: that I may bewail the passions that hold me captive through ignorance, O Lady full of God's grace.[^9826]

*Glory:* The heavenly ranks, the ministering spirits, the assembly of Prophets and Apostles, and Thy Martyrs pray unto Thee unceasingly, O Compassionate One: grant Thy people remission of transgressions.[^9827]

*Both now:* Beseech the Merciful One, O all-merciful and Most Pure Lady, to vouchsafe me now His divine compassions; for having mercilessly acquired unmerciful ways, I have become worthy of the eternal fire.[^9828]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Thou art my strength, O Lord, Thou art my power, Thou art my God, Thou art my rejoicing; Who didst not leave the bosom of the Father, and didst visit our poverty; therefore with the Prophet Habakkuk I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind.[^9829]

*Troparion 1:* Tear up the malicious handwriting of my sins, O Most Pure One, with the divine spear that pierced the divine side of Him Who willed to become incarnate of thee; and pray Him to inscribe me in the book of the saved, who have foolishly estranged myself from Him.[^9830]

*Troparion 2:* That I may glorify thee with voices of thanksgiving, that I may magnify thee with divine love, O All-Immaculate Lady: who through thy great childbirth didst magnify all those that had estranged themselves; vouchsafe divine forgiveness unto me who have greatly estranged myself and grievously fallen.[^9831]

*Glory:* The laws of nature were abolished in thee, for beyond nature thou didst bear God the Word; therefore with faith I pray thee, O All-Immaculate One: save by thy prayers him who hath sinned exceedingly beyond human nature, and hath estranged himself from God, now turning back.[^9832]

*Both now:* The enemy hath wounded my whole soul with the arrows of sin, and defiled my heart with pleasures, and led me away from the straight path; therefore, O Virgin, I cry unto thee: turn me back, heal me, and save me.[^9833]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Why hast Thou cast me away from Thy countenance, O unwaning Light, and a strange darkness hath covered me, the wretched one? But turn me back, and guide my paths to the light of Thy commandments, I pray Thee.[^9834]

*Troparion 1:* Generations of generations now bless thee, O Virgin, ever-blessed by the heavenly hosts, as having united the earthly with the heavenly; they praise thee, the only blessed among women, the setting-right of fallen Adam.[^9835]

*Troparion 2:* That I may remain whole, and obtain an unshakeable stance, walking well and doing what is pleasing unto God: pray thou, O good Sovereign Lady, who didst bear the Good Word Who delivered us from unreason.[^9836]

*Glory:* O Word, Who didst shed Thy Blood upon the Cross, by the exceeding abyss of Thy mercy dry up the abyss of my passions; and for the sake of the Theotokos vouchsafe me in contrition to be well-pleasing unto Thee, our God.[^9837]

*Both now:* O Virgin Mother Maiden, Bride of God, who didst bear in the flesh the Uncontainable One, the glory of the Angels and the plague of demons: vouchsafe divine healing unto my heart which hath been wounded by sin.[^9838]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine iniquities, and lead me up from the abyss of evils, I pray Thee; for unto Thee have I cried, and hear me, O God of my salvation.[^9839]

*Troparion 1:* Human nature was shown mercy through thee, O merciful Sovereign Lady, for thou didst bear the Merciful One, O All-Pure; therefore I pray thee: have mercy on my soul.[^9840]

*Troparion 2:* The ranks of the bodiless powers, together with Thy Martyrs, Prophets, and Apostles, pray unto Thee, O Christ: for the sake of her that bare Thee, save Thy people from every tribulation.[^9841]

*Glory:* O unplowed land that didst bear the Cultivator and Creator of all: by divine understanding show forth my soul, which hath grown barren with passions, to be fruitful.[^9842]

*Both now:* I have transgressed the commandments that lead unto God, and willy-nilly have become enslaved to wicked passions; therefore I pray thee, O Ever-Virgin: snatch me away from their dominion.[^9843]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (40 times). Glory: Both now:*[^9844]
*Sessional Hymn, Tone 8:* We the faithful praise the Virgin Mother Maiden, the heavenly piety of the angelic powers and the laudable height for our souls: Rejoice, thou that didst conceive in thy womb the Creator of Angels! Rejoice...[^9845]

...thou cleansing of the fallen, who didst incarnate God the Word! Rejoice, O Bride unwedded![^9846]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The fire was abashed in Babylon of old before God's condescension; therefore the Children, dancing with joyful step in the furnace as in a meadow of flowers, sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9847]

*Troparion 1:* By thy ineffable childbirth thou hast made us worthy of awesome and unutterable things, O Theotokos; therefore deliver me from the awesome torment, and fill me with ineffable joy, that I may ever hymn thee, the much-hymned one.[^9848]

*Troparion 2:* Having spent my life in slothfulness, I have become unfruitful, and I dread the examination and the unquenchable fire of Gehenna; O thou that didst bear the unendurable Fire, O Theotokos, hasten to deliver me by thy prayers.[^9849]

*Glory:* The great wonder of thy childbirth is higher than all wonders, O pure Virgin; therefore I cry unto thee, O Mother of God: show marvelous Thy mercies upon me, and deliver and save me from the wrath to come.[^9850]

*Both now:* As the Benefactor God and plenteous in mercy, have compassion, O Savior, for the sake of her that bare Thee, upon me who have lived in slothfulness and disregarded Thy holy laws and Thy precious commandments, O Christ.[^9851]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* In his fury the Chaldean tormentor heated the furnace sevenfold for the godly; but seeing them saved by a higher power, he cried unto the Creator and Deliverer: Ye children, bless; ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all the ages.[^9852]

*Troparion 1:* Quicken my deadened soul, raise it up fallen, heal it wounded; calm my mind, still the waves of temptations, O Virgin, and save me who cry: Ye children, bless; ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^9853]

*Troparion 2:* As the thief I cry unto Thee: Remember me, O Lover of mankind! I weep as the harlot, and I cry: I have sinned, even as of old the prodigal; receive me, the despairing one who repents, for the sake of the Theotokos, that I may fervently cry unto Thee: Ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^9854]

*Glory:* In servant-like manner we cry unto thee, O all-hymned Theotokos, together with the countless noetic Powers, the Prophets and Venerable Ones, and the Apostles and...[^9855]

...Martyrs: make supplication for all men who divinely sing: Ye priests, sing; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^9856]

*Both now:* Thou art more honorable than the transcendent angelic ranks, O all-immaculate Theotokos; for from a virginal and unwedded womb thou didst bear their Creator and Lord, incarnate in two essences, unconfusedly and unchangeably in one Hypostasis, O Theotokos.[^9857]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Every ear trembled at the ineffable condescension of God, that the Most High willingly came down even unto the flesh, having become man from a virginal womb; therefore, we the faithful magnify thee, the most pure Theotokos.[^9858]

*Troparion 1:* O divine bed of Solomon, which the sixty mighty ones, the words of divine Scripture, now surround, upon which God rested: keep me, who am ever surrounded by myriads of demons, unharmed by thy power, O pure Ever-Virgin.[^9859]

*Troparion 2:* O thou that droppest divine sweetness, having borne the Sweetness of all: sweeten my soul, which hath been embittered by the venom of the serpent, alienating me from bitter harm ever through thine intercession, O unashamed Protectress of the faithful.[^9860]

*Glory:* O Jesus, Sun of glory, enlighten my wretched soul through the supplications of the pure Maiden of God who bare Thee, and of the bodiless servants, Thine honored Apostles, the Hierarchs, the Prophets, the chosen Martyrs, and Thy Venerable Ones.[^9861]

*Both now:* I am terrified, O only King, of Thy second and fearful coming, being wholly condemned and perplexed, O Lord; therefore before the end I cry unto Thee: for the sake of her that bare Thee, spare me and save me, O merciful, good Lover of mankind.[^9862]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion Prayers. After the Our Father: dismissal troparion and the rest as usual.*[^9863]

## Monday: The Holy Bodiless Powers[^9864]

*On Monday at Matins.*
*After the first stichology, Penitential Sessional Hymns, Tone 8.*[^9865]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Look upon my humility with a compassionate eye, O Lord, for in a little while my life is ending, and from deeds there is no salvation for me; therefore I pray: look upon my humility with a compassionate eye, O Lord, and save me.[^9866]

*Stichos: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, neither chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^9867]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* Since the Judge is at hand, take heed, O soul, and ponder the hour of the fearful day; for judgment is without mercy unto those who have shown no mercy. Therefore before the end cry aloud: Spare me, O Savior! For Thou alone art without sin.[^9868]

*Glory: Both now: Theotokion:* Having received the command mystically in his mind, the bodiless angel came with haste into Joseph's dwelling, saying unto the Unwedded One: He that bowed the heavens by His descent is contained wholly and unchangeably in thee. And beholding Him having taken a servant's form in thy womb, I am amazed and cry unto thee: Rejoice, O Bride unwedded![^9869]

*After the second stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 8.*[^9870]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Pondering that fearful day, O my soul, watch, kindling thy lamp, illuminating it with oil; for thou knowest not when the voice shall come unto thee, saying: Behold, the Bridegroom! Beware therefore, O my soul, lest thou slumber like the five virgins; but watchfully wait, that thou mayest meet Christ with rich oil, and He will grant thee the divine bridal chamber of His glory.[^9871]

*Stichos: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, neither chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^9872]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* As the harlot I fall down before Thee, that I may receive remission, and instead of myrrh I offer tears from my heart unto Thee: that even as her Thou mayest have compassion on me, O Savior, and grant me cleansing of sins; for like her I cry unto Thee: deliver me from the mire of my deeds.[^9873]

*Stichos: God is marvelous in His saints, the God of Israel. Martyrion:*[^9874]

*Martyrion:* Today this temple is illumined with heavenly light; for in it the angelic hosts rejoice, and with them the choirs of the righteous make merry in memory of the passion-bearers. By their prayers send down peace upon Thy world, O Christ, and great mercy.[^9875]

*Glory: Both now: Theotokion:* The Master and Creator of all, having taken flesh from thy most pure womb, showed thee forth as protectress unto men, O All-Immaculate Lady. Therefore we all flee unto thee, asking cleansing of transgressions, O Sovereign Lady, and...[^9876]

...to be delivered from eternal torment and from every anguish there (we pray thee); that with faith we may cry unto thee: pray unto Christ God to grant remission unto all, O Maiden, who with faith flee unto thy protection.[^9877]

*After the third stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 8.*
*Tone 8, Podoben: "That which was commanded mystically" (Повелѣ́нное):*[^9878]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* How long, O my soul, dost thou live in negligence? How long dost thou pine away in despondency? Arise from the sleep of despondency, O wretched one, and taking thy deeds to heart, groan and tremble at the sentence of the righteous Judge. What wilt thou answer in that hour? Or how wilt thou escape the burning that awaits thee, remaining uncorrected? Before the end cry aloud unto the Judge: Grant me remission of transgressions, O Savior, for Thou alone art long-suffering.[^9879]
