*Sessional Hymn 2:* Receive before the end, O soul, the groaning of the publican and the lamentation of the harlot; cease not confessing with lamentation and weeping unto Him Who knows thy secret deeds, crying aloud with fasting and vigilance and prayer: I have sinned! Cleanse me, O Savior, through the prayers of Thine Angels, as Thou art compassionate, and save me.[^9880]

*Glory: Both now: Theotokion:* O pure and blessed Lady rejoiced by God, for the sake of the mercy of compassions of Him Who was born of thee, unceasingly pray with the Hosts on high and the Archangels, and with all the Bodiless Powers, to give us before the end pardon and cleansing of sins, and correction of life, that we may find mercy.[^9881]

*Penitential Canon to our Lord Jesus Christ, and to His Holy Martyrs.*
*(Whose acrostic is: Snatch me from the fire of Gehenna, O my Christ; Joseph's).*
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Having passed through the water as on dry land, and having escaped the Egyptian evil, the Israelite cried aloud: Let us sing unto our Deliverer and God.[^9882]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*[^9883]

*Troparion 1:* Deliver me from Gehenna, which I have procured for myself by improper deeds, O Deliverer; and kindle in my thought the divine fire of Thy love.[^9884]

*Troparion 2:* Having been darkened, I bowed down unto passions, and appeared irrational, though honored with reason, O Lord: grant recovery unto my soul, and by the judgments that Thou knowest, save me.[^9885]

*Refrain: Holy Martyrs, pray to God for us.*[^9886]

*Martyrion 1:* Having their body tormented with scourgings, the all-lauded crown-bearing martyrs manifested an upright and unbending disposition, and love toward the Creator.[^9887]

*Martyrion 2:* Rivers of blood having flowed from the holy bodies of Thy glorious passion-...[^9888]

...bearers, choked the thorns of idolatry by Thy power, O Compassionate One.[^9889]

*Theotokion:* Heal me, O Pure Lady, who am grievously diseased with deadly sin, from carnal and spiritual passions, that with faith I may ever call thee blessed.[^9890]

*Second Canon to the Bodiless Holy Angels.*
*(Whose acrostic is according to the alphabet. A creation of Kyr Theophanes).*
*Tone 8. Heirmos the same.*[^9891]

*Refrain: Holy Archangels and Angels, pray to God for us.*[^9892]

*Troparion 1:* As chiefs of the holy Angels, enjoying the vision of God in radiance, pray unto the Giver of good and the Savior for us, O Archangels.[^9893]

*Troparion 2:* Held fast by dire distresses, we the faithful now flee unto you as our defenders, O divine Archangels: earnestly entreat the Master now for us.[^9894]

*Theotokion:* Be thou unto me a refuge, O Virgin, a harbor, a wall, and a protectress, O Mother of God, who didst bear God in the flesh, the all-compassionate Deliverer.[^9895]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord, Supreme Creator of the celestial vault and Builder of the Church, do Thou establish me in Thy love, O Summit of desire, Confirmation of the faithful, O only Lover of mankind.[^9896]

*Troparion 1:* With what eyes shall I then behold Thee, O Christ Savior, having kept not one of Thy commandments on earth? How shall I stand before Thine unendurable throne, giving account of mine immeasurable evils?[^9897]

*Troparion 2:* Stretching forth the hands of Thy compassions, receive me even as formerly the prodigal, who likewise have become enslaved to dishonorable passions and estranged from Thee, O Jesus, most good Lover of mankind.[^9898]

*Martyrion 1:* Having been established upon the rock of the knowledge of God, O passion-bearing Martyrs of Christ: with the weapon of faith ye uprooted the thorns of senselessness, and cultivated the fruits of suffering.[^9899]

*Martyrion 2:* Let the Martyrs be praised, the unshakable pillars, the all-radiant adornments of the Church of the true faith, the most sacred sheep of Christ slain of their own will.[^9900]

*Theotokion:* I have acquired thee, O Most Holy Lady, as a guardian in tribulations; thee...[^9901]

...after God I have as my sleepless protectress; may I find thee, O Most Pure One, delivering me from all condemnation in the day of judgment.[^9902]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Heirmos the same.*[^9903]

*Troparion 1:* Adorned with manifold gifts of the angelic rank, appearing as leaders of the order, O Archistrategoi, steadfastly preserve the churches of Christ by your intercessions. (Twice)[^9904]

*Troparion 2:* Crowned now with the beauties of orthodoxy, the fulfillment of the faithful, with the weapon of good-will: deliver us from distresses as glorious protectors of God, O divine Archangels.[^9905]

*Theotokion:* Thou wast the divine tabernacle of incorruptible life, revealed as the only pure Virgin Mother from the ages: therefore, O All-Hymned Lady, guide me who am in the shadow of death unto life now by thy prayers.[^9906]

*Penitential Canon. Ode 4. Heirmos:* From the overshadowing mountain, even the only Theotokos, the Prophet with divine vision perceived Thee, O Word, about to become incarnate, and with fear he glorified Thy power.[^9907]

*Troparion 1:* Great is the struggle of the departing soul, fearful the trembling, when the Judge sits, and men who have sinned are judged! Woe is me! What shall I do, the condemned one?[^9908]

*Troparion 2:* Having a heart filled with defilements, and the barely bearable burdens of sins, I flee unto Thy compassions, O Master: despise me not, but have compassion on me, I pray Thee.[^9909]

*Martyrion 1:* Precious was your death in the sight of God, O valiant passion-bearers; for ye did not deny Him, though surrounded by myriads of pains and wounds.[^9910]

*Martyrion 2:* The enemy was wounded by the wounding of the martyrs, his prideful blood fell: and the Bestower of their crowns is magnified, glorified in divine songs.[^9911]

*Theotokion:* Thee, the pure receptacle of the Master, O Mother of God, I pray with faith: cleanse me from every defilement, and reveal me as a dwelling of the all-effecting and Holy Spirit.[^9912]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 4. Heirmos:* I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I have understood Thy works, and I have glorified Thy Godhead.[^9913]

*Troparion 1:* O foremost pair of the assembly of Archangels, those who re-...[^9914]

...who flee unto your protection, save ye from every calamity. (Twice)[^9915]

*Troparion 2:* Beholders of the divine brightness and beneficence, O all-glorious Archistrategoi, enlighten now your flock.[^9916]

*Theotokion:* Sacredly do the divine hosts of the bodiless hymn thee, O all-immaculate Mother of God; for thou didst bear their Creator.[^9917]

*Penitential Canon. Ode 5. Heirmos:* Enlighten us by Thy commandments, O Lord, and by Thine arm on high grant us Thy peace, O only Lover of mankind.[^9918]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*[^9919]

*Troparion 1:* By a deceitful disposition have I provoked Thee to anger, O Lord, doing improper things; but have compassion on me who return, and save me.[^9920]

*Troparion 2:* Having a mind that rises not above evil things, I am insensible, senseless one that I am; resolve my perplexity, O Jesus, and save me.[^9921]

*Martyrion 1:* The Giver of light hath set you, O divine Martyrs, as stones shining with the knowledge of God, and destroying the darkness of error.[^9922]

*Martyrion 2:* Unto deceitful laws ye opposed the divine laws, and proclaiming God, ye eagerly gave yourselves over to be put to death for His sake.[^9923]

*Theotokion:* Put to death, O Pure Lady, the movements of my flesh, who by thy childbearing, O Maiden, didst put to death the living sin of the forefather.[^9924]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Same Heirmos.*

*Troparion 1:* Standing round about God, O Archistrategoi, and illumined with the rays streaming thence, preserve your flock. (Twice)[^9925]

*Troparion 2:* Pray ye unto our Master and God that deliverance be granted unto us, as protectors of the deliverance of all.[^9926]

*Theotokion:* Mystically do we call thee true Myrrh, O All-Immaculate, who didst bear God in the flesh, Who pours forth the gifts of sweet fragrance.[^9927]

*Penitential Canon. Ode 6. Heirmos:* Jonah alone didst Thou settle in the sea-monster, O Lord; but me who am bound in the nets of the enemy, as him from corruption, save Thou.[^9928]

*Troparion 1:* Shine upon me the sun-like light of repentance, O Christ Light-Giver, who am in the darkness of transgressions, that I may sing of Thy goodness.[^9929]

*Troparion 2:* Before Thy fearful judgment-seat...[^9930]

...I tremble ever, yet from evil deeds I desist not, serving evil custom; correct me, O Christ, that I may sing of Thy goodness.[^9931]

*Martyrion 1:* The choirs of the honorable martyrs suffered, and conquered the demonic legions, and joined in joy with the angelic choirs; by their prayers, O Lord, save our souls.[^9932]

*Martyrion 2:* By Thy strength didst Thou show Thy martyrs to be strong, O Lord, and they cast down all the power of the ancient supplanter; by their prayers, O Lord, save our souls.[^9933]

*Theotokion:* That with thankful voices I may ever praise thee, O All-Immaculate, drive away the darkness of my soul, and by the light of repentance loose my gloomy transgressions.[^9934]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 6. Heirmos:* I will pour out my prayer unto the Lord, and unto Him will I declare my griefs; for my soul is filled with evils, and my life hath drawn near unto hades; and like Jonah I pray: Lead me up from corruption, O God.[^9935]

*Troparion 1:* Having now been vouchsafed to stand unwaveringly before the awesome throne, O God-beholders, and to be filled with the radiance of the Holy Trinity: pray ye that we who flee unto you be delivered from tribulations and passions, O Archangels. (Twice)[^9936]

*Troparion 2:* Pray ye, O God-beholding Archangels, Gabriel and Michael all-glorious, that those who hymn you in songs may attain unto the Master's pilgrimage, and be vouchsafed everlasting joy and divine brightness.[^9937]

*Theotokion:* Let the ensouled bridal chamber of the Master be praised, which is bright with the radiant beams of virginity, like a lily shining in the midst of the thorny turmoil, even the most pure and comely Virgin Theotokos.[^9938]

*Penitential Canon. Ode 7. Heirmos:* The Children that went down from Judea in Babylon of old, by faith in the Trinity trampled upon the furnace flame, singing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9939]

*Troparion 1:* Grant me groaning, O Christ, even as of old unto the publican and as unto the harlot, having washed away with tears the festerings of mine evils; and have compassion on me who cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9940]

*Troparion 2:* Having fallen among soul-destroying thieves, O Savior, and grievously...[^9941]

...wounded, heal me with the oil of sincere repentance, and grant me compunction to sing unto Thee: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9942]

*Martyrion 1:* Ye died unto the world, and in no wise denied the life-bearing Christ Who became dead, O valiant Passion-bearers, suffering and singing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9943]

*Martyrion 2:* Confessing the one nature of the Trinity in three Hypostases, O wise Passion-bearers, ye destroyed the polytheistic error of idols, singing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9944]

*Theotokion:* God the Word of God, having dwelt in thy womb, O pure Virgin Mother, shows thee forth as protectress unto all that are wronged, who cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9945]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Same Heirmos.*

*Troparion 1:* Manifold grace hath the Benefactor of all granted unto you, O divine Archistrategoi; whose Church now singing, save ye: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers. (Twice)[^9946]

*Troparion 2:* Strengthened by the power of the All-Seeing One, ye clearly oversee all the ends of the earth, and save all who sing with faith: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9947]

*Theotokion:* Having thee now as a saving haven, O Virgin Theotokos, we escape worldly perils and turmoils, crying unto thy Son: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^9948]

*Penitential Canon. Ode 8. Heirmos:* Him that was glorified on the holy mountain, and in the bush through fire revealed the mystery of the Ever-Virgin unto Moses, praise ye the Lord, and supreme exalt Him unto all ages.[^9949]

*Troparion 1:* Brought down by carnal self-indulgence, and foolishly having distanced myself from Thee, O Word, and having become like unto all the beasts: despise me not, O Savior, but turning me back before the end, save me.[^9950]

*Troparion 2:* In no wise do I desist from sins, never working conversion, and I cry, wretched one that I am: I have sinned against Thee, O Lord; soften my hardened soul, O Compassionate One.[^9951]

*Martyrion 1:* The valiant passion-bearers of the Lord, having been baptized with streams of blood, were not defiled with second blemishes, and as crown-bearers they dance unceasingly with the Angels,[^9952] *Martyrion 2:* Girded with the hope of future blessings, the martyrs of piety bravely endured the cruelty of torments; and having finished their course, they stand unceasingly before the throne of the Master.[^9953]

*Theotokion:* Thou art the confirmation of those that stand, and the restoration of those that have fallen, O Virgin; therefore raise me up who have fallen, that I may glorify thee, the blessed one full of grace.[^9954]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 8. Heirmos:* The King of heaven, Whom the angelic hosts hymn, praise ye and supreme exalt Him unto all ages.[^9955]

*Troparion 1:* The protectors and chanters of Thine in- ineffable and unutterable glory, receive Thou, O Christ, now praying unto Thee unto ages. (Twice)[^9956]

*Troparion 2:* O only Good Christ, receive now the angelic hosts praying unto Thee for us, who hymn Thee unto ages.[^9957]

*Theotokion:* With thy brightest radiants, O Mother of God, thou dost enlighten those who sing of thee with faith, who praise thee unto all ages.[^9958]

*My soul doth magnify the Lord: with the refrain: More honorable than the Cherubim:*[^9959]

*Penitential Canon. Ode 9. Heirmos:* Every ear was terrified at the ineffable condescension of God, that the Most High willingly came down even unto the flesh, having become man from a Virgin's womb; therefore, the all-pure Theotokos do we, the faithful, magnify.[^9960]

*Troparion 1:* Even as of old Thou didst cleanse the harlot that fell down before Thee with weeping, O Savior; and even as Thou justifiedst the publican, O Word, who only groaned; and even as Thou didst accept Manasseh, and even as Thou hadst mercy on David who repented: so also receive me, and save me, O Lover of mankind.[^9961]

*Troparion 2:* Groan and shed tears, O soul; desist from thy former transgressions, and fall down openly before Him Who knows thy secrets, and cry warmly: I have sinned against Thee, O Lord; freely have compassion on me, Thou Who hast a heart of compassions, O Plenteous in mercy.[^9962]

*Martyrion 1:* The God-radiant passion-bearers suffered patiently upon earth, and have now received the unshakable inheritance of the kingdom, and rejoicing they partake of heavenly sweetness; by their prayers, O Christ God, make us partakers of Thy glory.[^9963]

*Martyrion 2:* Ye were shown forth as luminaries, having enlightened the unwaning Orient, and ye destroyed the night of godlessness, O All-Wise Ones,...[^9964]

...and with sacred rays ye enlighten all who magnify your light-bearing feast, O Passion-bearers.[^9965]

*Theotokion:* Enlighten, O Sovereign Lady, door of light, the eyes of my heart, I pray, which the deep darkness of sin hath darkened; and send down upon me the dawn of repentance, and from eternal fire, by thine intercession, O Pure One, set me free.[^9966]

*Canon to the Bodiless Powers. Ode 9. Heirmos:* Confessing thee in truth as the Theotokos, saved through thee, O pure Virgin, with the bodiless choirs we magnify thee.[^9967]

*Troparion 1:* Show now Thy Church to be an imitator of the choirs of the Bodiless in virtue, O Christ, fencing Thy flock with Angels.[^9968]

*Troparion 2:* Pray ye, all-glorious Angels, our protectors, that salvation from God may be granted unto our souls who flee under your protection.[^9969]

*Theotokion:* As a dawn unto those in darkness and wandering, O Virgin, holding the Sun of righteousness in thy hands, Christ, didst thou shine forth.[^9970]

*It is truly meet: Little Litany, Exapostilarion, and the usual Psalms.*[^9971]

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 8.*[^9972]

*Sticheron 1:* When I take into my mind the multitude of evils wrought by me, and come to the thought of that fearful examination, seized with trembling I flee unto Thee, the God Who lovest mankind; therefore despise me not, I pray Thee, O only Sinless One: grant compunction unto my humble soul before the end, and save me.[^9973]

*Stichos: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad... And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^9974]

*Sticheron 2:* Grant me tears, O God, even as formerly unto the sinful woman; and vouchsafe me to wet Thy feet, which freed me from the way of error, and to offer unto Thee as sweet-smelling myrrh a pure life, created for me by repentance: that I also may hear Thy desired voice: Thy faith hath saved thee, go in peace.[^9975]

*Stichos: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and establish Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands establish Thou. Martyrion:*[^9976]

*Martyrion:* What shall we call you, O Saints? Cherubim? For upon you Christ hath rested. Seraphim? For unceasingly ye glorified Him. Angels? For ye turned away from the body. Powers? For ye...[^9977]

...work wonders. Many are your names, and greater are your gifts. Pray that our souls may be saved.[^9978]

*Glory: Both now: Theotokion:* The heavenly hosts sing of thee, O Maiden full of grace, unwedded Mother; and we glorify thine unfathomable childbearing, O Theotokos: pray that our souls may be saved.[^9979]

*It is good to give praise: Trisagion Prayers; after the Our Father: dismissal troparion, litany, and the rest.*[^9980]

*On Monday, the Beatitudes, Tone 8.*[^9981]

*Troparion 1:* Remember us, O Christ, Savior of the world, even as Thou rememberedst the thief upon the Tree; and vouchsafe all, O only Compassionate One, Thy heavenly kingdom.[^9982]

*Troparion 2:* Heal, O Christ, the passions of my soul, O only Physician of souls and bodies; and having washed me with streams of compunction, cleanse me wholly as Thou art tender-hearted.[^9983]

*Troparion 3:* Standing round about the throne of the honorable Godhead, Thrones, Cherubim, Principalities, and Authorities, the other chieftains of the bodiless hosts, sacredly sing.[^9984]

*Troparion 4:* Kindled with the burning of the love of Christ, the sufferers remained unburned in the midst of the fire, having burned up the thorns of impiety by divine grace.[^9985]

*Glory: Triadikon:* O Thrice-Holy Trinity, unity supremely originating, deliver Thy servants from unquenchable fire, through the entreaties of Thy divine bodiless powers, and vouchsafe them Thy kingdom.[^9986]

*Both now: Theotokion:* Isaiah once foresaw thee as a scroll, O Lady rejoicing in God, wherein with the Father's finger was the Word ineffably written, inscribing us in the book of life.[^9987]

## Tuesday: The Holy Forerunner[^9988]

*On Monday Evening.*[^9989]
*On "Lord, I have cried," Penitential Stichera, Tone 8.*[^9990]
*Tone 8, Podoben: "O most marvelous wonder" (Ѽ пресла́внагѡ чꙋдесѐ):*[^9991]

*Sticheron 1:* Neither compunction have I acquired, nor a fountain of tears, nor warm confession, nor weeping that cleanses, nor contrition of heart; neither have I become an emulator of the publican, nor of the har-...[^9992]

...-lot, nor of the prodigal youth. How then shall I find forgiveness of my many sins? But by whatever judgments Thou knowest, O Christ, save me.[^9993]

*Sticheron 2:* Estranged have I become from every divine commandment, and for the better virtue I have in every way cared not, and foolishly have I spent all my life in slothfulness; in fornication have I wrought all my deeds, improper and unlawful; therefore, O Christ, have compassion as Thou art tender-hearted, and freely save me.[^9994]

*Sticheron 3:* Rebuke me not in Thine anger, the work of the hands of Thy goodness, who have severed myself by an unreasoning mind, O Thou Who for my sake didst deign to be as I am, for the sake of the ineffable abyss of compassion, O Lover of mankind; but through the prayers of the Ever-Virgin, Thy Mother, O Word, vouchsafe me divine conversion as God.[^9995]

*Other Stichera, to the Holy Forerunner John.*[^9996]
*Tone 8, Podoben: "O most marvelous wonder" (Подо́бенъ то́йже):*[^9997]

*Sticheron 1:* O blessed Forerunner John, make my humble soul ever to grow with love toward the Lord by thy sacred intercessions; quench the fire of my passions, leading me unto the fulfillment of divine commandments, and truly cleanse the feelings of my heart, that I may glorify thee.[^9998]

*Sticheron 2:* Offspring of the barren woman, and the garden of purity of the Master, adornment of men, O all-lauded Forerunner John, divine guardian of my humble soul: by thy prayers and help grant good favor, delivering me from the serpent's deceit, and from his malicious barks and assaults.[^9999]

*Sticheron 3:* Thou art wholly sweetest, and filled with divine sweetness, O Ever-Glorious One, and thou makest glad all who with faith come unto thee, sweetening the feelings both of soul and of body, ever delivering us from diseases and afflictions, and evil assaults, and soul-corrupting passions.[^10000]

*Glory: Both now: Theotokion:* With faith do I flee unto thy protection, O pure Mother of God: from tribulations and perils, and the confusion of passions, and from demonic affliction save me, as having an abyss of mercy, and having appeared as mediatress of salvation, O Sovereign Lady who didst bear God the merciful, the only all-compassionate and plenteous in mercy.[^10001]

*O Gladsome Light: and the rest.*[^10002]
*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 8.*[^10003]

*Sticheron 1:* Thee, King and Master, the Angels unceasingly hymn; but I...[^10004]

...fall down before Thee, crying as the publican: O God, cleanse me and have mercy on me.[^10005]

*Stichos: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven... So look our eyes unto the Lord our God, until He have mercy on us.*[^10006]

*Sticheron 2:* Being immortal, O my soul, be not engulfed by the waves of this life; but rise up, crying aloud unto thy Benefactor: O God, cleanse me and save me.[^10007]

*Stichos: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly filled with contempt; our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of the prosperous, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^10008]

*Martyrion:* O Martyrs of the Lord, pray unto our God, and entreat for our souls a multitude of compassions, and cleansing of many transgressions, we beseech you.[^10009]

*Glory: Both now: Theotokion:* Rejoice, praise of the universe! Rejoice, temple of the Lord! Rejoice, overshadowing mountain! Rejoice, refuge of all! Rejoice, golden candlestick! Rejoice, honorable glory of the Orthodox! Rejoice, paradise! Rejoice, divine table! Rejoice, tabernacle! Rejoice, all-golden jar! Rejoice, hope of all![^10010]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion Prayers, and the rest as usual.*[^10011]

*On Monday at Compline.*[^10012]
*Supplicatory Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^10013]
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^10014] Having passed through the water as on dry land, and having escaped the Egyptian evil, the Israelite cried aloud: Let us sing unto our Deliverer and God.[^10015]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^10016]

*Troparion 1:* Knowing thee, O Mother of God, to be a prayerful fountain of waters, pouring forth unto the faithful streams for souls and bodies, therefore do we unceasingly glorify thee.[^10017]

*Troparion 2:* Beyond nature didst thou bear the divine and originating Word, O Virgin, and hast delivered our nature from corruption; therefore free me from irrational and unnatural passions.[^10018]

*Troparion 3:* The pastures of carnal passions now surround me, and grievously afflict me; visit speedily thy servant, O Virgin, and save me from those who oppress me.[^10019]

*Both now: Theotokion:* He that upholds all creation, and bears and saves it, appeared circumscribed upon thine arm, O Pure One, and renewed the human race.[^10020]

*Ode 3. Troparion 1:* Lull the confusion of the cruel sickness that now holds me fast, O most pure Sovereign Lady, and free me from transgressions and passions.[^10021]

*Troparion 2:* An ineffable abyss of thy compassions, O Most Pure One: whereby deliver me also from transgressions and diseases.[^10022]

*Glory:* Make haste and visit me that am diseased, O Most Pure One, and deliver me from grievous sicknesses and all manner of sorrow.[^10023]

*Both now:* Magnify the mercy and protection of thy prayers upon me, O Most Pure One, and deliver me from perils and afflictions.[^10024]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I have understood Thy works, and I have glorified Thy Godhead.[^10025]

*Troparion 1:* Show forth unto my heart ever, O Most Pure One, a place of cleansing: the remembrance of the saints, and the cleansing of transgressions.[^10026]

*Troparion 2:* Grant me salvation of soul and body, O All-Immaculate One; and unto him that is sick grant healing, and deliverance from cruel woes.[^10027]

*Glory:* Abolish the wiles of the subtle demons, O Most Pure One, and the uprisings of passions; and grant power unto us that are weak.[^10028]

*Both now:* From thy womb, O Most Pure One, the Sun of righteousness hath shone forth and enlightened the world; with Whose divine rays enlighten me also.[^10029]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Why hast Thou cast me off from Thy presence, O Never-Setting Light, and alien darkness hath covered me, the wretched one? But convert me, and direct my paths unto the light of Thy commandments, I pray.[^10030]

*Troparion 1:* By thy childbearing were the bonds of death dissolved, and the dominion of corruption was bound, O most holy and all-hymned Virgin; therefore quickly loose also the chains of my transgressions, and my heavy sorrows.[^10031]

*Troparion 2:* Deliver me, O Sovereign Lady, from the subtleties of demons and the malice of men; and quickly heal my soul and bodily disease, O thou who alone didst bear the Physician of all flesh and spirits, the Savior and Lord.[^10032]

*Glory:* As the tongs of the divine Coal, O Most Pure One, I pray thee: burn up every passionate lust of thy servant, and cruel infirmities hardly to be borne, and dry up the torrents of sorrows.[^10033]

*Both now:* Thy Son hath granted thee as salvation unto all, O all-pure Sovereign Lady, who have believed in Him, and who confess Him as God manifestly incarnate of thee; therefore save me from various perils and afflictions.[^10034]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine iniquities; and lead me up from the depth of evils, I pray; for unto Thee have I cried, and hear me, O God of my salvation.[^10035]

*Troparion 1:* Look upon me with thy merciful eye, O Sovereign Lady, and deliver me speedily from cruel diseases, and from all expected affliction now, and from snares and falling.[^10036]

*Troparion 2:* From a foolish and passionate disposition, and from the envy of improper men, and from all malice and worldly affliction, deliver me, O most pure Sovereign Lady, by thy prayers.[^10037]

*Glory:* He that created us was created in thee, O Virgin, delivering human nature from corruption; and deliver me, O Pure One, from the temptations that hold me fast, by thy prayers.[^10038]

*Both now:* Thou wast shown forth as a most pure temple of the uncontainable God, O most pure Sovereign Lady: make me also a temple of His grace by thy prayers, and keep me unharmed.[^10039]

*Then, Lord, have mercy, 40 times. Glory: Both now:*[^10040]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 8:* As a Virgin and alone among women, who without seed hast borne God in the flesh, all we generations of men call thee blessed; for the fire of the Godhead dwelt in thee, and as an infant thou dost suckle the Creator and Lord. Therefore, both the angelic and the human race, we worthily glorify thy most holy Childbearing, and with one accord cry unto thee: pray unto Christ God to grant remission of sins unto those who worthily sing of thy glory.[^10041]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The Children that went down from Judea in Babylon of old, by faith in the Trinity trampled upon the furnace flame, singing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^10042]

*Troparion 1:* Heavy sicknesses of recompense have I found, and held fast by many a disease I am in pain; but I pray thee, O Theotokos: help me, and speedily grant health by thy prayers.[^10043]

*Troparion 2:*  
O blessed Virgin, haven of all the faithful in sorrow: deliver me from every temptation and grief and the malice of those that envy me, and set me free from sins and manifold diseases.[^10044]

*Glory:*  
O golden jar,[^10045] from which life-creating myrrh hath flowed forth unto the faithful: take away the disease of my soul and body, and the filth of transgressions, through thine intercessions, O pure Parent of God.

*Both now:*  
Unceasingly glorifying the Word, co-beginningless with the Father and the Spirit, Who was ineffably born of thee, O Mother of God, in praise we sing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^10046]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*  
Sevenfold did the Chaldean tormentor furiously kindle the furnace for the godly;[^10047] yet seeing them saved by a greater power, he cried unto the Creator and Deliverer: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.

*Troparion 1:*  
All we who faithfully flee unto thy protection proclaim the majesty of thy manifold wonders, O Sovereign Lady; but change me now from cruel disease and sicknesses both of soul and body, and show me forth healthy in both respects, to glorify Thy Son Christ unto the ages.[^10048]

*Troparion 2:*  
Violently have the winds of them that envy blown, and rivers of malicious men have vehemently beaten against my spiritual tabernacle, O pure Virgin;[^10049] but even as of a wildly raging sea, do thou destroy the surge of all sorrows, and grant me also calm by thy prayers unto all ages.

*Glory:*  
He that formed the earthly body of man, and then placed in him an immortal soul,[^10050] is being fashioned in thee, O Virgin, and renews him again; Whom do thou show forth unto me as favorable and easily appeased through thy prayers, and grant strength unto all that sorrow, and speedy deliverance.

*Both now:*  
With light dost thou ever illuminate them that glorify thee, O Most Pure Lady, for from thy womb the unwaning Light shone forth; and from the fear of the night, and dark and demonic howling,[^10051] thou deliverest thy servants; from which snatch me also away, and save me from the evils that encompass me, through thy prayers.

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*  
In truth we confess thee to be the Theotokos, who through thee are saved, O pure Virgin, with the bodiless choirs magnifying thee.[^10052]

*Troparion 1:*  
Exalt the horn of the Church, O Most Pure Lady; and grant strength unto the faithful against the heathens, and establish the scepters of the kingdom.[^10053]

*Troparion 2:*  
Cast down the proud, and conquer the regiments of aliens that desire wars, O pure Virgin; and deliver thy servants unharmed from them, O Maiden.

*Glory:*  
O sole defender of Orthodox Christians that glorify thee: through thy prayers speedily put to shame the weapon-bearing Hagarenes.[^10054]

*Both now:*  
Make wise the choirs of the faithful, O Virgin, strengthening them against enemies invisible and visible; and save them from all sorrow.

*Then: It is truly meet: and the rest as usual, and the Dismissal.*[^10055]

*On Tuesday morning.*  
*After the first stichology, penitential sessional hymns. Tone 8:*[^10056]  
With a compassionate eye, O Lord, behold my lowliness, for shortly my life draws to an end, and from works there is no salvation for me; therefore I pray: with a compassionate eye behold my lowliness, and save me.[^10057]

*Stichos: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, neither chasten me in Thy wrath.*[^10058]

Take care, O my soul, as the Judge is come, and consider the hour of the fearful day! For judgment is without mercy to them that have showed no mercy.[^10059] Therefore, before the end cry aloud: Spare me, O Savior, for Thou alone art without sin.

*Glory: both now: Theotokion:*  
As the immovable foundation of the faith and the precious gift of our souls, we the faithful magnify the Theotokos in hymns: Rejoice, thou who containedst the Rock of life in thy womb! Rejoice, hope of the ends of the earth, protection of those in sorrow! Rejoice, Bride unwedded![^10060]

*After the second stichology, penitential sessional hymns. Tone 8:*[^10061]  
Like the harlot I fall down before Thee, that I may receive forgiveness; and instead of myrrh, I offer tears from the heart unto Thee: that even as her Thou mayest have compassion on me, O Savior, and grant me cleansing of sins; for like her I cry unto Thee:

Deliver me from the mire of my deeds.[^10062]

*Stichos: O Lord, rebuke me not in Thine anger, neither chasten me in Thy wrath.*

My lifespan is coming to an end, life passes away, and Thy fearful throne is prepared, O Savior: judgment awaits me, threatening me with fiery torment and unquenchable flame; grant me a cloud of tears and extinguish its power, O Thou Who desirest all men to be saved.[^10063]

*Stichos: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^10064]

*Martyrion:*  
Ye appeared as spiritual luminaries, O holy martyrs: for by faith ye abolished the gloom of delusion, ye enlightened the lamps of your souls, and with the Bridegroom entered with glory into the heavenly bridal chamber; and now pray ye, we beseech you, that our souls may be saved.[^10065]

*Glory: both now: Theotokion:*  
O Most Pure Virgin, Mother of Christ, heal the cruel passions of my soul, I pray thee; and grant forgiveness of my transgressions, which I have senselessly committed: having defiled my soul, and sullied my body, wretch that I am; woe is me! What shall I do in that hour when the Angels shall separate my soul from my wretched body? Then be thou my helper and protectress, for I, thy servant, have placed my hope in thee.[^10066]

*After the third stichology, sessional hymns. Tone 8, Podoben: "What was commanded mystically" (Повелѣ́нное та́йнѡ):*[^10067]  
Taking into my mind the day and hour of Thy fearful, awesome, and unbribable judgment seat, O Master Christ, I tremble like an evildoer; for I have shameful deeds and cruel actions, which I alone have diligently wrought. Therefore with fear I fall down before Thee, and cry aloud with pain: Through the prayers of Thy Forerunner, O Many-Merciful One, save me.[^10068]

*Another Sessional Hymn:*  
O my soul, languishing through life carelessly, arise at last, look toward repentance; weep bitterly from the depth of thy heart, lest by any means thou mourn there without avail; shudder as thou meditatest upon the Master's second coming, and before the judgment condemn thyself: and thou shalt escape that righteous judgment.[^10069]

*Glory: both now: Theotokion:*  
Being entreated, O Christ, through the prayers of Thy Bodiless Hosts, and of Thy Forerunner, and of the Disciples, and Prophets, and Martyrs, and of all the Saints, and of the Theotokos, Thine unwedded and good Mother: grant us to walk in Thy light; and vouchsafe us to obtain Thy Kingdom, for the tender mercy of Thy loving-kindness.[^10070]

*Penitential Canon to our Lord Jesus Christ, and to His holy Martyrs. The work of Lord Joseph.*  
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^10071]  
Unto Him Who crushes battles by His arm, and led Israel through the Red Sea, let us sing, as unto our Deliverer and God, for He hath been glorified.[^10072]

*Refrain: Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee.*

*Troparion 1:*  
Fill my heart with compunction, O Christ, that through repentance I may enter into Thy tabernacles, and with confession pray unto Thee Who loosest me from debts.[^10073]

*Troparion 2:*  
Loose me, O Word, from the bonds of my countless evils, that in repentance I may tread Thy righteous paths, which guide unto the divine resting-place of eternal beauties.

*Martyrion 1:*  
The great magnificence of Thy martyrs, O Christ, was lifted up on high; for having suffered all-gloriously, they were magnified with Thy supreme graces.[^10074]

*Martyrion 2:*  
By the sprinkling of the divine blood of the holy sufferers, the sacrificial blood offered to the enemy ceased, and earthly things were sanctified by the grace of the Spirit.

*Theotokion:*  
Show unto me paths of repentance, O Virgin, and turn back the ways that lead me unto sin, that I may hymn thee, the much-praised Parent of God.

*Another Canon, to the holy Forerunner John. The work of Joseph.*  
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^10075]  
Unto Him Who shook off the tyranny of Pharaoh in the sea, and guided Israel dryshod, let us sing unto Christ, for He hath been glorified unto the ages.[^10076]

*Refrain: Holy and great John, Forerunner of the Lord, pray to God for us.*

*Troparion 1:*  
O Forerunner, preacher of repentance, pray unto the Savior and Lord that I may repent from my whole soul, enlightening the mind and heart of me who honor thee with love.[^10077]

*Troparion 2:*  
As the most fair desert lamb, O Forerunner, martyr of Christ, guide me who now dwell in the desert of passions unto the life of repentance, by thy divine prayer.

*Troparion 3:*  
Speedily free me from tormenting sin through thine intercession, O wise Forerunner, I pray thee, and dispel the demonic storm raised against me.

*Theotokion:*  
Save me, O Mother of Truth, who am cruelly storm-tossed by passions and often submerged; and direct me, O Pure Lady, unto the calm haven of salvation.[^10078]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*  
My heart is established in the Lord, my horn is exalted in my God;

my mouth is enlarged over mine enemies, I have rejoiced in Thy salvation.[^10079]

*Troparion 1:*  
Having washed away the malicious defilements of my heart, vouchsafe me to appear blameless before Thee in the fearful day, O my Christ, as Thou art all-good.[^10080]

*Troparion 2:*  
The apostate through craftiness hath prevailed to put me to death with the sting of sin, O Word; yet do Thou Thyself heal me, O Christ, with the life-bearing herb of repentance.

*Martyrion 1:*  
Let us stand manfully, the passion-bearers cried one to another, lest anyone cast away the warfare! The Master stands before us as helper, while we suffer with valiant mind.[^10081]

*Martyrion 2:*  
As the most honorable stones of the Church, the divine pillars of piety, the conquerors of delusion, let all us faithful honor the martyrs of the Lord in songs.

*Theotokion:*  
O Theotokos, restoration of the fallen, raise me up who have fallen into the pit of mine evils; and establish me, O Sovereign Lady, upon the rock of the commandments of God.[^10082]

*Canon of the Forerunner. Ode 3. Heirmos:*  
Establish our mind and hearts, O Thou Who by Thy word didst establish the heavens, that we may hymn and glorify Thee, unto the salvation of our souls.[^10083]

*Troparion 1:*  
Rain down upon me also drops of repentance, O thou who in the river didst baptize the abyss of compassion, the Lord of all, O Forerunner, martyr worthy of glory.[^10084]

*Troparion 2:*  
Being ever tossed in mind by the tempestuous waves of life, I flee beneath thy protection, O Forerunner of the Savior; make haste to help me, thy servant.

*Troparion 3:*  
In nightly prayers I invoke thee, the daily luminary unto the world, O divinely-blessed Forerunner; enlighten the senses of my heart.[^10085]

*Theotokion:*  
Save me, O Mother of Truth, who am cruelly storm-tossed by passions and often submerged; and direct me, O Pure Lady, unto the calm haven of salvation.

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*  
I have heard, O Lord, the report of Thee and was afraid, that being ever-existing God, by ineffable counsel Thou camest forth from the Virgin, having become incarnate. Glory to Thy condescension, O Christ; glory to Thy power.[^10086]

*Troparion 1:*  
The deceitful enemy deceived me by the tasting of sin, and removed me far from Thee, O Good One, and made me food for his wild beasts;

O only Savior, make haste to deliver me.[^10087]

*Troparion 2:*  
The unmanifest and hidden things of mine Thou Thyself knowest, O Lord; on me who have sinned greatly against Thee have compassion according to Thy many mercies, O Word of God, as Thou art merciful, and grant me cleansing time for repentance.

*Martyrion 1:*  
Though overwhelmed by tortures as by waves, O Martyrs, by the pilotage of Christ ye were guided unto the havens of the heavenly kingdom: being adorned in truth by Him with victorious crowns.[^10088]

*Martyrion 2:*  
Having renewed the soil of the heart by piety, O Martyrs, ye cast upon it the seed of confession: and manifestly by grace ye reaped the good hundredfold ear of suffering.[^10089]

*Theotokion:*  
O All-Praised Sovereign Lady, with a loud voice we hymn thee: fill my mind with every joy, granting me good weeping, and occasions of repentance, and understanding of salvation.

*Canon of the Forerunner. Ode 4. Heirmos:*  
I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I understood Thy works, and glorified Thy Godhead.[^10090]

*Troparion 1:*  
I weep for myself, ever possessing an uncorrected life: save me and have compassion on me, O Forerunner, who am perishing by sins.[^10091]

*Troparion 2:*  
In prayers and supplications may I find thee as a helper, O Blessed One, strengthening my soul, and illuminating my thought.

*Troparion 3:*  
Guide me who am engulfed by the tempest of transgressions and in peril, O Baptist of Christ, unto the haven of divine understanding.

*Theotokion:*  
Having humbled me who live high-mindedly, save me, O Most Pure Lady, who barest Him Who exalted our humbled nature.[^10092]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*  
Dispel the darkness of my soul, O Christ God, Giver of light, having driven out the primal darkness of the abyss; and grant me the light of Thy commandments, O Word, that rising early I may glorify Thee.[^10093]

*Troparion 1:*  
O Thou Who by Thy command didst cleanse the harlot and the lepers, O Creator of all: cleanse my humble soul from filthy sin, and adorn it beautifully with shining garments, O Master, I pray Thee.[^10094]

*Troparion 2:*  
O Christ God, having loosed me from the bonds of my many transgressions, direct me to walk unhindered in Thy paths: that being freed from the flesh and having settled in holy resting-places, I may glorify Thee.[^10095]

*Martyrion 1:*  
Having woven for themselves a robe of glory out of pains, O Martyrs, and having put it on beautifully, they dwell in joy in the kingdom on high, being adorned with fair crowns of victory.[^10096]

*Martyrion 2:*  
With wise mind the Martyrs exchanged transient things for those that abide; for having been encompassed by the straits of manifold tortures, ye attained unto the true breadth of the heavenly kingdom, O wise ones, rejoicing.[^10097]

*Theotokion:*  
O Holy Theotokos, who barest in the flesh the Holy Word, Who alone rests in all the Saints: sanctify my mind which ever abides in evil deeds.

*Canon of the Forerunner. Ode 5. Heirmos:*  
From the night of ignorance, may the day of the knowledge of God shine forth early in the light of Thy face, O Christ, Thy praise in our hearts.[^10098]

*Troparion 1:*  
Enlighten me who have gone astray in the night of life, O glorious Forerunner, being the lamp of the Sun of righteousness.[^10099]

*Troparion 2:*  
In the future judgment, when I am to stand before the Lord, may I find thee as a defender, O Forerunner, delivering me from fearful condemnation.

*Troparion 3:*  
Establish me who am assaulted and shaken by demonic temptations upon the rock of the divine wills, O ever-praised one.[^10100]

*Theotokion:*  
As thou art higher than all creatures, O Mother of God, Ever-Virgin, show me forth higher than the snares of the enemy.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*  
Even as Thou didst deliver the prophet from the lowest depth, O Christ God, so also deliver me from my sins as Thou art the Lover of mankind; and direct my life, I pray Thee.[^10101]

*Troparion 1:*  
Receive me repenting, even as once Thou receivedst the Ninevites who believed the divine preaching of Thy prophet, O Christ; and steer my life, I pray unto Thee.[^10102]

*Troparion 2:*  
Like the publican I sigh, like the harlot I shed tears, like Peter I cry aloud, being submerged by many transgressions, O Christ: give me a hand of help, and save me.[^10103]

*Martyrion 1:*  
As divinely radiant luminaries, O Martyrs, by the rays of suffering ye ever illuminate the earthly world, and drive away the deep darkness of delusion.[^10104]

*Martyrion 2:*  
Having found a most blessed end, O all-holy Martyrs, ye ever honor the blessed God, being delighted by His brightness.[^10105]

*Theotokion:*  
The jar that received the manna once prefigured thee, O Theotokos; for thou barest Christ, Who rained down the manna of understanding unto all that honor thee.[^10106]

*Canon of the Forerunner. Ode 6. Heirmos:*  
Grant me a radiant robe, O Thou Who deckest Thyself with light as with a garment, O Many-Merciful Christ our God.[^10107]

*Troparion 1:*  
With the axe of true repentance, wholly cleanse my heart, O all-wise John, making it fruitful with virtues.[^10108]

*Troparion 2:*  
Held fast by the sea-monster of temptations, from the depth of my heart I cry unto thee, O Forerunner: free me from cruel afflictions.[^10109]

*Troparion 3:*  
O my soul, repent fervently! Behold, the tribunal hath arrived! Look up, crying: O Jesus my God, for the sake of Thy Forerunner do Thou have compassion on me.

*Theotokion:*  
O Pure Lady, who alone by a word barest the Word in the flesh, we pray: deliver our souls from the snares of the enemy.[^10110]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*  
O Thou Who by an Angel didst save the children from the fire, and turnedst the roaring furnace into dew: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^10111]

*Troparion 1:*  
As I was conceived and born in iniquities, O Compassionate One, I have sinned more than all men; grant me time of conversion that justifies me.[^10112]

*Troparion 2:*  
I exalted myself senselessly, even as the Pharisee of old, and with a cruel fall am I fallen; and the enemy sees me and rejoices. O Word of God, despise me not.[^10113]

*Martyrion 1:*  
The assembly of honorable martyrs, the unconquerable host, the holy legion that acted manfully on earth, hath been inscribed in the city in the heavens.[^10114]

*Martyrion 2:*  
Having willingly passed through suffering, the mediatress of immortality, O Martyrs, ye pour forth a stream of healings, which drives away the passions of men.[^10115]

*Theotokion:*  
Beyond cause dost thou bear the Cause of all, Who through the abundance of goodness became man; therefore in unison we call thee blessed, O Pure Lady.[^10116]

*Canon of the Forerunner. Ode 7. Heirmos:*  
O Thou Who in the beginning didst establish the earth, and by Thy word didst establish the heavens: Blessed art Thou unto the ages, O Lord God of our fathers.[^10117]

*Troparion 1:*  
O thou who didst prepare the ways of the Lord, O Forerunner, direct my paths also thereto, that I may cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^10118]

*Troparion 2:*  
O herald of the Light, enlighten my soul, and deliver me from cruel darkness and burning Gehenna, who flee unto thee with an undoubting soul.

*Troparion 3:*  
Being the fruitful offspring of a barren woman, transform the barrenness of my heart into fruitfulness through thy prayers, O wise Baptist of Christ.[^10119]

*Theotokion:*  
O chosen Ewe-Lamb of God the Word, pray unto God Who was incarnate of thee, to number me with the chosen sheep in the fearful hour.[^10120]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*  
O Thou Who coverest Thy high chambers with waters, Who settest the sand as a bound to the sea, and upholdest all things: Thee the sun hymns, Thee the moon glorifies, unto Thee all creation offers a hymn, as unto the Creator of all unto the ages.[^10121]

*Troparion 1:*  
With all manner of evildoings hast thou defiled thy hands, O my soul, and how dost thou lift them up on high, conversing with God? Thy feet hast thou made unprofitable, walking unto shameful deeds. Make haste to walk through repentance in saving paths.[^10122]

*Troparion 2:*  
I have never abided in Thy commandments, O Lord, nor have I wrought Thy will for a single day, O Good One. With what eyes then shall I gaze upon Thee then, when Thou executest righteous judgment and dispatchest the guilty into the fire of Gehenna?[^10123]

*Martyrion 1:*  
Ye extinguished the kindled furnaces of polytheism, O wise Sufferers, pouring forth your blood as abundant water, unrighteously shed for the love of the Creator; therefore ye inherit the torrent of delight.[^10124]

*Martyrion 2:*  
Ye transcended the earthly body of lowliness, O Saints, and endured tortures—even the severing of hands and feet—as though another were suffering; therefore ye have now been vouchsafed the highest life unto the ages.[^10125]

*Theotokion:*  
Unto me who am tossed by the storm of sin, I cry unto thee, O pure Sovereign Lady: by thine intercession direct me unto saving repentance and unto the all-calm haven: that I may behold the light of salvation, being ever darkened by slothfulness.[^10126]

*Canon of the Forerunner. Ode 8. Heirmos:*  
The beginningless King of glory, before Whom the heavenly hosts tremble: praise Him, ye priests, ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^10127]

*Troparion 1:*  
Pray unto the Lamb of God Who takes away the sins of the world, Whom thou didst show forth unto all, O Baptist, to put to death my passions and to save my soul.[^10128]

*Troparion 2:*  
Lighten the burden of my soul, overcome them that war against me, O Baptist of the Lord, and show me forth unwounded by their evildoing.

*Troparion 3:*  
Fleeing, thou didst remove far off, O Prophet, and didst make thy dwelling in the impassable desert; therefore I pray thee: make empty the passions of my soul right speedily.[^10129]

*Theotokion:*  
Thou art the Vine, O Virgin, who barest the ripe Cluster: watering me now with the drink of compunction, take away the drunkenness of mine evils.[^10130]

*Then: My soul doth magnify the Lord:*  
*Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^10131]  
Blessed be the Lord God of Israel, Who hath raised up a horn of salvation for us in the house of David His servant; wherein the Dayspring from on high hath visited us, and directed us into the way of peace.[^10132]

*Troparion 1:*  
That I also being saved may thankfully magnify Thee, O Christ, look upon me who have received many wounds upon my shoulders, and heal them, pouring upon them the wine and oil of the knowledge of Thy compassion, O Savior.[^10133]

*Troparion 2:*  
Even as Thou didst deliver the good-natured thief who uttered his voice from murder and from myriad evils, as Thou hadst compassion on the weeping harlot, on Peter the great disciple, and on David the prophet: have mercy also on me who am in despair, O Savior.[^10134]

*Martyrion 1:*  
Having become conformed to the sufferings of Him Who suffered for our sake, O passion-bearers, together with Him are ye now glorified, being deified by divine partakings, and shining brighter than the rays of the sensible sun, and illuminating the hearts of the faithful.[^10135]

*Martyrion 2:*  
The virtue of the holy sufferers hath shone forth; every city is enriched through faith with these truly unstolen treasures, which abundantly flash forth the grace of all-glorious wonders; whom we hymn in song as fervent protectors.[^10136]

*Theotokion:*  
With the illuminating lightnings of the Son of God Who came forth from thy womb, O All-Immaculate, enlighten those who sing of thee in faith; and from unenlightened darkness and eternal torment deliver us by thine intercession.[^10137]

*Canon of the Forerunner. Ode 9. Heirmos:*  
The Nativity of the Ever-Virgin, prefigured on the mountain unto the lawgiver in the fire and the bush, for the salvation of us the faithful, in unceasing hymns we magnify.[^10138]

*Troparion 1:*  
When I am about to stand before Thy fearful throne, O Word, and the cause of my deeds is examined: what answer shall I turn to, wretch that I am? For the sake of Thy Baptist, O Lord my God, then spare Thou me.[^10139]

*Troparion 2:*  
As the voice of the Word, direct my cries unto God, O all-wise Baptist, and deliver me from demonic malice and the temptation of men, that according to duty I may call thee blessed.[^10140]

*Troparion 3:*  
Nourish me with the immortal food of Christ's commandments; give me to drink of the water of life, O Baptist and Prophet; and present me saved unto God, who flee beneath thy protection.[^10141]

*Theotokion:*  
O Sovereign Lady, pure and most-glorified Virgin, with the Forerunner pray unto Thy Son and King, to save from every necessity those who bless thee in faith.[^10142]

*Then: It is truly meet: litany, Exapostilarion, and the usual psalms.*[^10143]

*At the Aposticha, penitential stichera. Tone 8:*  
When I take into mind the multitude of evils wrought by me, and come into the thought of that fearful reckoning: held by trembling, I flee unto Thee, the Lover of mankind, my God. Therefore despise me not, I pray Thee, O only Sinless One: grant compunction to my humble soul before the end, and save me.[^10144]

*Stichos: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord: and we rejoiced and were glad. In all our days, let us be glad for the days wherein Thou didst humble us, for the years wherein we saw evils. And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thine handiwork, and guide their sons.*[^10145]

Grant me tears, O God, even as once unto the sinful woman, and vouchsafe me to wet Thy feet, which have set me free from the path of delusion, and to offer unto Thee a pure life as fragrant myrrh, formed in me by repentance: that I also may hear Thy desired voice: Thy faith hath saved thee; go in peace.[^10146]

*Stichos: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us; and direct Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands direct Thou.*[^10147]

*Martyrion:*  
Greatly did ye contend, O Holy Ones, having valiantly endured torments from transgressors, and confessed Christ before kings; and having departed from this life, ye still work miracles in the world, and heal the diseased by your sufferings, O Holy Ones: pray ye that our souls may be saved.[^10148]

*Glory: both now: Theotokion:*  
Thy shelter, O Virgin Theotokos, is a spiritual infirmary; for fleeing thereto, we are delivered from diseases of the soul.[^10149]

*It is good to give praise: Trisagion, and the rest as usual.*[^10150]

*On Tuesday, the Beatitudes. Tone 8:*  
Remember us, O Christ, Savior of the world, even as Thou rememberedst the thief upon the Tree; and vouchsafe all, O only Compassionate One, Thy heavenly kingdom.[^10151]

*Troparion 1:*  
As Thou hast an abyss of compassion, O Christ, dry up the abyss of my transgressions; and through tears of compunction transform my petrified soul.[^10152]

*Troparion 2:*  
Having of old baptized Christ in water, O divine Baptist: direct me, who am storm-tossed by the surging waves of passions, unto the haven of repentance by thy supplications.[^10153]

*Martyrion:*  
Having quenched the fire of idolatry with your blood, O holy Passion-Bearers, ye ever pour forth streams of healings, curing manifold passions.[^10154]

*Glory:*  
O Beginningless Father, and Son, and Divine Spirit, through the prayers of the Baptist, appease the longstanding passions of my soul, I pray, and save me, Thy servant.[^10155]

*Both now:*  
Deliver us from shameful passions and from cruel torment in hades, by thy supplications, O all-pure Theotokos, who piously call thee blessed.[^10156]

## Wednesday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross[^10157]

*On Tuesday evening.*[^10158]  
*At "Lord, I have cried," stichera of the Cross. Tone 8.*  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "Martyrs of the Lord" (Мꙋ́ченици Гдⷭ҇ни):*[^10159]  
Nailed to the Cross, O Christ, pierced in hands and feet, Thou wast wounded in Thy holy side, pouring forth unto me drops of divine salvation, O All-Good One: blood and water, that Thou mightest wash away my corruption and defilement. Glory to Thy goodness, O All-Compassionate One.[^10160]

Thou didst endure suffering, O Master, that Thou mightest grant dispassion unto all that worship Thy passions, and Thy voluntary slaughter, and the lance, and nails, and reed: which Thou didst endure willingly with longsuffering, that Thou mightest also mediate for me dispassion, O Lord, for the sake of Thy passions.[^10161]

The undefiled Heifer, seeing the Young Bull lifted up upon the Tree of His own will, weeping with compunction, cried aloud: Woe is me, most beloved Child! What hath the thankless assembly of the Jews repaid Thee, desiring to make me childless of Thee, O All-Beloved?[^10162]

*Other stichera, to the Most Holy Theotokos. Same Tone.*  
I endure not, Child, to see Thee fallen asleep upon the Tree, Who givest vigilance unto all: that Thou mightest grant divine and saving vigilance unto them that of old fell asleep through the tasting of the fruit in all-destructive sleep: the Virgin said, weeping, whom we magnify.[^10163]

An unjust assembly lifted Thee up upon the Cross, the Lamb Who takes away the transgressions of the world; and with a lance they pierced Thy ribs, O Long-Suffering One, and nailed Thy hands and feet. O cruel malice! Oh, what audacity! The All-Pure One thus cried out, shedding tears of compunction.[^10164]

What is this sight which I now behold, O Master? Thou Who in Thine hand wondrously upholdest all creation: hangest Thou unjustly upon the Tree like a lamb, O Word of God, suspended by disobedient servants? Oh, what patience! Oh, Thy goodness, O All-Compassionate One! weeping, the All-Immaculate said.[^10165]

*Glory: both now: Stavrotheotokion:*  
When the Ewe-Lamb and Virgin beheld Thee, her Lamb, led to the slaughter, she followed after Thee with tears, O Word, and cried aloud: Whither hastenest...[^10166]

...Thou, my Child? I will go with Thee, O sweetest One! for I cannot bear not to see Thee, O my most merciful Jesus.[^10167]

*O gladsome Light: and the rest.*[^10168]

*At the Aposticha, stichera of the Cross. Tone 8:*  
Lifting Thyself up upon the Cross, O Christ God, Thou hast saved mankind. We glorify Thy sufferings.[^10169]

*Stichos: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven: behold, as the eyes of servants look unto the hands of their masters, as the eyes of the maiden look unto the hands of her mistress, so do our eyes look unto the Lord our God, until He have compassion on us.*[^10170]

Thou wast nailed to the Cross, O Christ God, and hast opened the gates of Paradise. We glorify Thy Divinity.[^10171]

*Stichos: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly filled with contempt: our soul is exceedingly filled; let reproach come upon them that prosper, and contempt upon the proud.*[^10172]

*Martyrion:*  
Thy martyrs, O Lord, forgot worldly things, disregarding tortures for the sake of the life to come, and were shown forth to be inheritors thereof; therefore they rejoice together with the angels. Through their prayers grant great mercy unto Thy people.[^10173]

*Glory: both now: Stavrotheotokion:*  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "O Lord, though Thou didst stand before the tribunal" (Гдⷭ҇и, а҆́ще и҆ на сꙋди́щи):*[^10174]  
O Lord, when the sun beheld Thee, the Sun of Righteousness, suspended upon the Tree, it hid its beams, and the moon changed its light into darkness; and Thine all-immaculate Mother was wounded in her maternal heart.[^10175]

*Now lettest Thou depart: Trisagion. After Our Father: troparion, litany, and dismissal.*[^10176]

*On Tuesday at Compline.*  
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*  
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^10177]  
Having traversed the water as dry land, and escaped the Egyptian evil, the Israelite cried aloud: Unto our Deliverer and God let us sing.[^10178]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:*  
Encompassed by many temptations, I flee unto thee, seeking salvation: O Mother of the Word and Virgin, save me from grevious and cruel ills.[^10179]

*Troparion 2:*  
Assaults of passions trouble me, filling my soul with much despondency: bring peace, O Maiden, by the calm of Thy Son and God, O All-Immaculate.[^10180]

*Glory:*  
Having given birth unto the Savior and God, I pray thee, O Virgin, to deliver me from cruel misfortunes; for fleeing now unto thee, I stretch forth both soul and understanding.[^10181]

*Both now:*  
Suffering in body and soul, vouchsafe me divine visitation and provi-...[^10182]

...-dence from thee, O only Mother and Parent of God.[^10183]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*  
Supreme Creator of the heavenly vault, O Lord, and Builder of the Church: do Thou establish me in Thy love, Summit of desires, Firm Support of the faithful, O only Lover of mankind.[^10184]

*Troparion 1:*  
As protection and shelter of my life do I set thee, O Virgin Parent of God: steer me unto thy haven, O Cause of good things, Firm Support of the faithful, O only All-Praised.[^10185]

*Troparion 2:*  
I pray thee, O Virgin, to dispel the turmoil of my soul and the tempest of my grief; for thou, O Bride of God, hast given birth to the Prince of peace, Christ, O only All-Pure.[^10186]

*Glory:*  
Having borne the Benefactor, the Cause of good things, pour forth the wealth of beneficence unto all; for thou canst do all things, as having borne Christ, Who is mighty in strength, O divinely-blessed Lady.[^10187]

*Both now:*  
When I am tried by cruel diseases and painful passions, O Virgin, do thou help me; for I know thee to be an inexhaustible, unfailing treasury of healings, O All-Immaculate.[^10188]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*  
I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I understood Thy works, and glorified Thy Godhead.[^10189]

*Troparion 1:*  
Thou who didst bear the Pilot, the Lord: calm the tempest of my passions and the storm of my transgressions, O Bride of God.[^10190]

*Troparion 2:*  
Grant the abyss of thy compassions unto me who invoke them, O thou that barest the Compassionate One, and the Savior of all that hymn Thee.[^10191]

*Glory:*  
Delighting in thy gifts, O All-Pure, we chant a song of thanksgiving, knowing thee to be the Mother of God.[^10192]

*Both now:*  
As I lie upon the bed of my sickness and infirmity, as thou art a lover of goodness, help me, O Theotokos, only Ever-Virgin.[^10193]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*  
Enlighten us by Thy commandments, O Lord, and by Thine arm on high grant us Thy peace, O only Lover of mankind.[^10194]

*Troparion 1:*  
Fill my heart with gladness, O Pure One, granting thine incorruptible joy, who didst bear the Cause of gladness.[^10195]

*Troparion 2:*  
Deliver us from dangers, O pure Theotokos, who didst bear eternal redemp-...[^10196]

...-tion, and the peace that surpasses all understanding.[^10197]

*Glory:*  
Dispel the gloom of my transgressions, O Bride of God, by the illumination of thy brightness, who didst bear the divine and eternal Light.[^10198]

*Both now:*  
Heal the infirmity of my soul, O Pure One, having vouchsafed me thy visitation, and grant me health by thy prayers.[^10199]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*  
I will pour out my prayer unto the Lord, and unto Him will I declare my griefs; for my soul is filled with evils, and my life hath drawn near unto hades; and like Jonah I pray: Lead me up from corruption, O God.[^10200]

*Troparion 1:*  
As He hath saved my nature, held by corruption and death, from death and corruption by giving Himself up to death: O Virgin, pray unto Thy Lord and Son to deliver me from the evildoing of enemies.[^10201]

*Troparion 2:*  
I know thee as the protectress of my life and a steadfast guardian, O Virgin, who dissolvest the turmoils of temptations and drivest away the assaults of demons; and I pray thee ever to deliver me from the corruption of my passions.[^10202]

*Glory:*  
We have acquired thee as a wall of refuge, and the all-perfect salvation of our souls, and relief in afflictions, O Maiden! and in thine illumination we ever rejoice, O Sovereign Lady: and now do thou save us from passions and perils.[^10203]

*Both now:*  
Now I lie infirm upon a bed, and there is no healing for my flesh; but having borne God and the Savior of the world, and the Deliverer from diseases, I pray unto thee who art good: raise me up from the corruption of diseases.[^10204]

*Then: Lord, have mercy (3 times). Glory: both now:*  
*Sessional Hymn. Tone 8:*  
Beholding the Lamb, and Shepherd, and Savior of the world upon the Cross, she that bare Thee said, shedding tears: The world indeed rejoices, receiving redemption; but my maternal heart burns, beholding Thy crucifixion, which Thou endurest for the sake of all, O my Son and my God.[^10205]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*  
The Children that went down from Judea in Babylon of old, by faith in the Trinity trampled upon the furnace flame, singing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^10206]

*Troparion 1:*  
As Thou wast pleased, O Savior, to order our salvation, Thou didst dwell in the Virgin's womb, whom Thou showedst forth to the world as protectress: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^10207]

*Troparion 2:*  
Implore the Desirer of mercy, Whom thou didst bear, O pure Mother, that they may be delivered from transgressions and spiritual defilements who cry in faith: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^10208]

*Glory:*  
A treasury of salvation, and fountain of incorruption, and pillar of strength, and door of repentance hast Thou revealed to them that cry unto her that bare Thee: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^10209]

*Both now:*  
Vouchsafe to heal bodily weakness and diseases of soul, O Parent of God, of them that approach thy protection with love, O Virgin, who didst bear for us Christ the Savior.[^10210]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*  
The King of heaven, Whom the angelic hosts hymn: praise and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^10211]

*Troparion 1:*  
Despise not those who need help from thee, O Virgin, who sing and supremely exalt thee unto the ages.[^10212]

*Troparion 2:*  
Thou healest the weakness of my soul and the diseases of my body, O Virgin, that I may glorify thee, O Pure One, unto the ages.[^10213]

*Glory:*  
Thou pourest out wealth of healings unto them that faithfully hymn thee, O Virgin, and supremely exalt thine ineffable childbearing.[^10214]

*Both now:*  
Thou drivest away the assaults of temptations and the onslaughts of passions, O Virgin: therefore we sing of thee unto all ages.[^10215]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*  
Truly confessing thee to be the Theotokos, who through thee are saved, O pure Virgin, with the bodiless choirs we magnify thee.[^10216]

*Troparion 1:*  
Turn not away from the stream of my tears, O Virgin who didst bear Christ, Who wipes away every tear from every face.[^10217]

*Troparion 2:*  
Fill my heart with joy, O Virgin, who didst receive the fulness of joy, consuming sinful sorrow.[^10218]

*Glory:*  
Enlighten with the rays of thy light, O Virgin, driving away the darkness of ignorance, those who piously confess thee to be the Theotokos.[^10219]

*Both now:*  
In the place of affliction and weakness, heal him that is brought low, O Virgin, transforming illness into health.[^10220]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion. After Our Father: troparion and the rest as usual.*[^10221]

*On Wednesday morning.*  
*After the first stichology, sessional hymns of the Cross. Tone 8:*[^10222]  
Seeing the Prince of life hanging upon the Cross, the thief said: If He were not God incarnate Who is crucified with us, the sun would not have hidden its beams, nor would the earth have quaked, trembling; but Thou Who endurest all things, remember me, O Lord, in Thy kingdom.[^10223]

*Stichos: Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship the footstool of His feet, for He is holy.*[^10224]

In the midst between two thieves, Thy Cross was found as a righteous balance: bringing down the one into hades by the weight of blasphemy, but relieving the other of transgressions unto the knowledge of theology. O Christ God, glory to Thee.[^10225]

*Glory: both now: Stavrotheotokion:*  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "Guiding unto wisdom" (Премꙋ́дрости):*[^10226]  
When thou didst see Him Who was incarnate of thee lifted up upon the Cross between two thieves, O Virgin, taking up lamentation, thou didst cry aloud: Woe is me, sweetest Child! How art Thou voluntarily crucified, Who takest away the sins of the world as Thou art tender-hearted? desiring as God that songs of praise be offered unto Thee by men.[^10227]

*After the second stichology, sessional hymns. Tone 8:*[^10228]  
In the midst of Eden a tree blossomed death, but in the midst of the earth a Tree grew life; for having at first tasted the fruit, being incorruptible, we became corruptible; but having attained the second, we enjoyed incorruption. For by the Cross, as God, Thou savest mankind.[^10229]

*Stichos: But God is our King before the ages; He hath wrought salvation in the midst of the earth.*[^10230]

Of old in Paradise a tree stripped me bare, the enemy bringing in mortification by tasting; but the Tree of the Cross, bearing the garment of eternal life for men, was planted upon the earth, and the whole world was filled with every joy. Beholding it exalted, let us the people cry aloud in faith harmoniously unto God: Full of glory is Thy house.[^10231]

*Stichos: Marvelous is God in His saints, the God of Israel.*  
*Martyrion:*  
Ye appeared by faith as ever-radiant luminaries unto the universe, having placed all your martyr-hope upon God; and with the spiritual oil of the Holy Spirit ye enlightened the lamps of your souls. Therefore also as spiritual bowls...[^10232]

...pouring forth healings unto men like waters, ye appeared unto the Church, O all-lauded passion-bearers: pray ye unto Christ God to grant remission of transgressions unto them that celebrate your holy memory with love.[^10233]

*Glory: both now: Stavrotheotokion:*  
The Ewe-Lamb, seeing her Lamb and Shepherd and Deliverer unjustly lifted up upon the Cross, weeping bitterly cried aloud: The world indeed rejoices, receiving through Thee redemption; but my maternal heart burns, beholding Thy crucifixion, which Thou endurest for the tender mercy of Thy loving-kindness, O All-Good God, O Sinless Lord. Unto her let us faithfully cry: Show forth thy compassion toward us, O Virgin, and grant remission of sins unto them that worship His sufferings.[^10234]

*After the third stichology, sessional hymns. Tone 8:*  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "That which was commanded mystically" (Повелѣ́нное та́йнѡ):*[^10235]  
Beholding Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Christ, the unwaning Sun: through fear the sun was immediately darkened, the earth was shaken, and the stones split with trembling; and the veil of the temple was rent, and the dead arose from the graves, glorifying Thy fearful and divine condescension, O our only God.[^10236]

A tree of old brought forth bitterness in Eden, but the Tree of the Cross blossomed sweet life; for Adam, having eaten, slipped into corruption, but we, delighting in the Flesh of Christ, are made alive, and being mystically deified receive the ever-existing Kingdom of God; therefore also in faith we cry: Glory to Thy passion, O Word.[^10237]

*Glory: both now: Stavrotheotokion:*  
Being ever preserved by the Cross of Thy Son and God, O Virgin, we overcome all demonic assaults and wiles, truly chanting of thee as the true Theotokos, and in love all generations call thee blessed, O All-Pure, even as thou didst foretell. Therefore grant remission of our sins by thy prayers.[^10238]

*Canon to the Precious and Life-Creating Cross.*  
*(Whose acrostic without the Theotokia is: Thanksgiving unto God nailed to the Tree.) The work of Joseph.*[^10239]  
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^10240]  
The wonderworking rod of Moses of old plunged the charioteer Pharaoh into the deep, striking the sea crosswise and dividing it; yet it saved Israel, the fleeing wayfarer, as he sang a hymn unto God.[^10241]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy precious Cross.*

*Troparion 1:*  
O Word, Who wast put to death upon the Cross, Thou hast restored me to life who was dead through the pleasurable eating of the tree, and adorned me with glory. I bow down before Thy sov-...[^10242]

...-ereign might, glorifying Thy sufferings and unsearchable tender-heartedness.[^10243]

*Troparion 2:*  
When the uncultivated Vine was stretched upon the Tree, It poured forth for us the wine of divine grace that gladdens hearts, utterly destroying the drunkenness of delusion, and cleansing sins.[^10244]

*Martyrion 1:*  
Adorned with wounds, O Great-Martyrs, and beautified with the pains of sufferings, ye stood with glory in beauty before the beneficent Master, rejoicing all-gloriously and known as God-like.[^10245]

*Martyrion 2:*  
Manifestly strengthened by divine power, O Saints, ye manfully cast down the strong one; and being beautifully adorned with victorious crowns, ye stood before God, rejoicing.[^10246]

*Theotokion:*  
Standing by Thy Cross, O Lord, the Unwedded Maiden, beholding Thy wounding, O Master, being wounded said: Woe is me, my Child! having escaped pains in Thy birth, now am I painfully torn with anguish.[^10247]

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*  
*Tone 8. Same Heirmos.*[^10248]  
*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*

*Troparion 1:*  
In thee, O Virgin, have I placed the hope of my salvation; wash me clean of all defilement of sin, and make me pure, to be well-pleasing unto God and Thy Son, and to labor for His all-holy name.[^10249]

*Troparion 2:*  
O Gate of light, enlighten mine eyes, which the dark serpent hath darkened by the gloom of transgressions; and open unto me the gates of repentance, O Virgin, guide me unto life, and snatch me away from flame and darkness.[^10250]

*Troparion 3:*  
O Mother of God, as thou hast boldness toward the Only-Begotten Word born of thee, co-beginningless with the Father: pray, O All-Immaculate, to deliver my soul from demonic malice, and from fire, and from every torment.[^10251]

*Theotokion:*  
Blessed is the fruit of thy womb, O Bride of God, through Whom all we earthborn were delivered from the curse, O blessed and all-pure Lady: ineffable wonder, incomprehensible knowledge, the salvation of all the faithful![^10252]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*  
Thou Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens by understanding, and didst found the earth upon the waters: establish me, O Christ, upon the rock of the Church; for there is none holy save Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^10253]

*Troparion 1:*  
Thou Who didst establish the heavens, and didst found the earth, and didst bind the sea by a word: Thou wast bound for my sake, and nailed unto the Cross, that Thou mightest loose me from the bonds of sin, O Lover of mankind.[^10254]

*Troparion 2:*  
Rushing against the Tree of the Cross, the enemy was slain together with his evildoing demons; and he that was condemned by the deceitful tasting was shown mercy, and by the confirmation of piety creation was established.[^10255]

*Martyrion 1:*  
Given over by deceitful persecutors unto manifold wounds, and swords, and wild beasts, and bodily stripping, the divine and noble sufferers remained uncaptured by a higher beckoning.[^10256]

*Martyrion 2:*  
Looking with understanding and sober thought unto the things to come, the glorious Martyrs of Christ utterly disregarded passing things; therefore, rejoicing, they endured intolerable pains.[^10257]

*Stavrotheotokion:*  
Beholding the Lamb unjustly lifted up upon the Tree, the much-hymned Ewe-Lamb cried aloud, weeping, shedding maternal tears, and praising His longsuffering, she glorified Him.[^10258]

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^10259]  
*Ode 3. Heirmos:*  
Thou art the confirmation of them that flee unto Thee, O Lord; Thou art the light of the darkened, and my spirit hymns Thee.[^10260]

*Troparion 1:*  
Directing all my desire unto thee, O pure Sovereign Lady: quickly turn me away from fleshly desires.[^10261]

*Troparion 2:*  
Make the pure rays of repentance to dawn upon me, O Sovereign Lady, gate of light, and consume the gloom of my sins.[^10262]

*Troparion 3:*  
O only All-Immaculate, deliver us from every blemish, from assailants of temptations, and from everlasting fire.[^10263]

*Troparion 4:*  
Make haste to visit him that is sick, O All-Pure: and deliver me from grievous wounds and every affliction.[^10264]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*  
Thou art my strength, O Lord, Thou art also my power; Thou art my God, Thou art my rejoicing, Who hast not left the bosom of the Father, yet hast visited our poverty; therefore with the Prophet Habakkuk I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind.[^10265]

*Troparion 1:*  
The human race was recalled from the fall which the first-fashioned gave of old; for the Creator of all was lifted up upon the Tree, having His fingers bloodied, and being voluntarily nailed by nails in His hands, and pierced in His side with a spear.[^10266]

*Troparion 2:*  
The Cross stood, and all deceit fell down; when Thou, O Savior, wast stripped bare, the stranger was stripped bare, and Adam was clothed with the robe of divine incorruption; creation was enlightened, while the sun was darkened, as Thou, O Christ, wast crucified upon the Tree.[^10267]

*Martyrion 1:*  
As sheep of the new slaughter, O Martyrs, ye offered yourselves unto the Word Who was sacrificed, having dried up the sea of delusion by streams of blood through divine grace, and ever consuming the outpouring of passions by the shower of miracles, O glorious ones.[^10268]

*Martyrion 2:*  
Ye endured the severing of all limbs, O Martyrs: the uprooting of teeth and nails, the unsparing cutting off of hands, tongue, feet, and bodily joints; therefore ye were accounted worthy of exceedingly great glory, standing before the God of all.[^10269]

*Stavrotheotokion:*  
Seeing her young Bull lifted up upon the Tree, the spotless Heifer cried aloud with exclamation: O Child! How did the assembly of the lawless not pity Thee at all, Who didst show pity unto them, but by deceitful consideration purposed unjustly to deliver Thee unto death?[^10270]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 4. Heirmos:*  
I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I have understood Thy works, and I have glorified Thy Divinity.[^10271]

*Troparion 1:*  
Enlighten with light my soul that is darkened by transgressions, O Ever-Virgin, who didst bear the Sun of righteousness.[^10272]

*Troparion 2:*  
Snatch me away from temptations, and the soul-destroying storms of life, and free me from everlasting fire, O Bride of God.[^10273]

*Troparion 3:*  
O sacred vessel of virginity, and dwelling-place of Him Who is uncontainable by nature: enlighten my soul that is darkened by many passions.[^10274]

*Troparion 4:*  
O All-Holy Bride of God, Sovereign Lady of the world: save me, delivering me from perils, and driving away the turmoil of passions.[^10275]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*  
Why hast Thou cast me away from Thy face, O unwaning Light, and strange...[^10276]

...darkness hath covered me, wretch that I am? But convert me, and unto the light of Thy commandments direct my paths, I pray Thee.[^10277]
