*Troparion 1:*  
That Thou mightest deliver me from pleasurable tasting, O Longsuffering One, Thou didst willingly taste gall; and that Thou mightest strip off from me the deadness of passions, O Jesus, Thou didst deign to be nailed naked upon the Tree. I hymn Thy tender-heartedness.[^10278]

*Troparion 2:*  
Making new my soul that was corrupted by passions, O Word, Thou didst commit Thy soul unto the Father, hanging upon the Tree; and the soulless earth could not bear understanding these things, but quaked with fear, hymning Thee.[^10279]

*Martyrion 1:*  
Ye were adorned, O Martyrs, beautified by divine sufferings, and having followed in the steps of the Only-Begotten Word of the unoriginate Father, Who by sufferings granted dispassion unto all; for this cause ye are glorified with Him.[^10280]

*Martyrion 2:*  
Having estranged yourselves from earthly things, ye inherited unseen things, taking up your abode in the heavenly divine tabernacles, and being immaterially deified by divine communion, O invincible Martyrs of the Savior.[^10281]

*Stavrotheotokion:*  
A strange vision do I behold! cried the All-Hymned, how Thou Who shakest the whole earth by Thy glance, being exalted upon the Tree, hast fallen asleep, desiring to awaken them that sleep from the ages? I bow down, O Son, before Thy longsuffering.[^10282]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 5. Heirmos:*  
Rising early from the night we cry unto Thee: Lord, save us; for Thou art our God, besides Thee we know no other.[^10283]

*Troparion 1:*  
We hymn thee, O all-hymned Virgin, who didst contain the Word of God within thy womb, O All-Immaculate.[^10284]

*Troparion 2:*  
From unquenchable fire and the worm, deliver me, O Mother of God, as thou hast the power to show mercy with healing.[^10285]

*Troparion 3:*  
A wall and might of the faithful art thou, O All-Holy, saving them from temptations that hymn thee.[^10286]

*Troparion 4:*  
Heal my ailing soul, O thou that didst bear the Salvation of all, and Him that takes away infirmities, O pure Sovereign Lady.[^10287]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*  
The abyss of sins and the storm of transgressions trouble me, and drag me into the deep of grievous despair; but stretch forth Thy strong hand unto me as unto Peter, O Pilot, and save me.[^10288]

*Troparion 1:*  
All the heavenly powers hymned and were amazed, beholding Thee, O Word...[^10289]

...hanging upon the Cross; and wounded Adam, O Savior, was healed by Thy wound, and the curse was driven away.[^10290]

*Troparion 2:*  
Mankind is loosed from bonds insoluble, when Thou, O Word, wast bound in the flesh; and the tormentor is bound like a bird, mocked by all the faithful. Glory, O Christ, to Thy tender-heartedness.[^10291]

*Martyrion 1:*  
Ye appeared, O God-illumined passion-bearers, like burning coals, consuming every substance of impiety by grace, yet being wholly consumed upon coals of fire, and receiving divine refreshment.[^10292]

*Martyrion 2:*  
Revealed as sheep of the true Shepherd, O passion-bearers, ye remained uninjured in the midst of wild wolves; and having accomplished a noble course, O divine ones, ye took up your abode in the heavenly fold.[^10293]

*Stavrotheotokion:*  
I bare Thee as the Lord of life, and fair with beauty beyond the sons of men, cried the Virgin; and how dost Thou die now, O Son, having no beauty in Thy crucifixion, Who didst adorn all things by Thy beckoning?[^10294]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 6. Heirmos:*  
Cleanse me, O Savior! for many are my iniquities, and lead me up from the depth of evils, I pray; for unto Thee have I cried, and give ear unto me, O God of my salvation.[^10295]

*Troparion 1:*  
O Mary, pure and all-honorable dwelling-place of the Creator of all: grant me tears that cleanse the soul, and snatch me away from the future judgment and torment.[^10296]

*Troparion 2:*  
O Gate of God, show forth the divine entrances unto my humble soul, entering into which with confession, I shall receive remission of evils, O Theotokos.[^10297]

*Troparion 3:*  
The abyss of sins and waves of despair tempest my mind: have compassion, O Sovereign Lady, and stretch forth thy hand unto me and save me, who didst bear the Savior.[^10298]

*Troparion 4:*  
Thee as protectress and wall have all we faithful acquired; who in the depth of evils, and turmoils, and afflictions ever are in peril, O Theotokos, only refuge of the faithful.[^10299]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*  
The condescension of God made the fire ashamed in Babylon of old; therefore the Children in the furnace, dancing with joyful foot as in a meadow, sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^10300]

*Troparion 1:*  
Thou wast wounded in Thy hands, O Christ, with which Thou didst work miracles, and Thou didst endure wounds, healing all my wounds. I hymn Thee, O only Longsuffering One, crying: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^10301]

*Troparion 2:*  
Thou wast nailed in Thy hands and feet with nails, O Savior, being crucified, and being pierced in the ribs, Thou didst pour forth drops of forgiveness for all, who hymn unceasingly and say: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^10302]

*Martyrion 1:*  
Let us form a choir, hymnodying the Martyrs of God, who were numbered with the angelic choirs, and enlighten earthly beings, and ever sing: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^10303]

*Martyrion 2:*  
Sanctified in holy splendors, ye took up your abode, sending forth both sanctification and deliverance unto all that praise you, O divine Martyrs, and hymn Christ: O God of our fathers, blessed art Thou.[^10304]

*Stavrotheotokion:*  
O unwaning Sun, how hast Thou set, being crucified upon the Tree? the Virgin cried unto Thee, O Word; and seeing it, the sun stayed its course, unable to shine while Thou wast suffering, O Master. I hymn Thy guilelessness, O Son.[^10305]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 7. Heirmos:*  
The Hebrew Children in the furnace boldly trampled upon the flame, and turned the fire into dew, crying: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God, unto the ages.[^10306]

*Troparion 1:*  
I pray thee, O All-Pure: mortify the sin that dwells in me, and vouchsafe me to obtain life, O Virgin, and deliver me from the portion of them that are tormented there.[^10307]

*Troparion 2:*  
Diverse passions tempest me; O pure one who didst bear the Fountain of dispassion, deliver me from their dominion and from everlasting fire, O Theotokos, by thy prayers.[^10308]

*Troparion 3:*  
By willful disposition I sin, and am enslaved by unseemly habits; now unto thy customary mercy I flee: save me that am in despair, O all-holy Theotokos.[^10309]

*Troparion 4:*  
Quench the flame of my passions, and calm the storm of my heart, O pure Mother of God. Deliver me, O All-Pure, from the torment of demons and from everlasting fire.[^10310]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*  
Sevenfold did the Chaldean tormentor furiously heat the furnace for the godly; but seeing them saved by a higher power, he cried unto the Creator and Deliverer: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^10311]

*Troparion 1:*  
From the root of Jesse, O Christ, Thou didst sprout forth, being incarnate, and Thou rootest up the grown thorns of Adam's transgression, wearing a crown of thorns; and being nailed upon the Tree, Thou healest the curse that grew from the tree, and savest them that sing: Ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^10312]

*Troparion 2:*  
That Thou mightest make man God, O Lover of mankind, Thou becamest man, and partaking of the Cross, Thou wast pierced in the ribs, and given vinegar with gall to drink; whence saved by Thy passions, O Word, we cry thankfully: Ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Christ unto all ages.[^10313]

*Martyrion 1:*  
Being bound, O Martyrs, and slaughtered as lambs, and unmercifully baked upon fire, and given over unto wild beasts, and beheaded, ye rejoiced with unspeakable joy, crying: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Christ unto the ages.[^10314]

*Martyrion 2:*  
O crown-bearing Martyrs, companions of the Angels, who have trampled down the bodiless enemies: make prayer concerning us unto the Lord, that we may live in love and in much oneness of mind, crying with an undoubting heart: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Christ unto the ages.[^10315]

*Stavrotheotokion:*  
Painfully groaning, she cried aloud maternally, and unable to endure the inward turmoil, she looked upon the Cross toward Him that was born from her womb, crying: What is this vision, O Child? How dost Thou suffer, Who art impassible by nature, desiring by all means to free the human race from passions?[^10316]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 8. Heirmos:*  
The King of heaven, Whom the angelic hosts hymn: praise and supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^10317]

*Troparion 1:*  
O Virgin Mother of God, lighten my sins and the burden of transgressions, that I may magnify thee.[^10318]

*Troparion 2:*  
O thou that didst bear the Judge and God: by thy favorably reconciling prayers show Him to be merciful unto me, O Pure One, that He may save me from everlasting fire.[^10319]

*Troparion 3:*  
My transgressions have multiplied greatly, O Theotokos: give me now a helping hand, and deliver me, unprofitable as I am, from unquenchable flame.[^10320]

*Troparion 4:*  
Enlighten the eyes of my heart, O All-Pure, I pray, which are blinded by the blackness of sin, and make them receptive of divine brightness, that for thy sake I may appear pure before thy Son.[^10321]

*My soul doth magnify the Lord:*[^10322]  
*Ode 9. Heirmos:*  
Heaven was struck with awe at this, and the ends of the earth were amazed, that God hath appeared in the flesh unto men, and thy womb became more spacious than the heavens. Therefore the ranks of Angels and of men magnify thee, O Theotokos.[^10323]

*Troparion 1:*  
Thou hast healed my wounds, O Master, having been wounded and having bloodied Thy hands; and unto saving paths to walk, as Thou art good, Thou hast directed me, O Lord: having dug Thy feet upon the Cross, which of old when the first ancestors saw walking in Paradise, they hid themselves.[^10324]

*Troparion 2:*  
When Thou wast set upright upon the Cross, the first-fashioned who had fallen with a great fall was righted, and all enemy might fell down, and all the earth was sanctified by the blood and water poured forth from Thy ribs. Therefore unceasingly we magnify Thee, O All-Compassionate One.[^10325]

*Martyrion 1:*  
Bound, O holy Martyrs, ye dissolved the bonds of the deceitful one, and by the bonds which ye patiently endured, ye bound him, and subdued him under your feet, full of shame, and made him a laughing-stock unto them that beheld, by divine grace.[^10326]

*Martyrion 2:*  
The earth was sanctified by the deposition of the holy relics of the Martyrs; for as a fountain pouring forth all manner of healings it divinely possesses these, unceasingly healing bodily and spiritual passions, and repelling demonic harm by divine grace.[^10327]

*Stavrotheotokion:*  
Having escaped maternal pangs at the time of Thy birth, O Longsuffering One: now as Thou hast partaken of voluntary passion and received sufferings, I have been pained in my womb, and with pain...[^10328]

...of soul am I filled! cried the All-Pure Lady; whom worthily we magnify.[^10329]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 9. Heirmos:*  
Every ear was struck with awe at the ineffable condescension of God, how the Most High voluntarily descended even unto the flesh, from a virginal womb, having become man; therefore, O all-pure Theotokos, we the faithful magnify thee.[^10330]

*Troparion 1:*  
Have mercy, O All-Pure, upon my wretched soul, and mortify destructive passions, and drive away tormenting perplexity; and grant holy, ever-flowing streams of tears, whereby I may be delivered from the grievous condemnation that awaits me.[^10331]

*Troparion 2:*  
A wall unto Christians, and a firm refuge unto the world, wherein we are saved, art thou, O pure Virgin, Bride of God: for God, having become incarnate of thee, granted thee unto all as a saving protection. Therefore, O Pure One, save me that am unworthy.[^10332]

*Troparion 3:*  
O unoriginate Word of the Father, and my Son, enthroned together with the Holy Spirit: how hast Thou stretched out Thy pure palms upon the Cross? What is this Thy great poverty now, O All-Good One? standing by Thy crucifixion, cried the All-Immaculate.[^10333]

*Troparion 4:*  
Distilling divine sweetness, O thou that didst bear the Sweetness of all! sweeten my soul that is embittered with the poison of the serpent, ever estranging me from bitter sin by thine intercession, O shameless protectress of the faithful.[^10334]

*It is truly meet: Litany. Exapostilarion and the usual psalms.*[^10335]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 8:*[^10336]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
The rod of Moses prefigured Thy precious Cross, O our Savior: for thereby Thou savest Thy people, as from the depths of the sea, O Lover of mankind.[^10337]

*Stichos: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad. ... And look upon Thy servants and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^10338]

*Sticheron 2:*  
That which grew of old in Eden, in Paradise, was a tree for food in the midst of the orchards; but Thy Church, O Christ, hath blossomed Thy Cross, pouring forth life unto all the world. But that one indeed slew by eating Adam who ate thereof; while this made alive the thief who was saved by faith: of whose salvation show us also partakers, O Christ God, Who by Thy passion...[^10339]

...hast destroyed the wiles of the enemy against us, and vouchsafe us Thy kingdom, O Lord.[^10340]

*Stichos: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, direct Thou the work of our hands.*[^10341]

*Martyrion:*  
O invincible Martyrs of Christ, having conquered deceit by the power of the Cross, ye received the grace of eternal life; not fearing the threats of the tormentors, wounded with torments ye rejoiced; and now your blood hath become healings for our souls: pray ye that our souls may be saved.[^10342]

*Glory: both now: Stavrotheotokion:*  
Seeing Thee hanging upon the Cross, O Lord, the Pure One who bare Thee, being nearby, weeping said: O Child, why dost Thou suffer these things in the flesh, and makest haste to leave me childless? Make haste to be glorified, that I may be magnified by Thy passion.[^10343]

*It is good to give praise: Trisagion, and after Our Father: troparion, litany, and the rest.*[^10344]

*On Wednesday, The Beatitudes.*[^10345]  
*Tone 8:*  
*Troparion 1:*  
Remember us, O Christ, Savior of the world, even as Thou didst remember the thief upon the Tree; and vouchsafe all, O only Compassionate One, Thy heavenly kingdom.[^10346]

*Troparion 2:*  
Prefiguring the Cross with his rod, Moses divided the deep, leading across the Israelite people; but we, portraying it, conquer our noetic enemies.[^10347]

*Troparion 3:*  
Of old Jacob, blessing the children, the sons of his sons, stretched out his hands crosswise, signifying Thy Cross, O Christ Savior, whereby all of us were freed from the curse.[^10348]

*Martyrion:*  
Zealous for Christ's passions, O passion-bearers, ye manfully endured bitter torments, and crowned with incorruptible crowns, ye dwell in the heavens.[^10349]

*Glory:*  
Glory to the Father, the only Immortal; glory to the Son, Who lives unto the ages; glory together to the All-Holy Spirit, Who sanctifies all creation.[^10350]

*Both now:*  
Out of thy virginal womb, O Pure One, shone forth the Creator of the sun and the moon; Whom beholding hanging upon the Tree, all creation trembled.[^10351]

## Thursday: The Holy Apostles and Saint Nicholas[^10352]

*On Wednesday Evening.*[^10353]  
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera to the Holy Apostles, Tone 8.*[^10354]  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "O Lord, though Thou didst stand before the tribunal" (Гдⷭ҇и, а҆́ще и҆ на сꙋди́щи):*[^10355]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
O Lord, Thou didst enlighten Thine Apostles with the rays of the Comforter, and having set them as luminaries for the confirmation of the faith, by the spiritual illumination of Thy knowledge, O Master: therefore we worship Thine ineffable love for mankind.[^10356]

*Sticheron 2:*  
O Lord, by the prayers of the Apostles Thou hast fenced this Thy flock, preserving it unharmed from the temptation of enemies, since Thou didst redeem it by Thy precious blood from the servitude of the stranger, as Thou art compassionate and a lover of mankind.[^10357]

*Sticheron 3:*  
Ye appeared together in the foundation of the Church like precious stones, shining with a radiant beam unto the universe the sure knowledge of God, O divine Apostles, standers-before the Trinity, and intercessors for our souls.[^10358]

*Then in the Menaion, stichera to the Saint, if there be any. If there be no Menaion:*[^10359]  
*Other Stichera, to the Holy Wonderworker Nicholas, Tone 8.*[^10360]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
O Lord, Thou hast glorified Thy precious hierarch Nicholas unto the ends of the earth, having given unto him the grace of miracles; and Thou hast shown him forth as a helper unto them that are in grievous perils, and in afflictions and tribulations, who fall down and ask his protection.[^10361]

*Sticheron 2:*  
O Lord, through the divine entreaties of Thy hierarch, have compassion, and free Thy servants from every peril, and from every affliction and assault of the enemy, who in faith worship Thine invincible might.[^10362]

*Sticheron 3:*  
O Lord, Thou hast shown forth the hierarch in the world as a sweet fragrance: for he makes fragrant the hearts of all the faithful by miracles, driving away diseases and passions of souls by Thy grace. By his prayers, as Thou art compassionate, save them that hymn Thee.[^10363]

*Glory: both now: Theotokion:*  
Behold the sighing of my contrite heart, O Bride of God. Accept, O pure Virgin Mary, the lifting up of my hands, as thou art a lover of mankind, and cast me not away, O All-Immaculate, that I may hymn and magnify thee, who hast magnified our race.[^10364]

*Joyful Light: and the rest.*[^10365]  
*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Apostles, Tone 8:*[^10366]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
O Lord, having fervently loved Thee upon the earth, the Apostles counted all things as refuse...[^10367]

...more vile than all things, that they might gain Thee alone, and for Thy sake they gave over their bodies unto wounds; therefore having been glorified, they pray for our souls.[^10368]

*Stichos: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven. ... So look our eyes unto the Lord our God, until He take pity on us.*[^10369]

*Sticheron 2:*  
O Lord, Thou hast magnified the memory of the Apostles upon the earth; for we all, having assembled together therein, doxologize Thee: that for their sake Thou grantest us healings, and unto all the world pacification by their prayers, and great mercy.[^10370]

*Stichos: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are greatly filled ... Our soul is exceedingly filled with the reproach of them that are at ease, and the contempt of the proud.*[^10371]

*Martyrion:*  
If there be any virtue, and if there be any praise, it is fitting for the Saints; for they bowed their necks unto the sword, for Thy sake Who didst bow the heavens and come down; they shed their blood for Thy sake Who didst empty Thyself and take the form of a servant; they humbled themselves even unto death, imitating Thy poverty. By their prayers, according to the multitude of Thy tender mercies, O God, have mercy on us.[^10372]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
I, O holy Virgin Theotokos, flee unto thy protection: I know that through thee I shall find salvation; for thou art able, O Pure One, to help me.[^10373]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart: Trisagion. After Our Father: troparion, litany, and dismissal.*[^10374]

*On Wednesday at Compline.*[^10375]  
*Supplicatory Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^10376]  
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*  
The charioteer Pharaoh did the wonderworking rod of Moses of old drown, striking crosswise and dividing the sea; but it saved Israel a fugitive, walking on foot, singing a song unto God.[^10377]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^10378]

*Troparion 1:*  
Thou didst bear incarnate the Word of God, Who was before bodiless, beyond nature, O all-immaculate Virgin Theotokos, Who in a God-manly manner lived in the world; therefore we all glorify thee, after God our protection.[^10379]

*Troparion 2:*  
Variegated with divers colors, O graced of God: the Father's Word having become flesh through ineffable compassion, beyond mind and word, O Blessed One, thou didst bear, remaining an undefiled virgin.[^10380]

*Glory...*  
*Troparion 3:*  
From a royal root, O Pure One, thou didst sprout forth; Christ the King, God's...[^10381]

...Word, incarnate beyond mind and understanding, thou didst bear from thy pure blood, shown forth two-fold in nature and single in hypostasis.[^10382]

*Both now...*  
*Troparion 4:*  
Whosoever proclaims thee to be Theotokos, O All-Pure, bears victory over all heresies: for Him Who unchangeably became flesh hast thou brought forth, O Mother of God, higher than all creation, the co-eternal Word of God.[^10383]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*  
Thou Who in the beginning didst establish the heavens by understanding, and didst found the earth upon the waters: establish me, O Christ, upon the rock of the Church; for there is none holy save Thee, O only Lover of mankind.[^10384]

*Troparion 1:*  
Into thy holy womb God took up His abode, and became incarnate as He willed, O Mother of God, and saved us by His life-bearing passions; therefore we glorify thee as the door of salvation.[^10385]

*Troparion 2:*  
He that breathed a soul into all men, having become incarnate upon the earth, lived with men, having come forth from thy womb without seed, the Pre-divine; therefore we all, the faithful, bless thee with hymns, O Pure One.[^10386]

*Glory...*  
*Troparion 3:*  
Having conceived in thy womb without wedlock, O Virgin, Him that was begotten of the Father before the ages, thou didst bear together God and man, perfect in both natures and indivisible.[^10387]

*Both now...*  
*Troparion 4:*  
The rod that budded Christ the blossom of incorruption, and the golden censer, do we all know thee to be, O Pure One, bearing the divine essence as a coal upon thine arm, O God-blessed Virgin.[^10388]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*  
Thou art my strength, O Lord, Thou art also my power; Thou art my God, Thou art my rejoicing, Who hast not left the bosom of the Father, yet hast visited our poverty; therefore with the Prophet Habakkuk I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind.[^10389]

*Troparion 1:*  
The ark that received the God-written law prefigured from afar the Life-originating Word in thy womb, ineffably conceived, O All-Immaculate, richly nourishing the souls of them that sing: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind.[^10390]

*Troparion 2:*  
Thou wast the true cause of salvation, O Mother of God, unto the Creator and Maker, having lent flesh from thy most pure blood; whereby the gates of death were shattered, and life was bestowed upon men.[^10391]

*Glory...*  
*Troparion 3:*  
That Thou mightest seek out the image buried by passions through the riches...[^10392]

...of goodness, Thou didst dwell in the Virgin's womb, out of which Thou, the Wisdom of God, didst create a temple, wherein having lived with men, O Compassionate One, Thou didst save the ends of the world.[^10393]

*Both now...*  
*Troparion 4:*  
After God we have acquired thee as protectress, O All-Immaculate: for thou wast the Mother of God the Creator and Maker, Who took our form, and saved it from corruption and perils, and glorified it with divine glory.[^10394]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*  
Why hast Thou cast me away from Thy face, O unwaning Light, and strange darkness hath covered me, wretch that I am? But convert me, and unto the light of Thy commandments direct my paths, I pray Thee.[^10395]

*Troparion 1:*  
As having conceived God the Maker, Creator, and Lord, O All-Pure, the generations of all men call thee blessed, and the noetic chieftains of the bodiless powers glorify thee as Mother of God.[^10396]

*Troparion 2:*  
The hosts of the higher powers call thee blessed, O All-Pure; for through thee alone do those upon earth, being united with the heavenly, hymn thy Nativity.[^10397]

*Glory...*  
*Troparion 3:*  
In an ineffable manner, O Virgin, the Word made His dwelling within thee, and became man, Who shone forth from the Father before the ages, and delivering men from corruption, leading them unto incorruptible life, the only Tender-hearted One.[^10398]

*Both now...*  
*Troparion 4:*  
Thou wast adorned, O all-pure Virgin, with the beauties of virginity, and hast loosed the ill-favoredness of the first Eve, having borne Christ Who bestows the robe of immortality upon them that hymn thee.[^10399]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*  
Cleanse me, O Savior! for many are my iniquities, and lead me up from the depth of evils, I pray; for unto Thee have I cried, and give ear unto me, O God of my salvation.[^10400]

*Troparion 1:*  
He Who by almighty power upholds all things clothed Himself in fleshly weakness from thee, O all-immaculate Virgin, for the benefaction of men, as He is the Lover of mankind.[^10401]

*Troparion 2:*  
The all-effecting Spirit came upon thee, O All-Immaculate, and the Word of God made His abode within thee, and ineffably became flesh, remaining unchanged.[^10402]

*Glory...*  
*Troparion 3:*  
Enlighten the souls of them that orthodoxly hymn thee: the sayings of the Prophets proclaimed beforehand thy most pure conception and thy birthgiving beyond speech, O Bride of God...[^10403]

*Both now...*  
*Troparion 4:*  
As the habitation of God, truly divine and most honorable, we piously hymn the most pure Mary; for she received God, containing Him Who is uncontainable and incomprehensible.[^10404]

*Then, Lord, have mercy (thrice). Glory... Both now...*[^10405]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 8:*  
As all-immaculate bride of the Creator, as unwedded Mother of the Deliverer, as being the vessel of the Comforter, O All-Hymned One: make haste to deliver me who am a foul dwelling of iniquity and have consciously become a plaything of demons, from their evildoing, making me a bright habitation through virtues, O giver of light; and disperse the corrupting cloud of passions, and vouchsafe me heavenly communion and unwaning light by thy prayers.[^10406]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*  
At God's condescension the fire was put to shame in Babylon of old; therefore the Children in the furnace, with joyful step, dancing as in a meadow of flowers, sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers.[^10407]

*Troparion 1:*  
O All-Pure Sovereign Lady, who didst conceive the Deliverer of all: vouchsafe me ineffable and divine deliverance, who cry aloud with a loud voice and sing: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^10408]

*Troparion 2:*  
Now all things are filled with divine light through thee, O All-Pure: for thou wast shown forth as the door whereby God conversed with the world, enlightening them that cry in faith: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^10409]

*Glory...*  
*Troparion 3:*  
Behold, now is fulfilled the divine prophecy of divine David: truly they entreat thy countenance, O pure Mother of God, who have acquired the wealth of grace, now blessing the God of our fathers.[^10410]

*Both now...*  
*Troparion 4:*  
Thou didst conceive, O All-Pure, God and Lord over all, Who was well-pleased to save the human race from death and corruption; Whom worthily hymning, we cry: Blessed is the God of our fathers.[^10411]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*  
Having become conquerors of the tormentor and of the flame by Thy grace, cleaving earnestly unto Thy commandments, the Children cried: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.[^10412]

*Troparion 1:*  
O Blessed All-Pure One, cleanse the wounds of my soul and the sinful...[^10413]

...stripes, having born God Who is over all, O pure Maiden, from a virginal unwedded womb: Whom bless, ye children, and supremely exalt unto the ages.[^10414]

*Troparion 2:*  
Thou wast the vessel of the unwaning Light, O All-Pure, shining with the beauties of virginity; thou hast illuminated all who from the soul confess thee to be the true Theotokos, and cry: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^10415]

*Glory...*  
*Troparion 3:*  
Sanctified by the Holy Spirit, O all-immaculate Theotokos, truly didst thou receive in thy womb the unoriginate and co-eternal Son of the Father, Who in thee became incarnate, O Pure One, for the benefaction of them that cry in faith: Ye children, bless; ye priests, sing, and supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^10416]

*Both now...*  
*Troparion 4:*  
As having born God beyond word and understanding, entreat Him, O all-pure Mother of God, to be clement unto all of us who are unworthy, at His dread and fearful coming, when He shall judge human nature, when all stand naked and are judged with fear.[^10417]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*  
Heaven was struck with awe at this, and the ends of the earth were amazed, that God hath appeared in the flesh unto men, and thy womb became more spacious than the heavens; therefore the ranks of Angels and of men magnify thee, O Theotokos.[^10418]

*Troparion 1:*  
Us who were rejected from eternal dwelling and who had fallen wretchedly into death, hast thou called back, O All-Pure, having become the Mother of the Deliverer, and vouchsafed us to run unto our former fatherland; therefore, O Mother of God, unceasingly we magnify thee.[^10419]

*Troparion 2:*  
Easily grant me, O Theotokos, to pass through life's wave of temptations, and the onset of passions, as thou art good, subduing them, and guiding me unto the virtue of the heavenly journey: that as benefactress I may unceasingly magnify thee.[^10420]

*Glory...*  
*Troparion 3:*  
O pure, most glorious Mother of God, save from temptations them that hymn thee with love, mercifully quelling turmoil, as thou didst bear God: for all that thou desirest to do, thou dost unhindered, and thou art able, O Virgin; therefore we all magnify thee...[^10421]

*Both now...*  
*Troparion 4:*  
Show forth the precious Church conquering juvenile heresies, which Christ Thy Son, O Full of Grace, as He is good, acquired by His precious blood, and save her from perils and from the violence that holds us, O Sovereign Lady.[^10422]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion. After Our Father: troparion and dismissal.*[^10423]

*On Thursday at Matins.*[^10424]  
*After the First Stichology, Sessional Hymns of the Apostles, Tone 8:*[^10425]  
*Sessional Hymn 1:*  
As luminaries of the world, and guides of our salvation, let us hymn the divine disciples of our God from the beginning; for they shone forth as light unto us that were in darkness, and proclaimed the Sun of glory unto all. Therefore also they cast down idol delusion, having preached the Trinity in one Godhead. Therefore we pray unto you, O Apostles: entreat Christ God, to grant us the remission of sins, and great mercy.[^10426]

*Stichos: Their sound is gone out into all the earth, and their words unto the ends of the world.*[^10427]

*Sessional Hymn 2:*  
Your most wise sound having gone out unto all the ends of the world, O truly all-praised Apostles of the Lord, manifestly proclaimed the knowledge of God unto all, and brought heathen ignorance unto understanding. Therefore also having driven away the mist of idolatry, the light of understanding shone forth unto them that were in darkness. Therefore we pray: entreat Christ God, to grant remission of transgressions unto them that honor your holy memory with love.[^10428]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Having fallen into many-woven temptations from enemies visible and invisible, and being held fast by the tempest of my countless sins, O All-Pure: I flee unto the warm intercession, and protection, and haven of thy goodness. Therefore, O All-Pure, entreat Him Who was incarnate of thee without seed on behalf of all thy servants who unceasingly hymn thee, O all-pure Theotokos: praying Him earnestly to grant remission of sins unto them that worship thy Nativity in faith.[^10429]

*After the Second Stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 8:*[^10430]  
*Sessional Hymn 1:*  
Into all the earth, as the Prophet said, went forth the sound of the most wise Apostles; for having preached the Word, all the nations...[^10431]

...they enlightened. Therefore worthily praising them with hymns, let us honor them, as they ever entreat Christ, that our souls may be saved.[^10432]

*Stichos: The heavens shall confess Thy wonders, O Lord.*[^10433]

*Sessional Hymn 2:*  
The Lord's testaments having received in their soul by faith as a wealth-producing treasure, the Apostles were shown forth as benefactors unto all: driving away demonic poverty, and enriching all with the wealth of God; unto whom let us all cry aloud: Enrich us also, we pray, who are now impoverished in virtuous deeds.[^10434]

*Stichos: God is wonderful in His Saints, the God of Israel.*[^10435]

*Martyrion:*  
By self-control having mortified the fiery appearances and stirrings of the passions, the Martyrs received Christ's grace to drive away infirmities from the sick: and both while living and after their end to work miracles, truly a most glorious wonder, that bare bones pour forth healings. Glory to the only all-wise Creator God.[^10436]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Protectress of the faithful, Mother of God, joy of the sorrowing, and great consolation of them that weep: entreat Him Who was born beyond nature from thy womb, O Virgin, the All-Holy One, unceasingly together with the holy Apostles on our behalf, to deliver us in the hour of trial from bitter condemnation.[^10437]

*After the Third Stichology, Sessional Hymns, Tone 8:*[^10438]  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "That which was commanded mystically" (Повелѣ́нное):*[^10439]  
*Sessional Hymn 1:*  
Luminaries bright were ye shown forth, eyewitnesses of Christ, ever enlightening the whole earth, and driving away the darkness of polytheism by the light of piety through your teachings; therefore enlighten me who am in the darkness of passions, walking without fear, by your holy prayers, O God-bearing Apostles.[^10440]

*Sessional Hymn 2:*  
With the net of divine words, catching reasonable men alive as fish, ye brought them as first-fruits unto our God: and having loved Christ's wounds, ye clothed yourselves therein, and were shown forth as imitators of His passions. Therefore having assembled, we worthily honor your festivity now, O glorious Apostles, and with one accord cry aloud: Entreat Christ God, that remission of transgressions be granted unto them that honor your holy memory with love.[^10441]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Entreat the incomprehensible Word, the Son of God, Who was ineffably born of thee beyond understanding, O Theotokos, together with the Apostles: to grant pure pacification unto the universe, and before the end to bestow upon us forgiveness of transgressions, and for the sake of extreme goodness to vouchsafe Thy servants the heavenly kingdom.[^10442]

*Canon to the Holy, Glorious, and All-Praised Apostles.*[^10443]  
*(Whose acrostic is: The choir of the most wise Apostles saves me. A composition of Joseph.)*[^10444]  
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*  
Having passed through the water as it were dry land, and having escaped the Egyptian evil, the Israelite cried aloud: Let us sing unto our Deliverer and God.[^10445]

*Refrain: Holy Apostles, pray to God for us.*[^10446]

*Troparion 1:*  
O light-like choir of the Apostles, standing before the great Light: illuminate my darkened heart, and guide me unto saving paths.[^10447]

*Troparion 2:*  
O intimate friends of the Deliverer, deliver me from the love of passions, who have been deceived by many delusions of life and am covered by the night of senselessness.[^10448]

*Troparion 3:*  
As ye are arrows of the Mighty One, O divine Apostles, heal them that are wounded by the weapon of the evil one, who flee under your protection in faith.[^10449]

*Theotokion:*  
He that is full is emptied in a God-befitting manner, and from thy womb, O pure Mother, He comes forth bearing flesh: saving by His tender-heartedness them that were deceived by the serpent's malice.[^10450]

*Another Canon, to the Holy Wonderworker Nicholas.*[^10451]  
*(Whose acrostic is: Unto thee, O Nicholas, the eighth song is fitting. Of Joseph.)*[^10452]  
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*  
That which was severed cut through the unseverable, and the sun beheld the earth which it had not seen; the water drowned the fierce enemy, and Israel traversed the impassable, while a song was sung: Let us sing unto the Lord, for gloriously is He glorified.[^10453]

*Refrain: Hierarch of Christ Nicholas, pray to God for us.*[^10454]

*Troparion 1:*  
With the choirs of the higher powers standing before the King of all, O blessed one: save us on earth who ever call upon thee from every evil temptation, O Nicholas, asking remission of our transgressions.[^10455]

*Troparion 2:*  
Enriched with thee as protector, O Nicholas, both by day and by night we cry unto thee in faith: come quickly now unto us that are in great peril through demonic evil snares and corrupting men; that having obtained quietness, we may praise thee.[^10456]

*Troparion 3:*  
By the strong sinews of thy prayers, O Nicholas, give over unto final strangling them that strive to strangle us, delivering us from their evil snares, unto...[^10457]

...praise crying aloud: Let us sing unto the Lord, for gloriously is He glorified.[^10458]

*Theotokion:*  
Apart from the laws of nature, thou didst bear God and the Lawgiver, Who became man; entreat Him, as He is good, O All-Immaculate, to overlook our iniquities, who ever cry aloud: Let us sing unto the Lord, for gloriously is He glorified.[^10459]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*  
Thou art the confirmation of them that flee unto Thee, O Lord; Thou art the light of the darkened, and my spirit hymns Thee.[^10460]

*Troparion 1:*  
Gird with power, O Apostles of the Savior, my heart that is weakened by passions and thoughts.[^10461]

*Troparion 2:*  
Proclaiming the one, thrice-radiant brightness of God, the disciples destroyed the mist of polytheism.[^10462]

*Troparion 3:*  
Assuage the evil corruption of my heart, as physicians of divine healing, O disciples of our God.[^10463]

*Theotokion:*  
Save me, O pure Sovereign Lady, who didst bear beyond nature the Deliverer of all, both Master, and Savior, and Lord.[^10464]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Same Heirmos.*[^10465]  
*Troparion 1:*  
That we may honor thee, that we may ever sing of thee: stretch forth unto us a helping hand, O hierarch Nicholas.[^10466]

*Troparion 2:*  
Lull to sleep by thy sleepless prayers, O Nicholas, the temptations that rise up against us, we pray thee.[^10467]

*Troparion 3:*  
Deliver me who am wholly assailed by passions and the temptations of evil men, by thy mediation, O holy Nicholas.[^10468]

*Theotokion:*  
Entreat the Deliverer to grant me remission of sins, O Pure One, and from grievous worldly temptations.[^10469]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*  
I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I have understood Thy works, and I have glorified Thy Divinity.[^10470]

*Troparion 1:*  
Ye were adorned, O glorious Apostles, beautified by divine splendors, and enlightened by the rays of Him Who showed you forth as luminaries.[^10471]

*Troparion 2:*  
Deliver me from every storm, O pilots of all, divine Apostles, who am in peril in the deep and tossed about by worldly temptations.[^10472]

*Troparion 3:*  
O God-chosen horses, who trouble the sea of polytheism: drown the abyss of my transgressions by your prayers.[^10473]

*Theotokion:*  
Thou didst incarnate from thy womb, O Virgin, the Word Who is co-effective with the Father: therefore thou wast shown forth higher than all creatures, O Sovereign Lady.[^10474]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Same Heirmos.*[^10475]  
*Troparion 1:*  
From the multitude of my transgressions I fall into many countless sorrows; make haste, O holy Nicholas, to help me, imitating the Benefactor.[^10476]

*Troparion 2:*  
I weep for myself who am perishing, living in slothfulness; by thy prayers, O Nicholas, lead me unto repentance.[^10477]

*Troparion 3:*  
Thou who didst deliver the three youths who were about to die, O father Nicholas: deliver me from every distress and from eternal condemnation.[^10478]

*Theotokion:*  
Strengthen me, O All-Pure, to do heavenly understandings; and direct the movements of my soul unto the life-bearing entrances.[^10479]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*  
Rising early from the night we cry unto Thee, O Lord: Save us; for Thou art our God, besides Thee we know no other.[^10480]

*Troparion 1:*  
Direct me who am hemmed in by many transgressions unto saving breadth, as helmsmen, O Apostles.[^10481]

*Troparion 2:*  
Deliver me who honor your all-honorable assembly, O God-beholding Apostles, from every dishonorable deed, I pray.[^10482]

*Troparion 3:*  
As ye were conformed unto the Savior's passions, O holy ones: drive away all voluptuousness from my soul, O Apostles.[^10483]

*Theotokion:*  
Heal my ailing soul, O all-pure Sovereign Lady, who didst bear Him Who takes away the infirmities of all.[^10484]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Same Heirmos.*[^10485]  
*Troparion 1:*  
Strengthen us by thy prayers, O God-blessed Nicholas, to keep the commandments of Christ, our only God.[^10486]

*Troparion 2:*  
Having lived in Myra, O Nicholas, fill with divine fragrance us who piously sing of thee.[^10487]

*Troparion 3:*  
Show mercy upon us by thy prayers, O hierarch Nicholas, entreating the all-merciful God, that we may be delivered from perils and tribulations.[^10488]

*Theotokion:*  
Being full of life-giving waters, O Maiden, give drink unto all of us who sing of thee in godly-mindedness.[^10489]

*Tone 8. Ode 6. Heirmos:*  
A prayer I will pour out unto the Lord, and unto Him will I declare my sorrows; for my soul is filled with evils, and my life has drawn near unto hades; and I pray as Jonah: From corruption, O God, lead me up.[^10490]

*Troparion 1:*  
Having shaken down the strongholds of the enemy by the invocation of prayers, ye cast down the idol temples, O disciples of Christ. But I pray with faith: Shatter the idols of my mind, and show me forth as a temple of God who am guilty of many sins.[^10491]

*Troparion 2:*  
Ye who were founded as stones upon the unshakable rock, O chosen eyewitnesses of God: save my heart which is harmfully founded upon the sand; for the rivers of temptations rush together, and fiercely engulf me with storm.[^10492]

*Troparion 3:*  
From deceitful love which is toward the flesh, and from him that appeared vile because of much malice, deliver me, O friends of the Word, and bring me unto the love of Him Who loved the forgiveness of sinners because of great mercy.[^10493]

*Theotokion:*  
As a sweet-briar rose, as a most pure lily, as a fragrant scent from the beautiful valleys of the world, the Creator chose thee, O Maiden, and having dwelt within thy womb and having been born, He filled all things with fragrance.[^10494]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Same Heirmos.*[^10495]  
*Troparion 1:*  
Thy heart was shown forth as a paradise, O Nicholas, having the Deliverer Christ in its midst as the tree of life; Whom entreat unceasingly: that He make all of us citizens of paradise, who have been enriched by thee as a fervent protector.[^10496]

*Troparion 2:*  
In slothfulness I spend my life, and without fear I sin, wretched that I am; and thinking upon the inescapable judgment seat there, I am gripped with fear: Have compassion upon me, O God, through the prayers of Nicholas, for Thou art tender-hearted.[^10497]

*Troparion 3:*  
At the storm of manifold temptations coming suddenly upon me because of the multitude of my transgressions, I cry aloud: Leave me not without protection, O blessed one; but stretch forth unto me a helping hand, as thou art merciful, and save me.[^10498]

*Theotokion:*  
A palace of the exalted King and a fiery throne wast thou, O Virgin: higher than the Cherubim, and having become beyond understanding above the Seraphim. Therefore thee all...[^10499]

...creation magnifies, as the Mother of the Creator.[^10500]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*  
The Hebrew children in the furnace boldly trampled upon the flame, and turned the fire into dew, crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God, unto the ages.[^10501]

*Troparion 1:*  
The night of slothfulness holds me fast, and the darkness of sin covers me; O all-wise Apostles, having appeared as the light of the world, make haste to enlighten my darkened heart.[^10502]

*Troparion 2:*  
Having appeared as another heaven declaring the glory of our God, deliver from the dishonor of passions, O God-proclaiming ones, them that flee in faith unto your sovereign protection.[^10503]

*Troparion 3:*  
Having appeared as fiery coals, O Apostles, ignited by the fire of the Comforter: burn up every material of our malice, and deliver us from the unquenchable fire unto the ages.[^10504]

*Theotokion:*  
Thou wast shown forth as the shadowed mountain unquarried by hand, from which a stone was cut off; therefore, O Sovereign Lady, grant compunction unto my darkened soul, petrified by bitter pleasures.[^10505]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Same Heirmos.*[^10506]  
*Troparion 1:*  
Thou didst pour forth streams of teachings, drying up the torrents of heresies, richly watering the souls of the faithful, O sacred minister Nicholas; therefore we honor thee.[^10507]

*Troparion 2:*  
Thou wast strengthened, empowered by the power of the Trinity, to destroy deceitful idols. Therefore I pray thee with faith, O father: Drive away the passionate idols of my mind.[^10508]

*Troparion 3:*  
Having become the primate of the people of Myra, O venerable one, after death thou pourest forth divine myrrh: driving away our foul-smelling infirmities, O all-blessed Nicholas, from them that approach thee with faith.[^10509]

*Theotokion:*  
Thou didst assuage the mortal course, having given birth unto the immortal God: Whom entreat, O Pure One, to mortify the passions of my humble body, and to vouchsafe me life.[^10510]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*  
Sevenfold the Chaldean tyrant madly kindled the furnace for the godly ones; but seeing them saved by a higher power, he cried aloud unto the Creator and Deliverer: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^10511]

*Troparion 1:*  
Strengthen my soul that is shaken by passions, O ye who are unyielding...[^10512]

...foundations and unshakable pillars of the Church, bulwarks of the faithful, catchers of them that are in the deep of destruction, calm havens of them that sing in faith: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto the ages.[^10513]

*Troparion 2:*  
Have compassion to bring me up by your prayers, who lie in the tomb of dark despair and in the lowest parts of the fall of pleasures, O disciples of the Savior: who were manifestly accounted worthy to be enriched with the Master's tender-heartedness, O Apostles, eyewitnesses of God, unto all ages.[^10514]

*Theotokion:*  
As radiant in the beauties of virtues wast thou shown forth, O All-Immaculate One: thou didst contain in thy womb the beneficent, all-creative splendor; Whom entreat diligently, O Pure One, to enlighten unto all ages my soul, held fast by the sins of passions and seeking the ancient beauty most splendidly.[^10515]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Heirmos:*  
The God-proclaiming children in the furnace, trampling upon the flame with the fire, sang: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord.[^10516]

*Troparion 1:*  
Enlightened as thou art, O godly-wise hierarch, by the light-bearing rays of the thrice-radiant Godhead: destroy the darkness of my passions by thy prayers, O Nicholas.[^10517]

*Troparion 2:*  
Thou who didst deliver the three commanders from death by thy fearsome appearing, O sacred initiate Nicholas: deliver us also from every death-bearing harm.[^10518]

*Troparion 3:*  
Establish us by thy prayers, who are shaken by demonic temptations and by abusive men, and preserve us unharmed, O all-blessed Nicholas.[^10519]

*Theotokion:*  
Offer supplication unto the Lord, to have compassion and to save them that flee unto thee in faith, O all-holy Virgin, helper of men.[^10520]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*  
Heaven was amazed at this, and the ends of the earth were astonished, that God appeared bodily unto men, and thy womb became more spacious than the heavens. Therefore the ranks of Angels and men magnify thee, O Theotokos.[^10521]

*Troparion 1:*  
Having shattered the jaws of soul-corrupting lions by the power of God, O Apostles, as ye were appointed princes upon the earth by Christ according to the Psalm, piously subduing it unto the Spirit: therefore the unruly movements of my heart...[^10522]

...subdue unto the divine law.[^10523]

*Troparion 2:*  
Having proclaimed in the world the Word of God, Who appeared comely beyond all the sons of men, ye made your feet beautiful, O all-wise heralds of good tidings, bringing peace and life. Therefore pacify by your prayers my soul, troubled by passions.[^10524]

*Troparion 3:*  
Having mortified your members which are upon the earth, ye clothed yourselves in all life, prefiguring by your passions the honorable Passion. Therefore revive me who am slain by the arrow of the evil one's malice, by the remedies of true repentance, O God-blessed Apostles.[^10525]

*Theotokion:*  
Heal my soul, ailing with grievous passion, as thou art merciful, O thou who didst give birth unto the all-good God; and deliver me ever, O All-Immaculate One, from enemies inciting and assailing me, that being saved I may diligently magnify thee, who hast magnified our race.[^10526]

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Heirmos:*  
Truly confessing thee as Theotokos, saved through thee, O pure Virgin, with the bodiless choirs we magnify thee.[^10527]

*Troparion 1:*  
As a deliverer in affliction for them that pray to thee in faith, deliver me from every malice, O Nicholas, entreating the good God and Lord.[^10528]

*Troparion 2:*  
Being now with the heavenly choirs, O most holy father Nicholas: entreat the good God to save us.[^10529]

*Troparion 3:*  
The judgment is at the doors; take heed, O my soul, and cry aloud unto God the Judge: Through the prayers of Nicholas, save me, O Lord.[^10530]

*Theotokion:*  
Being the receptacle of the Light, O Virgin, enlighten my soul, darkened by passions, that in faith and love I may ever glorify thee.[^10531]

*It is truly meet: Litany, and Photagogicon, and the customary Psalms.*[^10532]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Apostles, Tone 8:*[^10533]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
O Lord, the Apostles having diligently loved Thee upon the earth, counted all things as refuse, that they might gain Thee alone; and for Thy sake they gave up their bodies unto wounds. Therefore, being glorified, they pray for our souls.[^10534]

*Stichos: We were filled in the morning with Thy mercy, O Lord, and we rejoiced and were glad: ... And look upon Thy servants, and upon Thy works, and guide their sons.*[^10535]

*Sticheron 2:*  
O Lord, Thou hast magnified the Apostles' memory upon earth; for we all, having assembled together therein, doxologize Thee: for through them Thou grantest us healings, and unto all the world peace through their prayers, and great mercy.[^10536]

*Stichos: And let the brightness of the Lord our God be upon us, and direct Thou the works of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands do Thou direct.*[^10537]

*Martyrion:*  
Having clothed yourselves well in the breastplates of faith, and armed yourselves with the weapon of the Cross, ye were shown forth as stalwart soldiers. Ye manfully resisted the tormentors, and cast down the devil's deceit; having become victors, ye were accounted worthy of crowns. Pray unceasingly for us, that our souls may be saved.[^10538]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*  
Snatch me, O Sovereign Lady, from the hand of the man-slaying serpent, which desires to swallow me utterly by subtlety. Shatter his jaws, I pray thee, and destroy his wiles: that having escaped from his claws, I may magnify thy protection.[^10539]

*It is good to give praise: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparion and the rest.*[^10540]

*On Thursday, The Beatitudes, Tone 8.*[^10541]  
Remember us, O Christ, Savior of the world, as Thou didst remember the thief upon the Tree; and account all worthy, O only Compassionate One, of Thy heavenly kingdom.[^10542]

*Troparion 1:*  
The Apostles were shown forth as the trumpets of Christ: ye raised up them that lay in the graves of false belief, and made them partakers of divine life.[^10543]

*Troparion 2:*  
By the mystical seed of the Word, O God-proclaiming ones, ye made the barren hearts of all the nations fruitful in divine understanding; therefore ye are worthily called blessed.[^10544]

*Troparion 3:*  
Having stripped themselves of all malice, the sufferers valiantly passed through the midst of cruel torments, and from heaven were clothed in the robe of salvation.[^10545]

*Glory... Triadikon:*  
Mortify the assaults of evil-working passions of my wretched soul, O honorable, all-holy Trinity, through the prayers of Thy divine Apostles, that being saved I may glorify Thee.[^10546]

*Both now... Theotokion:*  
Rejoice, tongs of the divine Coal. Rejoice, seal of the Prophets and preaching of the Apostles, O all-immaculate Theotokos, through whom we were delivered from corruption.[^10547]

## Friday: The Precious and Life-Giving Cross[^10548]

*On Thursday Evening.*[^10549]  
*On "Lord, I have cried," Stichera of the Cross, Tone 8.*[^10550]  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "O most marvelous wonder" (Ѽ пресла́внагѡ чꙋдесѐ):*[^10551]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
O most marvelous wonder! The life-bearing garden, the all-holy Cross, lifted on high is manifested today; all the ends of the earth doxologize, the demonic legions are terrified. O what a gift has been bestowed upon earthly beings! Whereby, O Christ, save Thou our souls, for Thou alone art tender-hearted.[^10552]

*Sticheron 2:*  
O most marvelous wonder! As a cluster full of life, having borne the Most High, the Cross is seen raised from the earth today; whereby we all were drawn unto God, and death was swallowed up utterly. O most honorable Tree, whereby we received immortal food in Eden, glorifying Christ.[^10553]

*Sticheron 3:*  
O Thine abundant goodness toward us, O good Jesus! How Thou didst humble Thyself, and becamest man, and wast pleased to suffer, enduring the Cross and shameful death for the sake of unprofitable servants! What worthy, God-befitting gift shall we offer Thee? Only in thanksgiving do we, the faithful, glorify Thee.[^10554]

*Other Stichera, to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 8, Same Podoben.*[^10555]  
*Sticheron 4:*  
O the ineffable goodness of Him Who was incarnate of thee, O all-hymned Theotokos! For as the Lover of mankind He endured the Cross and death, that He might save the world which He created. Entreat Him: that He deliver me also, wretched and sorely afflicted, from torment, and establish me where the unwaning light shines.[^10556]

*Sticheron 5:*  
"What is this sight seen, which is beheld by mine eyes, O Master? Holding all creation, Thou hast been lifted up upon the Tree, and diest, Who givest life unto all!" the Theotokos said weeping, when she beheld lifted up upon the Cross God and man, Who had ineffably shone forth from her.[^10557]

*Sticheron 6:*  
Deliver from perils by thy precious prayers the flock which Thy Son and God acquired, sprinkling it with blood, O All-Immaculate One, driving away visible and invisible enemies like beasts; and fill their faces with dishonor, as the hymn-singer David proclaimed of old, O Pure One.[^10558]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*  
Beholding Thee nailed upon the Cross, O Jesus, and willingly receiving...[^10559]

...the passions, the Virgin and Thy Mother, O Master, cried aloud: "Woe is me, sweet Child! How dost Thou endure wounds unjustly, O Physician Who didst heal human infirmity, and by Thy tender-heartedness didst deliver all from corruption?"[^10560]

*Then, "O Gladsome Light." Prokeimenon of the day. "Vouchsafe, O Lord."*[^10561]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 8:*[^10562]  
*Sticheron 1:*  
Thou wast lifted up upon the Cross, O Christ God, and didst save the human race. We glorify Thy sufferings.[^10563]

*Stichos: Unto Thee have I lifted up mine eyes, unto Thee that dwellest in heaven: ... so look our eyes unto the Lord our God, until He take pity on us.*[^10564]

*Sticheron 2:*  
Thou wast nailed upon the Cross, O Christ God, and didst open the gates of paradise; therefore we glorify Thy Divinity.[^10565]

*Stichos: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us, for we are utterly: ... our soul has been exceedingly filled with the reproach of them that prosper, and with the contempt of the proud.*[^10566]

*Martyrion:*  
Martyrs of the Lord, ye sanctify every place, and heal every infirmity. And now pray, we beseech you, that our souls may be delivered from the snares of the enemy.[^10567]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*  
The unblemished heifer, beholding the Young Bull willingly nailed upon the Tree, weeping mournfully, cried aloud: "Woe is me, most beloved Child! What has the ungrateful Jewish assembly rendered unto Thee, desiring to make me childless of Thee, O All-Beloved?"[^10568]

*Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart, and the rest.*[^10569]

*On Thursday Evening, at Compline.*[^10570]  
*Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 8.*[^10571]  
*Ode 1. Heirmos:*  
Let us sing unto the Lord, Who led His people through the Red Sea, for He alone has been gloriously glorified.[^10572]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^10573]

*Troparion 1:*  
Make Christ merciful unto me, O all-immaculate Virgin, snatching me away in the day of fearsome condemnation.[^10574]

*Troparion 2:*  
Rain down upon me drops of compunction, I pray, O Sovereign Lady, washing away the filth that is in me, that I may glorify thee.[^10575]

*Glory... Troparion 3:*  
Enlighten me, O Virgin, who didst give birth unto the unwaning Light, driving away the deep darkness of my slothfulness.[^10576]

*Both now... Troparion 4:*  
Save me, O Theotokos, who am perishing in many sins; and from every torment...[^10577]

...and bitter condemnation deliver me.[^10578]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*  
O Lord, Maker of the vault of heaven and Builder of the Church: do Thou establish me in Thy love, O summit of desires, confirmation of the faithful, O only Lover of mankind.[^10579]

*Troparion 1:*  
Having fallen from holy living, O All-Immaculate One, I was likened unto the beasts, and became wholly condemned; but thou who didst give birth unto the Judge, deliver me from every condemnation, and save me.[^10580]

*Troparion 2:*  
Unto thee I flee ever, O Sovereign Lady, compassed about by a multitude of temptations: have compassion and save me, O thou who didst give birth unto the Savior and Lord of all, O only All-Hymned One.[^10581]

*Glory... Troparion 3:*  
O impassable gate leading unto God, O Maiden: open unto me the gates of repentance, I pray, cleansing the filth of my sins with the showers of thy mercy, O thou full of God's grace.[^10582]

*Both now... Troparion 4:*  
Deliver me from the assault of passions, O Sovereign Lady, and now vanquish mine warring enemies; establish me upon the rock of the divine desires, and enlighten my soul, O gate of divine light.[^10583]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*  
I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I have understood Thy works, and I have glorified Thy Divinity.[^10584]

*Troparion 1:*  
Heal the wounds of my soul, O Virgin full of God's grace; enlighten my mind, O Pure One, darkened by the assaults of passions.[^10585]

*Troparion 2:*  
Call me unto repentance, O All-Immaculate One, who sleep in despondency and am covered with sin, as thou art the Mother of the Word.[^10586]

*Glory... Troparion 3:*  
O unwedded Sovereign Lady, who didst give birth unto the incarnate Word: enlighten my soul, and deliver me from Gehenna and torment.[^10587]

*Both now... Troparion 4:*  
Upon thee I have placed all my hope, O Mother-Virgin: preserve my soul, O thou who didst give birth unto God, my Savior.[^10588]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*  
Why hast Thou cast me away from Thy face, O unwaning Light, and an alien darkness has covered me, the wretched one? But turn me back, and direct my ways unto the light of Thy commandments, I pray.[^10589]

*Troparion 1:*  
Heal the all-incurable passions of my soul, O Maiden, and rekindle my lamp which has been extinguished through slothfulness, and guide me unto the paths of repentance, O Virgin...[^10590]

...that in faith and love I may glorify thee.[^10591]

*Troparion 2:*  
I condemn myself before the trial, O thou who hast been made glad of God: I bear shameful deeds, a solitary condemned one; but stand by me, as thou art the protectress of all, and deliver me from bitter condemnation.[^10592]

*Glory... Troparion 3:*  
Cease not delivering me from captivity, and from the soul-corrupting turmoil that grips me, and from the cruel passions that slay me, O all-holy Maiden, protectress of sinners, ready helper of men.[^10593]

*Both now... Troparion 4:*  
Revive me who am wholly slain by the sting of death through transgression, O All-Immaculate One: who didst conceive Life for the world, the Deliverer and King, and guide me unto the light.[^10594]

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*  
Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine iniquities; and lead me up from the deep of evils, I pray; for unto Thee have I cried, and do Thou give ear unto me, O God of my salvation.[^10595]

*Troparion 1:*  
Revive me who am slain by many transgressions, O pure Virgin, who ineffably didst give birth unto Life for men, O Theotokos, and guide me to do the will of the Lord.[^10596]

*Troparion 2:*  
Thee all we faithful have acquired as protectress and wall, who are ever submerged in the deep of evils, turmoils, and sorrows, O Theotokos, only refuge of the faithful.[^10597]

*Glory... Troparion 3:*  
O thou who didst wither the orchards of godlessness by thy holy offspring, O All-Immaculate One: consume the enemy's malice that ever grows within me, O Pure One.[^10598]

*Both now... Troparion 4:*  
Sanctify my mind, and enlighten my heart, O holy Mother of God: and change the evils that hold me fast, that I may glorify thee, my firm protectress.[^10599]

*Then, "Lord, have mercy" (40). Glory... Both now...*[^10600]

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 8:*  
"O new wonder! O fearsome mystery!" cried the Ewe-lamb, beholding her Son outstretched upon the Tree of the Cross: "What is this, O immortal Word of God? How art Thou seen dead, and shakest the earth as almighty? But I hymn Thy fearsome and divine condescension."[^10601]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*  
The Hebrew children in the furnace boldly trampled upon the flame, and turned the fire into dew, crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God, unto the ages.[^10602]

*Troparion 1:*  
Be merciful unto me, O Virgin, and heal me who am wounded by the weapon of sin with the plaster of thy prayer; and snatch me away from the unquenchable fire unto the ages.[^10603]

*Troparion 2:*  
Deliver me, O Mother of the Savior, from the cruel captivity that grips me, from wicked thoughts, and from the seizures of transgression, that being ever saved, I may glorify thee as is due.[^10604]

*Glory... Troparion 3:*  
Now unto thee I flee, O Mother of God, bound with the cords of transgressions: through the tender mercy of loving-kindness loose me, O Virgin, and deliver me from the torment and malice of demons.[^10605]

*Both now... Troparion 4:*  
Have compassion and save me, O Virgin, who didst give birth unto the compassionate Word of God; and with the light that is in thee enlighten my soul, and deliver me from the cruel craft of demons.[^10606]

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*  
Sevenfold the Chaldean tyrant madly kindled the furnace for the godly ones; but seeing them saved by a higher power, he cried aloud unto the Creator and Deliverer: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^10607]

*Troparion 1:*  
He Who is in every way perfect and inaccessible by nature, appeared touchable unto me, clothed in flesh from thee, O Unwedded One: Whom entreat diligently to lighten the burden of mine iniquities, and to deliver me from the future judgment.[^10608]

*Troparion 2:*  
O thou who didst give birth unto the Judge and Lord beyond word, entreat Him as Thy Son, O All-Holy One: in the hour of judgment to deliver me from condemnation, and fire, and lightless darkness, and gnashing of teeth, who ever sing of thee in faith and piety.[^10609]

*Glory... Troparion 3:*  
O all-immaculate Parent of God, cleanse the wounds of my soul and sinful offenses, washing them with the fountain that flowed from the side of Thine Offspring, and with the streams that flowed therefrom; for unto thee do I cry, and unto thee I flee, and thee I entreat, who art full of God's grace.[^10610]

*Both now... Troparion 4:*  
Revive my soul, slain by the serpentine bite, O All-Immaculate One, who didst manifestly give birth unto Life alone; and hasten to do the desires of Him Who was born for our sake, O Virgin, as I cry aloud: Ye children, bless; ye priests...[^10611]

...ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Him unto all ages.[^10612]

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*  
Heaven was amazed at this, and the ends of the earth were astonished, that God appeared bodily unto men, and thy womb became more spacious than the heavens; therefore the ranks of Angels and men magnify thee, O Theotokos.[^10613]

*Troparion 1:*  
O gate of light, enlighten my soul, blinded by passions, darkened and imperiled by subtle thoughts; and deliver me from temptations, and from the afflictions and cruel torment of demons, and from the future fire and punishment.[^10614]

*Troparion 2:*  
Spare me, O Savior Who wast born of the Virgin, and Who didst preserve her that gave Thee birth incorrupt even after childbirth, when Thou shalt sit to judge my deeds: overlook mine iniquities and sins, as Thou art sinless, and as God art merciful and the Lover of mankind.[^10615]

*Glory... Troparion 3:*  
Bearing the heavenly Fire in thy hands as with tongs, O thou full of God's grace: burn up the passions of my soul, O Pure One, and deliver me from the fearsome judgment, and the fire, and the cruel vexation of demons.[^10616]

*Both now... Troparion 4:*  
By thine intelligible radiance enlighten our thoughts, hearts, and minds, O Maiden full of God's grace: that walking rightly in the paths of life, we may obtain mercy, ever proclaiming thy praises.[^10617]

*It is truly meet: Trisagion. After Our Father: Troparion and the rest.*[^10618]

*On Friday at Matins.*[^10619]  
*After the First Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 8.*[^10620]  
*Sessional Hymn 1:*  
Beholding the Author of life hanging upon the Cross, the thief said: "If He Who is crucified with us were not God incarnate, the sun would not have hidden its rays, nor would the earth have quaked trembling; but O Thou Who endurest all things, remember me, O Lord, in Thy kingdom."[^10621]

*Stichos: Exalt ye the Lord our God, and worship at His footstool, for He is holy.*[^10622]

*Sessional Hymn 2:*  
In the midst of two thieves Thy Cross was found to be a righteous balance: for the one indeed was dragged down into hades by the weight of blasphemy, but the other was lightened of sins unto the knowledge of theology. O Christ God, glory to Thee.[^10623]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*  
Being ever preserved by the Cross of Thy Son and God, O Virgin,...[^10624]

...we drive away demonic assaults and wiles: truly chanting thee as the Theotokos, and in love all generations call thee blessed, O Pure One, even as thou didst foretell; therefore grant us remission of sins through thy prayers.[^10625]

*After the Second Kathisma, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, Tone 8.*[^10626]  
*Sessional Hymn 1:*  
In the midst of Eden a tree blossomed forth death, but in the midst of all the earth a Tree put forth life: for tasting of the first, being incorruptible, we became corruptible; but attaining unto the second, we were nourished with incorruption. For by the Cross, as God, Thou savest the human race.[^10627]

*Stichos: But God is our King before the ages, He hath wrought salvation in the midst of the earth.*[^10628]

*Sessional Hymn 2:*  
In paradise a tree formerly stripped me naked, the enemy introducing mortality by tasting; but the Tree of the Cross, bearing for men the vesture of eternal life, was planted upon the earth, and all the world was filled with all joy; beholding which lifted on high, O people, let us cry aloud in faith unto God with one accord: Thy house is full of glory.[^10629]

*Stichos: Wonderful is God in His saints, the God of Israel.*[^10630]

*Martyrion:*  
With heavenly light this temple is enlightened today, for in it the angelic hosts rejoice; with whom also the choirs of men make merry in the memory of the passion-bearers. By their entreaties, O Christ, send down upon Thy world peace and great mercy.[^10631]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*  
Beholding Him Who was incarnate of thy pure blood, and Who beyond understanding was born of thee, O Pure One, hanging upon the Tree in the midst of malefactors, thy womb was pierced, and weeping maternally thou didst cry aloud: "Woe is me, my Child! What is this Thy divine and ineffable dispensation, whereby Thou hast brought to life Thy creation? I hymn Thy tender-heartedness."[^10632]

*After the Third Kathisma, Sessional Hymns, Tone 8.*[^10633]  
*Tone 8, Podoben: "That which was commanded mystically" (Повелѣ́нное та́йнѡ):*[^10634]  
*Sessional Hymn 1:*  
By Thy Cross, O Christ Savior, and by Thy death, the tyranny of the enemy was overthrown, and death was put to death: and the dead whom hades held bound within itself from the ages, it suddenly released, even all the captives, who hymn Thy dominion, and Thy fearsome and divine descent, O Good One, whereby Thou didst save all.[^10635]

*Sessional Hymn 2:*  
By the expectation of false deification, the forefather became the author of corruption unto all that came from him; but Thou by Thy Cross pourest forth life, as Thou art all-good; for Thou wast willingly nailed, that Thou mightest loose us from the first condemnation. Therefore we hymn Thy voluntary Passion, O Christ.[^10636]

*Glory... Both now... Stavrotheotokion:*  
The unblemished Ewe-lamb, beholding the Lamb and Shepherd hanging upon the Cross, cried aloud: "My Child! What strange and unexpected sight is this? How is the Life of all condemned unto death in likeness of earthly beings? But rise on the third day from the dead, O Word, as Thou didst say, that rejoicing I may glorify Thee."[^10637]

*Canon to the Precious and Life-Giving Cross.*[^10638]  
*(The acrostic whereof is: The Cross abides the fullness of all good things, Amen.)*[^10639]  
*The work of Joseph.*[^10640]  
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*  
Moses, tracing a cross with his rod straightly, cleaved the Red Sea for Israel that walked on foot; and striking it transversely, he brought it together again over the chariots of Pharaoh, drawing the invincible weapon. Therefore let us sing unto Christ our God, for He has been glorified.[^10641]

*Refrain: Glory, O Lord, to Thy precious Cross.*[^10642]

*Troparion 1:*  
Having stretched out Thy divine palms upon the Cross, O Jesus, Thou didst gather unto Thyself the creation made by Thy hand; and Thou didst deliver all from the hand of the evil one, and didst subdue him with a sovereign hand, O King of all. Therefore we the faithful sing unto Thy majesty, for Thou hast been glorified.[^10643]

*Troparion 2:*  
Harmful in Eden was the bitter tasting of the tree, which brought in death; but Christ, having been put to death upon the Tree, poured forth life unto all, and slew the serpent by divine power. Therefore let us sing unto Him, our God, for He has been glorified.[^10644]

*Martyrion:*  
Having acted valiantly, O Jesus, the multitude of Martyrs, by the Cross and Thy Passion, rose up against passions; and before enemies they confessed Thee Who reignest over creation, and endured torments and boundless afflictions. Therefore they all attained Thy glory of everlasting life.[^10645]

*Martyrion:*  
By the sprinkling of Thy divinely-wrought precious Blood, Thy Martyrs, O Lord, having found deliverance, truly poured out their own blood, being unjustly tormented rather than offer sacrifice unto defiled, soul-corrupting demons. Therefore they were offered as precious slayings unto Thee, O King of all.[^10646]

*Theotokion:*  
When she beheld Thee nailed upon the Cross, Who beholdest all things, the Immaculate One, weeping, said: "What is this, my Child? What have they rendered unto Thee who enjoyed Thy many gifts? How shall I endure these pains? Glory to Thy tender-heartedness and fearsome dispensation, O Long-Suffering One!"[^10647]

*Another Canon, to the Most Holy Theotokos.*[^10648]  
*Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*  
Having traversed the water as dry land, and escaped the evil of Egypt, the Israelite cried aloud: Let us sing unto our Deliverer and God.[^10649]

*Refrain: Most Holy Lady Theotokos, save us.*[^10650]

*Troparion 1:*  
My soul, darkened by the pleasures of life and untimeously troubled by worldly cares, O Mother of God, do thou enlighten with thine enlightenment.[^10651]

*Troparion 2:*  
The heavenly gates were opened by thy divine giving of birth, O Mother of God: grant entry thereinto unto my soul as thou art merciful, and guide me unto them.[^10652]

*Troparion 3:*  
My soul, pierced by the arrow of the evil one, and wounded by his wiles and cunning, O Virgin, heal by thy mercy.[^10653]

*Troparion 4:*  
O hope of the hopeless, restoration of the fallen, who didst give birth unto the divine Light: enlighten my soul that abides in darkness.[^10654]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*  
The rod is taken as an image of the mystery, for by budding it predetermines the priest; and for the formerly barren Church the Tree of the Cross has now blossomed forth, for strength and confirmation.[^10655]

*Troparion 1:*  
Into black vesture the sun changed, beholding Thee unjustly lifted up upon the Tree; and the rocks were rent, and all the earth shook with fear, O only Savior, deliverance of all.[^10656]

*Troparion 2:*  
Having stretched forth his hands, Moses prefigured the precious Cross; which we now portraying with pious mind, conquer all the foreign demonic legions, remaining free from all harm thereof.[^10657]

*Martyrion:*  
Enduring passions, the passion-bearers figured the Passion, having shared in manifold sufferings of Him Who suffered willingly, and put passions to death, and shone forth life unto the world.[^10658]

*Martyrion:*  
Having trodden the path of torments without turning back, the glorious trophy-bearers cast down from their hearts the stumbling-blocks of deceit, and hastened rejoicing unto divine rest.[^10659]

*Theotokion:*  
Apart from the customary laws...[^10660]

...of men, I gave Thee birth, O Child," the Theotokos said weeping, "and how have the lawless lifted Thee up upon the Tree in the midst of lawless ones, Who alone hast laid down the law of life?"[^10661]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 3. Heirmos:*  
O Lord, Maker of the vault of heaven and Builder of the Church: do Thou establish me in Thy love, O summit of desires, confirmation of the faithful, O only Lover of mankind.[^10662]

*Troparion 1:*  
Unceasing groanings, fervent tears, and a contrite heart grant me, O Virgin, that I may weep for the things wrought by me; and consume my passions that grow together with me, O only All-Hymned One.[^10663]

*Troparion 2:*  
Snatch away my soul, weighed down with sinful slumber and slipped down unto the womb of hades, O Sovereign Lady: and grant me the thought of true repentance, O divinely-blessed one.[^10664]

*Troparion 3:*  
Grant love of compunction and virtues, O All-Immaculate One, unto my soul submerged in transgressions, and to love heavenly living, and to have the desire of God.[^10665]

*Troparion 4:*  
Upon thee I place hope, O Mother of God, and quickly shall I be delivered from despair; for I know, yea, I know the wealth of thy tender-heartedness, knowing the strength of thy boldness.[^10666]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*  
I have heard, O Lord, the mystery of Thy dispensation; I have understood Thy works, and I have glorified Thy Divinity.[^10667]

*Troparion 1:*  
Bearing piety as a cedar, faith as a cypress, and love as a pine, let us worship the divine Cross.[^10668]

*Troparion 2:*  
By Thy Cross, O Savior, paradise was opened, and condemned man entered therein again, magnifying Thy goodness.[^10669]

*Troparion 3:*  
Men who were slain Thou didst bring to life, O Savior, having become dead; and Thou didst put to death the serpent who brought in sin.[^10670]

*Martyrion:*  
Having become conformed unto the passions of Christ, the divine Martyrs appeared also as partakers of heavenly splendor.[^10671]

*Martyrion:*  
Ye were adorned, O Martyrs, being united unto the comely Word; and ye were radiantly enlightened, having loved the Sun of righteousness.[^10672]

*Theotokion:*  
Thou didst give birth unto a new Child, Who before...[^10673]

...the ages is perfect, O all-immaculate Maiden, and Who perfects all things by the Cross and by goodness.[^10674]

*Canon to the Theotokos. Ode 4. Heirmos:*  
Thou art my strength, O Lord, Thou art my power; Thou art my God, Thou art my rejoicing, Who leaving not the bosom of the Father, didst visit our poverty; therefore with the Prophet Habakkuk I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind.[^10675]

*Troparion 1:*  
O thou who didst ineffably give birth unto God the Word: bind up the sores of my soul with effective herb, pouring on the precious Blood of Thy Son, Who destroyed the soul-corrupting belly of hades and poured forth resurrection unto the world.[^10676]

*Troparion 2:*  
O Bride of God, send down upon my soul streams of cleansing from transgressions, and wash away evil thoughts, accounting it worthy to be pure; for unto thy mediation and thy help have I fled, O Theotokos Virgin.[^10677]

*Troparion 3:*  
Unto thy protection I now flee, O Pure One: make haste to deliver me from the cruel storm of the enemy and from the troubling torrents of iniquity; and unto thy haven and unto thy stillness guide me unswervingly, O Mother of God.[^10678]

*Troparion 4:*  
The enemy with sinful arrows hath wounded all my soul, and defiled my heart with pleasures, and turned me away from the right way. Therefore I cry aloud unto thee: Having converted me, heal me and save me.[^10679]

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*  
O thrice-blessed Tree, whereon Christ was crucified, the King and Lord: whereby he that deceived by a tree fell, being deceived by thee, when God was nailed in the flesh, Who grants peace unto our souls.[^10680]

*Troparion 1:*  
Desiring to clothe us who were naked with the garment of incorruption, Thou wast stripped, and being crucified upon the Cross, Thou didst strip naked the enemy's wiles, O Christ; therefore we glorify Thy sufferings.[^10681]

*Martyrion:*  
The saving Blood that manifestly flowed from the side cleansed the world, and destroyed temple blood-sacrifices, and renewed those that were corrupted by the rational fruit, and poured forth incorruption unto our souls.[^10682]

*Martyrion:*  
Enlightened by the beauty of manifold wounds, the glorious Martyrs...[^10683]
